### [1] [Toward a foundation model for forest point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24787)
*Yuanwen Yue,Stefano Puliti,Damien Robert,Atakan Topaloğlu,Binbin Xiang,Maciej Wielgosz,Jan Dirk Wegner,Rasmus Astrup,Christian Rupprecht,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文以LitePT为骨干，探索面向3D林业的基础模型：先建立多任务SOTA监督基线，再利用跨机载、无人机和移动激光扫描的大规模无标注点云进行自监督预训练，并在四类林业任务和不同标注预算下系统评估其迁移价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林调查日益依赖AI从大规模3D点云中提取森林属性，但现有模型通常专用于单一任务、传感器和森林类型，导致适配成本高（标注、计算和专家知识）。作者希望验证单一预训练模型能否学习跨多样森林调查场景的可迁移表征，从而迈向3D林业基础模型。

Method: 以LitePT为骨干，首先建立强监督基线，在森林语义分割、实例分割、树种分类和林龄回归基准上取得新SOTA。随后构建覆盖机载、无人机和移动激光扫描、跨多样森林生态系统的大规模无标注语料，并对同一骨干进行自监督预训练。系统比较从零训练、监督预训练和自监督预训练三种策略，在四类代表性林业任务及不同标注预算下评估表征学习效果。

Result: 与从零训练相比，自监督预训练加速模型收敛，并在标注稀缺时持续提升性能。与任务特定监督预训练相比，自监督预训练能产生跨下游林业任务更可迁移的表征。研究还识别了预训练表征最具实际价值的适用区间，并指出实例判别而非森林语义是构建通用3D森林基础模型的主要剩余障碍。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练是迈向3D林业基础模型的有效路径，尤其在标注预算有限时优势明显，并可提升跨任务迁移性。未来需重点解决实例判别问题，以推动通用3D森林基础模型的发展。代码与模型已公开。

Abstract: Forest inventories increasingly rely on artificial intelligence (AI) models to derive forest attributes from large-scale 3D point clouds. Current models are typically specialized to a single task, sensor, and forest type, making adaptation expensive in terms of annotations, computation, and expertise. We ask whether a single pretrained model can instead learn transferable representations across diverse forest inventory settings. Inspired by recent developments in language modelling and computer vision, we take a step toward a foundation model (FM) for 3D forestry. Using LitePT as backbone, we first establish a strong supervised baseline that sets a new state of the art on forest semantic and instance segmentation, tree species classification, and age regression benchmarks. We then curate a large-scale unlabelled corpus spanning airborne, UAV, and mobile laser scanning across diverse forest ecosystems, and pretrain the same backbone using self-supervised learning. We systematically evaluate representation learning strategies by comparing training from scratch, supervised pretraining, and self-supervised pretraining across four representative forestry tasks, under varying annotation budgets. Compared with training from scratch, self-supervised pretraining accelerates model convergence and consistently improves performance when annotations are scarce. Compared with task-specific supervised pretraining, self-supervised pretraining yields more transferable representations across downstream forestry tasks. These findings identify the practical regime in which pretrained representations are most valuable and suggest that instance discrimination, rather than forest semantics, is the main remaining obstacle to a general-purpose 3D forest foundation model. Code and models are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [Relationally Grounded Latent World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24626)
*Fabian Schmidt,Markus Enzweiler,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作研究交通场景图能否作为潜在世界模型的特权语义监督，并在nuScenes上验证了显式关系结构对自动驾驶轨迹预测与碰撞率改善的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型能学习自动驾驶的预测性表征，但其状态中保留的关系语义往往是隐式的；作者希望显式注入交通场景中的关系结构，以提升潜在世界表征的语义质量与预测性能。

Method: 基于LAW，利用nuScenes 3D标注构建以行为主体为中心的场景图，将序列化关系结构用冻结文本嵌入模型编码为语义目标，并在训练时对齐视觉潜在表征；推理阶段移除监督分支，因此不需要场景图或3D标注，也不增加测试时计算。

Result: 在nuScenes上，相比重新训练的LAW基线，平均轨迹L2误差从0.661降至0.622（降低5.9%），碰撞率从0.456降至0.217（降低52.4%）；同时优于非结构化的caption式语义目标。

Conclusion: 显式关系结构作为特权语义监督可有效提升潜在世界模型表征学习，且无需增加推理开销，说明交通场景图是学习自动驾驶潜在世界表征的有用语义监督信号。

Abstract: Latent world models learn predictive representations for autonomous driving, but the relational semantics these states preserve often remain implicit. We investigate whether traffic scene graphs can serve as privileged semantic supervision for latent world representations. Building on LAW, we construct actor-centric scene graphs from nuScenes 3D annotations, encode their serialized relational structure using a frozen text embedding model, and align the visual latent representations with this semantic target during training. We remove the supervision branch at inference, so it requires neither scene graphs nor 3D annotations and adds no test-time computation. On nuScenes, our method reduces average trajectory L2 error from 0.661 to 0.622 (5.9%) and collision rate from 0.456 to 0.217 (52.4%) relative to our retrained LAW baseline. It also outperforms an unstructured caption-style semantic target, supporting the benefit of explicit relational structure for latent world-model representation learning.

</details>


### [3] [Do LiDAR Language Models Really Understand Spatio-temporal Relationships?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24452)
*Runyi Yang,Murat Akkoyun,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Junwei Zheng,Xiaoye Wang,Kailun Yang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何参照基准与诊断协议 LiDAR-Hallu（1 万问题、150 个 nuScenes 场景），揭示 4D LiDAR 语言模型的聚合准确率掩盖了其依赖捷径、无法区分所查询时空关系的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D LiDAR 语言模型仅以多项选择准确率评测，而始终选择同一选项几乎可追平两个 B4DL 派生配置的成绩，亟需检验模型是否真正区分所查询的物理关系。

Method: 构建 LiDAR-Hallu 基准与诊断协议：10,000 个问题覆盖物体存在性、自车相对位置、距离排序、相对运动与时间定位，并给出对象选择、时间比较和参考答案判定的明确规则；协议包含固定答案与候选内容对照、提示相同但参考答案相反的跨场景配对，以及关系特定召回，分析 100,000 条响应。

Result: 仅凭候选时长即可在不观察 LiDAR 的情况下预测时间答案；配对问题上模型常对参考答案相反的场景给出同一答案；关系特定分析显示两种配置在所有条件下均漏掉全部正向横向运动案例；时序打乱对比解码净收益甚微，修复多被新错误抵消，主要失败依旧存在。

Conclusion: 评估时空推理需检验模型能否区分所查询的物理关系，而不能仅依赖单题准确率；代码、检查点与数据已开源。

Abstract: Recent 4D LiDAR language models aim to reason about objects and their evolving spatial relationships. Yet, in our evaluation, always selecting the same option nearly matches the multiple-choice accuracy of two B4DL-derived configurations. We introduce LiDAR-Hallu, a geometry-referenced benchmark and diagnostic protocol with 10,000 questions across 150 nuScenes scenes. It covers object existence, ego-relative position, distance ordering, relative motion, and temporal localization, with explicit rules for selecting objects, comparing times, and determining reference answers. Our protocol combines fixed-answer and candidate-content controls, cross-scene pairs with identical prompts but opposite reference answers, and relation-specific recall. Analysis of 100,000 recorded responses reveals failures hidden by aggregate accuracy. Candidate duration alone makes temporal answers predictable without observing LiDAR. On paired questions, the models frequently give the same answer to scenes requiring opposite answers. Relation-specific analysis further shows that both configurations miss every positive lateral-motion case across all tested conditions. Temporal-shuffle contrastive decoding provides little net improvement, as repairs are largely offset by new errors and the main failures persist. These results show that evaluating spatio-temporal reasoning requires testing whether models distinguish the queried physical relationships, rather than relying on individual-answer accuracy alone. The source code, checkpoints, and data are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [4] [AnalogDepth: Multi-view Geometry from FPV drones under Analog Video Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24312)
*André Amorim,Pedro F. Proença*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作指出现有模拟FPV视频传输噪声会严重降低Depth Anything 3（DA3）的深度估计精度，并提出AnalogDepth，通过基于真实噪声库和LoRA知识蒸馏的参数高效适配方法提升DA3在模拟FPV图像上的深度与三维重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟视频传输（VTX）在FPV无人机中因低延迟、轻量和低成本仍广泛使用，但其噪声具有复杂的空间结构，不同于标准训练增强中常用的数字图像退化（如AWGN）。这种噪声会显著损害最先进前馈视觉几何基础模型DA3的精度，因此需要针对模拟FPV图像进行有效适配。

Method: 提出AnalogDepth参数高效训练流程：采用学生-教师知识蒸馏，将LoRA注入DA3的DINOv2骨干以适配模拟FPV图像；不依赖解析合成噪声，而是从多种条件下静态FPV录像构建噪声库，并将真实噪声注入与PSD匹配高斯合成及AWGN基线进行对比。

Result: 在三个室内场景的六个真实FPV飞行序列上，使用真实噪声库训练相比预训练DA3基线和两种高斯噪声变体，持续降低逐帧深度RMSE和3D重建Chamfer距离。

Conclusion: 复现真实模拟传输噪声的空间结构对于有效适配DA3至模拟FPV图像至关重要，真实噪声注入优于高斯近似噪声增强。

Abstract: Analog video transmission (VTX) remains widespread in FPV drones due to low latency, weight and low cost. However analog VTX suffers from complex spatially structured image degradation which differ fundamentally from digital image corruption (e.g. AWGN) used in standard training augmentation. This work shows that this type of noise severely degrades the accuracy of Depth Anything 3 (DA3), a state-of-the-art feed forward visual geometry foundation model. To address this gap, we present AnalogDepth, a parameter-efficient training pipeline that adapts DA3 to analog FPV imagery using student-teacher knowledge distillation with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) injected into the DINOv2 backbone. Rather than synthesizing noise analytically, we build a noise bank from static FPV recordings under diverse conditions and compare real-noise injection against PSD-matched Gaussian synthesis and AWGN as baselines. Experiments on six real FPV flight sequences across three indoor scenes show that training with our noise bank consistently reduces per-frame depth RMSE and 3D reconstruction Chamfer distance compared to the pretrained DA3 baseline and both Gaussian noise variants. These results demonstrate that replicating the spatial structure of real analog transmission noise is critical for effective adaptation.

</details>


### [5] [Colon3R: Cross-Domain 3D Reconstruction from Monocular Colonoscopic Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23961)
*Zhihao Xing,Yingyu Wang,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Colon3R：一个基于预训练VGGT的跨域半监督框架，将仿真/体模数据中的相机、深度和点图几何迁移到无标注在体结肠镜视频，实现更优的单目3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目结肠镜3D重建对机器人结肠镜很重要，但受弱纹理、镜面反射、视角重叠有限和非刚性组织运动影响。传统多视图方法依赖稳定对应和近似刚性假设，在结肠镜中常不成立；现有内镜方法多依赖域特定监督，而在体标注数据不足，难以将几何基础模型适配到临床结肠镜。

Method: Colon3R基于预训练VGGT，采用跨域半监督学习，从有标注的体模和仿真数据迁移耦合的相机、深度和点图几何到无标注在体结肠镜，且不需要目标域几何标注。与仅用源域微调不同，该方法通过教师模型生成跨视角监督，直接利用无标注在体视频。提出分层准刚性可靠性，在序列、有向对和像素三个层级选择可靠监督；并通过源保持适配在目标域适应中保留已学到的耦合几何。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在深度、点图和相机位姿估计上的整体性能优于现有最先进方法。真实在体结肠镜定性比较显示，在临床域偏移下，其重建比竞争方法更完整且几何一致性更好。

Conclusion: Colon3R无需目标域几何标注即可有效实现跨域在体结肠镜3D重建，提升深度、点图和相机位姿估计性能；代码将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: Monocular colonoscopic 3D reconstruction is important for surgical robotic colonoscopy, but remains challenging due to weak texture, specular reflections, limited view overlap, and non-rigid tissue motion. Conventional multi-view 3D reconstruction methods rely on stable correspondences and approximate rigidity, which are often violated in colonoscopy. Existing endoscopic methods often rely on domain-specific supervision, whereas there are not enough in-vivo labeled data available to adapt geometry foundation models to clinical colonoscopy. We present Colon3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised framework built on pretrained VGGT that transfers coupled camera, depth, and pointmap geometry from labeled phantom and simulated data to unlabeled in-vivo colonoscopy without requiring target-domain geometric annotations. Unlike source-only fine-tuning, which learns only from phantom and simulated data, Colon3R directly exploits unlabeled in-vivo video through teacher-derived cross-view supervision. Our proposed hierarchical quasi-rigid reliability selects reliable supervision at the sequence, directed-pair, and pixel levels, while source-preserving adaptation retains the learned coupled geometry during target-domain adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior overall performance over state-of-the-art approaches in depth, pointmap, and camera pose estimation. Qualitative comparisons on real in-vivo colonoscopy further show substantially more complete and geometrically consistent reconstructions than competing methods under clinical domain shift. The code will be public available after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [6] [Which Terrain Is Better? Preference Learning with VLM Prototypes for Off-Road Traversability Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23673)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,E-In Son,Dong-Wook Kim,Jung-Taak Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TravPro 将越野导航中的地形偏好建模为视觉可通行性排序，利用标准标注隐含的比较关系，在冻结 VLM 的 patch token 上学习偏好读出器，并蒸馏出稠密地形偏好图，在五个未见域上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越野导航中，障碍物避让可由自由空间检测或语义分割处理，但“哪片地形更好”通常依赖可通行性分数；该分数缺乏统一真值，只能退化为按语义类别预设值或自由空间置信度，描述的是区域是什么，而非机器人应偏好哪块区域。

Method: 作者提出视觉可通行性排序任务，用两个区域间的比较来监督地形偏好顺序。TravPro 将标准标注转化为有序区域对，在冻结的视觉-语言模型 patch token 上拟合小型读出器；token 先聚类为固定原型库，读出器学习每个原型的偏好分数，并作为教师将稀疏比较扩散为稠密偏好伪标签，最后用 RGB 学生网络蒸馏出稠密地形偏好图，同时输出非地面掩码以排除障碍物和背景。

Result: 在五个未见域上，TravPro 达到平均成对准确率 0.915，最强基线为 0.783；其排序对表面条件敏感，能表达按类别赋值无法表示的偏好。同一 VLM 与监督若通过提示方式或作为稠密目标使用，则无法产生这样的排序。

Conclusion: 关键在于如何利用 VLM 与监督信号：将标准标注转为区域比较、学习原型级偏好并蒸馏为稠密偏好图，可无像素级偏好标注地实现有效的越野地形排序。

Abstract: In vision-based off-road navigation, a robot needs to know not only which obstacles to avoid but also which terrain is better. The first is handled by freespace detection or semantic segmentation. The second is usually answered with a traversability score, but no universal ground truth exists for such a score, so perception falls back on a predefined value per semantic class or a freespace confidence. These scores say what a region is, not which region a robot should prefer. We therefore formulate this preference as visual traversability ranking, an ordering of visible terrain that can be supervised by comparisons between two regions. Standard annotations do not label preference, but they imply its direction. We present TravPro, which converts these annotations into ordered region pairs and fits a small readout on frozen vision--language model (VLM) patch tokens to these pairs. The tokens are clustered once into a fixed prototype bank, and the readout learns a preference score per prototype. The readout is then applied to every patch and serves as a teacher that turns sparse comparisons into dense preference pseudo-labels without pixel-wise annotation. An RGB student distills these maps into a dense terrain-preference map together with a non-ground mask that excludes obstacles and background from the ranking. On five unseen domains, TravPro reaches a mean pairwise accuracy of 0.915 against 0.783 for the strongest baseline, producing an ordering sensitive to surface condition that a per-class value cannot represent. The same VLM and the same supervision yield no such ordering when the VLM is prompted and the supervision is used as dense targets; what matters is how they are used.

</details>


### [7] [G6D: Geometric Learning-Free RGB-D 6D Pose Solver for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23566)
*Yixuan Liang,William Chen,Yunan Wang,Jizhou Yan,Zhao Jin,Changling Liu,Chuxiong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G6D 是一种无需学习、纯几何驱动的 RGB-D 6D 物体位姿求解器，通过模板几何匹配生成假设并利用轮廓与深度一致性精化，可在无 GPU 条件下部署并适用于机器人抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本 6D 位姿估计方法虽能泛化到未见物体，但多依赖大规模预训练模型，GPU 计算与内存开销大，难以部署到感知、规划与控制共享有限资源的机器人平台；同时学习到的中间表示几何可解释性有限，不利于任务特定适配。

Method: 输入 RGB-D 观测、物体实例掩码、相机内参和 CAD 模型；通过基于模板的几何匹配生成位姿假设，再以轮廓一致性和深度一致性进行精化，形成纯几何驱动的位姿估计范式。无需预训练视觉模型或目标特定训练，保留可解释的几何表示；可调假设数量实现精度-计算权衡，并支持仅 CPU 配置部署。

Result: 在 LineMOD 和五个 BOP19 数据集上取得先进性能；真实世界 pick-and-place 实验进一步验证了 G6D 在机器人操作中的适用性。

Conclusion: G6D 以无学习、几何驱动的方式缓解了对大规模预训练模型和 GPU 资源的依赖，兼顾可解释性与部署灵活性，适用于机器人 6D 位姿估计与操作任务，且项目已公开。

Abstract: 6D object pose estimation is fundamental to robotic manipulation and automation. Recent zero-shot methods have significantly improved generalization to unseen objects, but most still rely on large-scale pretrained models with substantial GPU computation and memory demands. These requirements complicate deployment on robotic platforms where perception, planning, and control share limited computational resources, while learned intermediate representations offer limited geometric interpretability for task-specific adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose G6D, a learning-free, geometry-driven RGB-D 6D pose solver. Given an RGB-D observation, an object instance mask, camera intrinsics, and a CAD model, G6D generates pose hypotheses through template-based geometric matching and refines them using silhouette and depth consistency, forming a purely geometry-driven pose estimation paradigm. This paradigm requires neither pretrained visual models nor target-specific training and preserves interpretable geometric representations throughout pose estimation. Moreover, adjustable hypothesis counts provide flexible accuracy-computation trade-offs, while a CPU-only configuration supports deployment without GPU resources. Experiments on LineMOD and five BOP19 datasets demonstrate advanced performance. Real-world pick-and-place experiments further demonstrate G6D's applicability to robotic manipulation. The complete project is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [MaskVLA: Visual Masking Against Trajectory Overfitting of Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23565)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Jiaying Huang,Ge Wang,Shenhao Yan,Jiahao Yang,Chengsi Yao,Qi Liu,Qing Zhao,Shuguang Cui,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaskVLA：一种基于视觉掩码的微调策略，通过随机遮挡主相机少量视觉信息，引导VLA模型利用腕部相机并学习更细粒度、任务相关的视觉特征，从而提升复杂操作任务的成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在有限数据微调时存在严重的轨迹过拟合问题，且未能有效利用腕部相机信息；需要一种方法引导模型关注更细粒度、任务相关的视觉特征，以形成稳健策略并提升泛化。

Method: MaskVLA采用掩码式微调：在训练中随机掩蔽主相机的一小部分视觉信息，促使模型自主利用腕部相机信息并学习更细粒度、任务相关且有效的视觉特征，进而产生稳健策略。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，MaskVLA相比π0和OpenVLA-OFT分别取得平均成功率提升23.2%和16.8%；在真实ALOHA机器人实验中也验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskVLA通过简单的主相机视觉掩码微调，缓解轨迹过拟合并增强视觉特征学习，能够提升VLA模型在复杂操作任务中的成功率与真实机器人泛化性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate vision-language understanding with executable robot actions, enabling end-to-end learning for robot control. However, our empirical analysis reveals that existing models exhibit severe trajectory overfitting when finetuned on limited datasets. To guide the model in effectively utilizing wrist camera information, we propose MaskVLA, a masking-based fine-tuning strategy. By randomly masking a small portion of the main camera's visual information, the model is guided to autonomously learn more fine-grained, task-relevant, and effective visual features. This process leads to the emergence of robust policies, thereby enhancing the model's capability to tackle complex manipulation tasks and improving its generalization performance. Our method has been comprehensively evaluated on RoboTwin 2.0, achieving an average success rate improvement of 23.2% and 16.8% compared to $\pi_0$ and OpenVLA-OFT, respectively. Furthermore, experiments on real-world ALOHA robots also demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [9] [Towards robust multimodal 3D object detection via visual foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23541)
*Ziying Song,Lin Liu,Hongyu Pan,Shaoqing Xu,Lei Yang,Mingzhe Guo,Caiyan Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoboDistill多模态3D目标检测框架，利用SAM等视觉基础模型提升自动驾驶感知在OOD损坏下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR与相机融合的3D检测方法在传感器噪声、恶劣天气和环境变化导致的分布外损坏下，难以保持鲁棒性。

Method: 设计四个关键模块：SAM-AD在自动驾驶图像上微调SAM以提取语义特征；AD-FPN多尺度细化与上采样SAM特征，实现与LiDAR特征融合；DGWA深度引导小波注意力抑制高频传感器噪声并保留上下文信息；KD Fusion以SAM-AD为教师，将高质量视觉知识蒸馏到轻量点云网络。

Result: 在27种挑战性OOD损坏设置中，RoboDistill相较代表性SOTA方法通常取得更强或具竞争力的检测性能与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了视觉基础模型与3D目标检测之间的差距，推动了面向真实自动驾驶应用的鲁棒多模态感知发展。

Abstract: Multimodal 3D object detection is fundamental to robust perception in autonomous driving because it integrates complementary information from LiDAR and camera sensors. However, existing methods often fail to maintain robustness under out-of-distribution (OOD) corruptions caused by sensor noise, adverse weather, and environmental changes. To address this problem, we propose RoboDistill, a robust and generalizable multimodal 3D object detection framework that leverages visual foundation models (VFMs), such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM). First, we introduce SAM-AD, a domain-specific pretraining strategy that fine-tunes SAM on autonomous-driving imagery to extract feature representations with rich semantic information. Second, we design the AD Feature Pyramid Network (AD-FPN) to refine and upsample SAM features at multiple scales for seamless fusion with LiDAR features. Third, we develop the Depth-Guided Wavelet Attention (DGWA) module, which suppresses high-frequency sensor noise while preserving critical contextual information. Finally, we introduce KD Fusion, in which the pretrained SAM-AD serves as a teacher that distills high-quality visual knowledge into a lightweight point-cloud network, thereby improving robustness under noisy conditions. Extensive experiments across 27 challenging OOD corruption settings show that RoboDistill generally delivers stronger or competitive detection performance and robustness relative to representative state-of-the-art methods. This work bridges the gap between VFMs and 3D object detection and advances robust multimodal perception for real-world autonomous-driving applications.

</details>


### [10] [Algebraic Consistency Alone Does Not Certify Temporal Structure in Latent Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23478)
*Di Wen,Ruodi Zhang,Kailun Yang,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑潜动作模型中“代数误差数量级下降即可证明编码了时间结构”的结论，指出该指标可能主要反映重建目标与解码器族，而非真实时间配对，并提出更严格的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期方法对动作无关视频两帧间的转移学习潜动作码，并施加加性组合与逆序反对称正则化，以标签无关的代数误差下降作为捕获时间结构的证据。作者旨在检验这一推断是否成立。

Method: 分析表明，重建目标会使解码转移趋近状态特征之差，而该形式对任意配对都满足加性与反对称恒等式，因此指标无法区分真实时间结构、无关状态编码或坐标约定。作者在五个源域比较受约束与无约束模型，并在破坏时间配对后重训约束模型，同时在LIBERO-GOAL、LIBERO-SPATIAL及线性动作解码上评估，还测试了violation-contrastive修复目标。

Result: 无约束但有训练的模型相对未训练锚点已取得83-97%的误差下降；残余误差同样受解码器族影响。破坏时间配对后重训的约束模型在各域真实数据上误差仍低于无约束模型。下游任务中保留时间配对无一致优势；随着代数误差改善，动作码的线性动作可解码性反而下降。violation-contrastive目标在测试配置下仅带来训练与测试三元组上的边际分离。

Conclusion: 代数误差下降不能证明潜动作码捕获了时间结构，现有评估缺少充分对照。作者建议采用基线校正指标、破坏时间配对后重新训练以及种子预算分析等验证协议。

Abstract: Latent action models infer a code for the transition between two frames of action-free video. Recent methods regularise this code to compose additively and reverse antisymmetrically, and report order-of-magnitude reductions in the resulting errors as a label-free certificate that the code has captured temporal structure. We show that this conclusion does not follow. Reconstruction drives the decoded transition toward a difference of state features, for which both identities hold for any pairing, a solution the metric cannot distinguish from one encoding nuisance state or a coordinate convention. Across five source domains, a trained but unconstrained counterpart already achieves 83-97% of the reduction relative to an untrained anchor. The residual fold is governed as much by the decoder family as by what is learned. A constrained model retrained after its temporal pairing is destroyed still reaches, in each domain, a lower error than the unconstrained model on real data. Downstream, preserving the temporal pairing yields no consistent advantage on LIBERO-GOAL or LIBERO-SPATIAL, and across the tested arms the code's mean linear action decodability falls as the algebraic error improves. We also test the most direct repair, a violation-contrastive objective that requires the algebra to fail on destroyed pairings: in the tested configurations it yields only a marginal separation within the reconstruction budget, on training and test triples alike. We recommend a validation protocol that these methods currently lack: a baseline-corrected metric, retraining on destroyed pairings, and a seed-budget analysis.

</details>


### [11] [BiView-Touch: Learning Bimanual Tactile Representations by Cross-Hand Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23352)
*Chenxin Liang,Youchen Lai,Chuqiao Lyu,Tianxing Chen,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BiView-Touch，一种仅依赖触觉的双手机交互表征学习框架：通过用同手可见区域和同步的对侧完整手补全被掩码的目标手潜在表示，学习跨手依赖；在 HumanTouch 和 BVT-20 上验证了低标签性能与迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手交互会产生同一物理过程的互补触觉视图，但现有触觉表征学习多独立建模双手，或仅在下游预测时融合，未充分探索跨手关系。

Method: BiView-Touch 仅使用触觉。学生编码器与几何条件方向解码器从目标手可见区域及同步的对侧完整手预测全视图 EMA 潜在目标；引入时间与布局反事实，促使模型对同步且解剖组织的源信息敏感。通过受控消融和源上下文干预验证其机制。

Result: 消融与干预表明，性能收益来自对时间对齐且解剖组织的对侧触觉上下文的结构化跨手依赖，而非仅双侧输入。在 HumanTouch 上，冻结表征在低标签设置中持续优于代表性自监督基线；仅用 5% 下游标签时，双侧手腕运动识别平衡准确率相对提升 7.1%，力推导交互阶段识别相对提升 14.1%。还提出 BVT-20 的 20 任务双侧触觉数据集，并展示跨录音会话、跨预训练语料及留出双手任务的迁移。

Conclusion: BiView-Touch 有效利用双手中跨手互补结构，提升触觉表征的低标签泛化与迁移能力；代码和数据集细节见匿名项目页。

Abstract: Bimanual interaction produces complementary tactile views of the same physical process, yet existing tactile representation learning largely models the two hands independently or combines them only for downstream prediction, leaving their cross-hand relationship unexplored. To exploit this overlooked structure, we introduce BiView-Touch, a tactile-only framework that completes masked target-hand latents from the remaining visible target-hand regions and the synchronized full contralateral hand. A student encoder with a geometry-conditioned directional decoder predicts full-view EMA latent targets, while temporal and layout counterfactuals encourage sensitivity to synchronized and anatomically organized source information. Controlled ablations and source-context interventions show that BiView-Touch learns structured cross-hand dependence on temporally aligned and anatomically organized contralateral tactile context, rather than benefiting from bilateral input alone. On the public HumanTouch dataset, its frozen representations consistently outperform representative self-supervised baselines across low-label settings. With only 5\% downstream labels, BiView-Touch achieves relative balanced-accuracy gains of 7.1\% on bilateral wrist-motion recognition and 14.1\% on force-derived interaction-phase recognition. We further introduce BVT-20, a 20-task bilateral tactile dataset, and demonstrate transfer across recording sessions and pretraining corpora, including transfer to a held-out bimanual task. Our code and dataset details are available on the anonymous project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [M3GA-Wild: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Multi-Modal Multi-session Ground-to-Aerial Place Recognition in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23003)
*Ethan Griffiths,Maryam Haghighat,Simon Denman,Clinton Fookes,Milad Ramezani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 M3GA-Wild，首个面向森林环境的跨地面-空中、多模态、多会话地点识别基准，统一并扩展已有森林定位数据集，提供同步 RGB、LiDAR、航拍影像/多高度 LiDAR 与 6-DoF 位姿标注，并给出基线评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林等非结构化自然环境中，跨平台、跨视角、长期自主定位面临严重视角差异、模态错位和域间隙，但缺乏统一且多模态的 ground-to-aerial 地点识别基准。

Method: 构建 M3GA-Wild 基准：整合地面 36 km 轨迹的同步 RGB 与 LiDAR，配准 370 公顷高分辨率航拍影像与多高度 LiDAR，并提供精确地理参考 6-DoF 位姿；覆盖不同视角、遮挡和环境条件，支持视觉、LiDAR、跨模态、多模态方法及单目深度估计基础模型评测。

Result: 基线实验表明：在严重视角差异下，基于 LiDAR 的方法显著优于纯视觉方法；现有多模态融合策略因跨模态对齐较差而收益有限；利用航拍 RGB 与地理参考航拍 LiDAR 评测单目深度基础模型时，现有方法仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 结果凸显跨平台定位中的模态错位和严重域间隙等关键挑战；M3GA-Wild 为鲁棒多模态定位与非结构化自然环境长期自主性研究建立了新基准，数据集和代码将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: We present M3GA-Wild, the first benchmark for multi-modal, multi-session ground-to-aerial place recognition in forests. M3GA-Wild unifies and extends existing forest localisation datasets, providing a holistic benchmark with synchronised RGB imagery and LiDAR from ground traversals spanning 36 km, aligned high-resolution aerial imagery and multi-altitude LiDAR covering 370 hectares, and accurate geo-referenced 6-DoF poses for precise evaluation. M3GA-Wild captures diverse forest scenes with varying viewpoints, occlusion, and environmental conditions, enabling systematic evaluation of visual, LiDAR, cross-modal, and multi-modal methods. Baseline experiments show that LiDAR-based approaches significantly outperform vision-only methods under severe viewpoint differences, while current multi-modal fusion strategies yield limited gains due to poor cross-modal alignment. By pairing aerial RGB imagery with geo-referenced aerial LiDAR, M3GA-Wild also enables evaluation of foundation models for monocular depth estimation as a cheap source of 3D geometry from forest imagery, with initial experiments revealing shortfalls of current methods. These results highlight key challenges in cross-platform localisation, including modality misalignment and severe domain gaps. M3GA-Wild establishes a new benchmark to support research in robust multi-modal localisation and long-term autonomy in unstructured natural environments. The dataset and code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [13] [General Collaborative Intelligence: Architecting Cognition for Resilient Multi-Agent Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22967)
*Lei Zhang,Chun Ye,Le Yang,Zhaozhong Wang,Deng-Ping Fan,Hang Dai,Binglu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述面向多智能体无人系统的协同智能，提出五维分类法与三项认知协同条件，系统梳理协作架构、神经通信、MARL具身闭环与韧性机制，并映射至V2X、无人机、工业物流和智慧城市；同时提出GCI-Bench五支柱评分协议与成熟度模型，以缓解基准饱和和评估碎片化，最后讨论可复现性、虚实迁移和协同退化等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体无人系统正从孤立、以自我为中心的感知转向协作智能，以克服遮挡、有限传感器范围和环境退化等单智能体无法摆脱的局部观测陷阱。现有综述多孤立考察架构、通信、具身、韧性与信任等维度，鲜少揭示其依赖关系，且面临基准饱和与评估碎片化。

Method: 本文通过两个互补视角进行统一综合：一是覆盖协作阶段、通信范式、融合架构、学习策略与应用领域的五维分类法；二是将认知协同操作化为语义消歧、语用信息交换与主动信息觅食三项条件。综述协作架构与拓扑、将信道视为可微管线组件的神经通信协同设计、基于多智能体强化学习的具身感知-动作闭环，以及同步、不确定性量化和标签高效学习等韧性机制；并将进展映射到V2X、无人机、工业物流与智慧城市四类应用域及安全-隐私-效用三元组。进一步提出GCI-Bench五支柱评分协议和成熟度模型。

Result: 形成五维分类与三项认知协同条件的统一分析框架，梳理协作架构/拓扑、神经通信协同设计、MARL具身闭环和韧性机制，映射四类应用域与安全-隐私-效用权衡；提出GCI-Bench五支柱评分协议与成熟度模型，以缓解基准饱和和评估碎片化，并识别可复现性、sim-to-real鸿沟及协作性能退化条件等挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为多智能体无人系统协同智能提供了统一术语、评估与路线图，强调需在真实世界不确定性下解决可复现性、虚实迁移和协作退化等问题，推动通用协同智能发展。

Abstract: Multi-agent unmanned systems are moving from isolated, ego-centric sensing toward collaborative intelligence, in which distributed agents exchange compact features to overcome a local observation trap that no single agent can escape: occlusions, finite sensor range, and environmental degradation. The field has matured across architectural, communication, embodied, resilience, and trust dimensions, yet existing surveys examine these dimensions in isolation and rarely expose their dependencies. This review offers a unified synthesis through two complementary lenses. The first is a five-dimensional taxonomy spanning collaboration stage, communication paradigm, fusion architecture, learning strategy, and application domain. The second is three cognitive synergy conditions, Semantic Disambiguation, Pragmatic Information Exchange, and Proactive Informational Foraging, that turn cognitive synergy into operational criteria. Across these lenses we survey collaboration architectures and topologies, neural-communication co-design that treats the channel as a differentiable pipeline component, embodied action-perception loops via multi-agent reinforcement learning, and resilience mechanisms for synchronization, uncertainty quantification, and label-efficient learning. We then map these advances onto four operational domains, V2X, unmanned aerial, industrial logistics, and smart cities, and onto the safety-privacy-utility triad. To counter benchmark saturation and evaluation fragmentation, we propose GCI-Bench, a five-pillar scoring protocol with a maturity model that makes the trade-offs of collaborative methods comparable across studies. A critical reflection on reproducibility, the sim-to-real gulf, and conditions under which collaboration degrades performance identifies open challenges and charts directions toward general collaborative intelligence under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [14] [Image Frame Dynamic Object Segmentation and Ego Motion Estimation using Radar Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22857)
*Astik Srivastava,Suhani Grover,Avinash Sharma,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Radar-Dot，一种利用雷达多普勒测量联合解决动态目标分割与自运动估计耦合问题的雷达-RGB框架；在 nuScenes 子集上验证了动态分割性能，并表明基于雷达静态点的滤波可提升自速度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶中，动态目标分割与自运动估计相互依赖：准确自运动估计通常需要静态场景观测，而识别静态观测又需要已知自运动。该耦合问题使二者难以独立求解，因此需要一种能同时利用两种模态信息的方法。

Method: Radar-Dot 首先利用雷达回波通过线性多普勒约束估计自速度，并采用基于残差的静态/动态分割与鲁棒估计来降低运动目标影响；随后将估计运动与度量深度、稠密光流结合，识别图像中观测运动与刚性场景运动不一致的区域，从而实现动态目标分割。

Result: 在 10 个 nuScenes 场景（nuscenes-mini 的一部分）的 394 个帧对上，该几何流水线取得 20.24% 的动态 IoU 和 33.67% 的 F1-score；同时，基于雷达的静态点滤波相比使用全部雷达回波，提升了自速度估计效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，雷达可作为联合提升自运动估计与动态目标分割的有效模态，展示了 Radar-Dot 框架在解决二者耦合问题上的潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic object segmentation and ego-motion estimation are closely coupled problems in autonomous driving, as accurate ego-motion estimation typically requires static scene observations, while identifying static observations requires knowledge of the ego motion. We present Radar-Dot, a radar--RGB framework that exploits radar Doppler measurements to address this coupling. Radar returns are first used to estimate ego velocity through a linear Doppler constraint, with residual-based static/dynamic segmentation and robust estimation used to reduce the influence of moving objects. The estimated motion is then combined with metric depth and dense optical flow to identify image regions whose observed motion is inconsistent with the rigid scene motion. Experiments on 10 nuScenes scenes (part of nuscenes-mini) demonstrate that the resulting geometric pipeline achieves 20.24% dynamic IoU and 33.67% F1-score over 394 frame pairs, while radar-based static-point filtering improves ego-velocity estimation compared with using all radar returns. These results demonstrate the potential of radar as a modality for jointly improving ego-motion estimation and dynamic object segmentation.

</details>

### [15] [Partial-Scan-and-Move Source Seeking for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23786)
*Bo Wang,Ishvar Sitaldin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种部分扫描-移动的源搜索策略：机器人利用偏移标量传感器仅扫描部分圆周即可估计梯度，并通过置信集决策移动，最终高概率在有限步内到达源附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统源搜索通常要求机器人每步移动前完成完整圆周扫描，耗时且低效；本文关注何时部分圆周测量已足够确定下一步动作。

Method: 机器人旋转采集标量场测量；针对部分扫描建立梯度估计方法，并构建考虑测量噪声与局部场变化的置信集；机器人据此判断是否已接近源，或是否已有足够信息沿下降方向移动。

Result: 在适当条件下，每次决策可在规定部分扫描内完成；机器人能以高概率在有限次移动内到达源的期望邻域。

Conclusion: 部分扫描信息足以支持源搜索决策，可减少完整扫描需求，并给出有限步、高概率收敛到源附近的理论保证。

Abstract: This paper presents a partial-scan-and-move strategy for source seeking with a mobile robot equipped with an offset scalar sensor. At each robot position, the sensor collects source field measurements while the robot rotates. Instead of requiring a complete circular scan before every move, we ask when the measurements collected over only part of the circle are already sufficient to determine the next action. We develop a gradient estimation method for partial scans together with a confidence set that accounts for measurement noise and local field variation. The robot uses this confidence set to decide whether it is close enough to the source or has enough information to move in a descent direction. We show that, under suitable conditions, each decision can be made within a prescribed partial scan and that the robot reaches a desired neighborhood of the source in finitely many moves with high probability.

</details>


### [16] [Task aware Dynamic Movement Primitives for failure detection and recovery in contact rich manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23151)
*Bhavnashri A,Sobia Shafi,Krishnapuram Himavarshini,Anuj Tiwari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任务感知的失败检测与恢复框架，将基于DMP的轨迹生成与实时阶段分类结合，用于紧公差销孔装配；通过QDA分类接近、对齐和插入阶段，并用马氏距离检测接触失败，触发螺旋搜索恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习示范框架如DMP能编码单次示范轨迹，但对初始抓取配置变化和外部接触力敏感，在接触丰富阶段易失败，因此需要任务感知的失败检测与恢复机制。

Method: 方法集成DMP轨迹生成与实时阶段分类。使用多模态传感器数据训练QDA，将销孔装配执行分为接近、对齐和插入阶段；以目标相对位置为特征，使分类无需重训练即可泛化到未见目标位置。在线使用基于力特征的马氏距离进行异常检测，区分接触失败与正常轨迹执行；检测到失败后，触发螺旋搜索恢复策略，在接触下主动重新对齐销，再恢复学习到的DMP插入。

Result: 实验表明，阶段分类准确率达到95%，并且仅使用单次示范即可在横向错位达3 mm的情况下实现可靠失败恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架将任务阶段分类、异常检测与恢复策略结合，提升了DMP在销孔装配等接触丰富任务中的鲁棒性，并具备对未见目标位置和一定初始错位的泛化能力。

Abstract: Assembly remains a challenging robotic manipulation task in presence of tight tolerances and complex contact interactions. While Learning from Demonstration (LfD) frameworks like Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) can effectively encode trajectories from a single demonstration, they are highly sensitive to variations in initial grasp configurations and external contact forces. Such variations often lead to task failures during the contact rich phases. This paper presents a task aware failure detection and recovery framework that integrates DMP based trajectory generation with real time stage classification. Utilizing Quadratic Discriminant Analysis (QDA) trained on multimodal sensor data, the framework segments execution into approach, alignment, and insertion stages for a Peg in Hole (PiH) assembly operation. By using goal relative position data as features, this classification generalizes to unseen goal positions without requiring retraining, matching the inherent generalization capability of DMPs. Anomaly detection is performed online using a Mahalanobis distance metric computed over force features, isolating contact induced failures from nominal trajectory execution. Upon failure detection, a spiral search recovery policy is triggered to actively realign the peg under contact before resuming the learned DMP insertion. The proposed approach is evaluated on an experimental setup achieving 95% stage classification accuracy, and demonstrates reliable failure recovery under lateral misalignments of up to 3 mm using only a single demonstration.

</details>


### [17] [Learning and Control Beyond Linearity: Towards a Non-asymptotic Theory for Bilinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22338)
*Yahya Sattar,Yassir Jedra,Robin Strässer,Frank Allgöwer,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该教程统一梳理了双线性学习与控制这一新兴领域，重点讨论有限样本学习保证、双线性观测下的控制以及稳定性设计，并展望与强化学习、机器学习的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以线性系统为基准，阐明双线性设定中哪些本质性质发生变化，以及近期理论如何应对有限样本学习与控制问题；同时将其与非线性控制、表示学习和数据驱动决策等更广泛主题联系起来。

Method: 学习方面强调适用于双线性设定的工具，如针对相依重尾协变量的一侧Bernstein不等式、分块论证和输入相关噪声的鞅集中不等式，并应用于完全观测双线性系统、部分观测双线性系统以及带双线性观测的线性系统。控制方面讨论双线性观测下的二次控制，指出经典分离原理失效，回顾基于信念空间滚动时域控制的可行方法；还涵盖状态反馈下双线性动力学的镇定，使用半定规划、LMI松弛、平方和方法和基于Koopman的提升。

Result: 该教程给出了双线性学习与控制的统一视角，展示了上述工具如何为多类双线性系统提供有限样本学习保证，并总结了双线性观测下控制与镇定的可处理方法。

Conclusion: 最后讨论了与强化学习和机器学习的联系，并指出双线性系统联合学习与控制中的若干开放问题。

Abstract: This tutorial provides a unified view of the emerging area of bilinear learning and control. Using linear systems as a benchmark, it explains what fundamentally changes in the bilinear settings, how recent theory addresses finite-sample learning and control, and how these ideas connect to broader themes in nonlinear control, representation learning, and data-driven decision making. For learning, we emphasize tools that are particularly useful in the bilinear settings, such as one-sided Bernstein's inequality for dependent and heavy-tailed covariates, blocking arguments, and martingale concentration for input-dependent noise. We then apply these tools to obtain finite-sample learning guarantees for fully observed bilinear systems, partially observed bilinear systems, and linear systems with bilinear observations. For control, we discuss quadratic control from bilinear observations, where the classical separation principle fails, and review tractable approaches based on belief-space receding horizon control. We also cover stabilization of bilinear dynamics under state feedback using semi-definite programming, LMI relaxations, sum-of-squares methods, and Koopman-based lifting. We conclude by discussing connections to reinforcement learning and machine learning, and some open problems in combined learning and control of bilinear systems.

</details>

### [18] [An Affordable AI-Integrated Smart Cane for Multimodal Mobility Assistance of Visually Impaired Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22277)
*Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Toqeer Ali Syed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种售价88美元、完全离线的AI智能手杖，基于Raspberry Pi Zero 2W，融合RGB视觉与ToF测距，采用INT8量化SSD MobileNet V1，提供距离感知振动触觉与实时音频警报。室内场景宏平均F1为0.82，端到端平均延迟330 ms，峰值功耗2.8 W；12人初步可用性研究SUS为78.5，用户感知积极且障碍意识增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过22亿人受视觉障碍影响，传统白杖无法检测高处障碍或提供语义化环境上下文；现有AI辅助导航系统通常依赖昂贵硬件或云连接，在资源受限场景中可及性不足。

Method: 设计低成本、全离线、多模态移动辅助智能手杖，在超低功耗Raspberry Pi Zero 2W上运行。系统融合RGB视觉感知与ToF距离估计，将INT8量化的SSD MobileNet V1模型与距离感知振动触觉反馈、实时音频警报相结合。为保证受限硬件上的运行稳健性，采用多进程架构，将传感器采集、神经网络推理和触觉反馈隔离为独立进程，并支持失效安全传感。评估包括室内移动场景实验和12名参与者的初步可用性研究。

Result: 室内移动场景实验中，宏平均F1-score为0.82，精确率为0.85，召回率为0.81；端到端平均延迟为330 ms，峰值功耗为2.8 W。12名参与者的初步可用性研究显示SUS为78.5，NASA-TLX（摘要未给出具体分数），用户感知积极，障碍感知能力增强。

Conclusion: 该原型验证了在成本敏感型移动辅助场景中部署隐私保护、边缘原生辅助智能的可行性。

Abstract: Visual impairment affects over 2.2 billion people worldwide, yet conventional white canes cannot detect elevated hazards or provide semantic environmental context. Existing AI-assisted navigation systems typically rely on expensive hardware or cloud connectivity, limiting accessibility in resource-constrained settings. This paper presents an affordable (\$88 USD), fully offline AI-integrated smart cane designed for multimodal mobility assistance on an ultra-low-power Raspberry Pi Zero 2W. The system fuses RGB vision sensing with Time-of-Flight (ToF) distance estimation, pairing an INT8-quantized SSD MobileNet V1 model with distance-aware vibrotactile feedback and real-time audio alerts. To ensure operational robustness on constrained hardware, a multiprocessing architecture isolates sensor acquisition, neural inference, and haptic feedback into independent processes with fail-safe sensing support. Experimental evaluation across indoor mobility scenarios demonstrates a macro-averaged F1-score of 0.82 (precision: 0.85, recall: 0.81), a mean end-to-end latency of 330\,ms, and a peak power draw of 2.8\,W. A preliminary usability study with 12 participants (SUS: 78.5, NASA-TLX) demonstrated positive user perception and enhanced obstacle awareness. The proposed prototype validates the feasibility of deploying privacy-preserving, edge-native assistive intelligence for cost-sensitive mobility assistance.

</details>

### [19] [Conflicting Pattern Formation by Teams of Anonymous, Fully Disoriented Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23454)
*Animesh Maiti,Prakhar Shukla,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究两组匿名、自主、oblivious 移动机器人同时执行冲突任务：一组聚集、一组圆周形成；在完全失向、无轴一致与共同手性的条件下，提出针对半同步、非刚性运动机器人的分布式算法，并采用分组弱多重检测假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作依赖方向轴一致和全局弱多重检测；本文旨在探究在更弱的完全失向机器人模型下，该冲突模式形成问题是否仍然可解。

Method: 在二维欧氏平面中，两组机器人各自知道自身任务但无法识别同组其他成员；去除轴一致和共同手性。作者设计分布式算法，针对半同步且非刚性运动的失向机器人，并假设聚集组具备全局弱多重检测，圆周形成组仅需局部弱多重检测。

Result: 所提算法能够在半同步失向机器人、非刚性运动条件下同时解决聚集与圆周形成这一冲突问题，且对多重检测能力的要求较此前工作更低并可按组区分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该冲突模式形成问题在完全失向设置下对半同步机器人是可行的，从而扩展了先前结果，并为更弱假设下的多组协作提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Two groups of autonomous, anonymous, and oblivious mobile robots are deployed in the two-dimensional Euclidean plane, each assigned a distinct task. We study a setting where the two groups must simultaneously solve two conflicting pattern formation problems: the \textit{gathering problem}, where robots gather at a point not known to them a priori, and the \textit{circle formation problem}, where robots occupy distinct positions on the boundary of a circle. Although each robot knows its own task, it cannot identify other members of its group. A prior solution~\cite{Conflict-1} addressed this problem for asynchronous robots having {\it direction-only axis agreement} and {\it global weak multiplicity detection} capability available to all robots in both groups. In contrast, in this work, we consider fully {\it disoriented robots} without any axis agreement or common \textit{chirality}. We study the feasibility of a solution to this problem for {\it disoriented robots}. We propose a distributed algorithm that solves the problem for semi-synchronous disoriented robots with non-rigid movements. Our proposed algorithm assumes global weak multiplicity detection only for the gathering group, while for the circle formation group, it requires local weak multiplicity detection.

</details>

### [20] [CHOREO: Every Humanoid Skill as a Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22274)
*Ziyi Sun,Jingwen Chen,Yuxi Wang,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yujun Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHOREO 提出无需训练即可组合异构人形技能，将不同来源技能统一为可执行运动轨迹，并在 Unitree G1/MuJoCo 上实现高成功率多动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人技能虽已通过强化学习、运动模仿和生成建模取得进展，但因表示、接口与控制器不兼容而彼此孤立，难以复用和组合。

Method: 核心观察是任何技能最终都可表达为可执行运动轨迹。CHOREO 将各能力转换为 SkillMotion 统一表示，融合运动状态、接触、语义和边界条件；再通过直接续接、三次 Hermite 混合或经验证的桥接动作进行组合，无需重训源模型或测试时更新模型。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 中的 Unitree G1 上，CHOREO 组织来自异构源的 2950 个已接纳 SkillMotion 资产，在 130 个多动作任务上达到 95.4% 序列成功率，其中八动作序列成功率为 93.8%。

Conclusion: 可执行轨迹为积累和组合预训练人形机器人能力提供了可扩展的统一接口，证明了无需训练即可实现异构技能组合的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics have produced diverse skills through reinforcement learning, motion imitation, and generative modeling. Yet these capabilities remain siloed because they are built around incompatible representations, interfaces, and controllers. We present CHOREO, a framework for training-free composition of heterogeneous humanoid skills. Our key observation is that, regardless of how a skill is learned, it can ultimately be expressed as an executable motion trajectory. Based on this observation, CHOREO converts each capability into SkillMotion, a unified representation that combines motion states, contacts, semantics, and boundary conditions. Skills are composed through direct continuation, cubic Hermite blending, or validated bridge motions, without retraining source models or updating models at test time. On Unitree G1 in MuJoCo, CHOREO organizes 2,950 admitted SkillMotion assets derived from heterogeneous sources and achieves 95.4\% sequence success across 130 multi-action tasks, including 93.8\% success on eight-action sequences. These results demonstrate that executable trajectories provide a scalable interface for accumulating and composing pretrained humanoid capabilities.

</details>


### [21] [Selective Commitment for Language-Guided Object Retrieval under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23131)
*Wonhee Koh,Sushil Samuel Dinesh,Hansol Ko,Shinkyu Park,Eungjoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环框架，在部分可观测条件下协调证据收集、场景交互、抓取承诺与弃权，用于依据参考容器关系检索语言指定目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的目标检索在真实机器人中常面临遮挡与部分可观测性，系统需在信息不足时主动收集证据，并在抓取候选、继续观察或弃权之间做出风险可控的决策。

Method: 维护关于目标身份、容器关系与目标存在性的持久联合信念，包含已跟踪目标、未观测目标与目标缺失三类假设；利用视角条件化的类别式VLM观测更新信念；以共形抓取合格性与机器人可行性约束承诺决策；通过有限时域信念空间规划选择信息收集动作。

Result: 在五类场景的仿真中，方法成功19/25，最佳任务适配基线为12/25，且是唯一在每个场景中至少成功一次的策略。消融显示跨视角记忆可提升部分遮挡下的成功率，但完整系统并未始终优于简化变体。真机试验表明可闭环再观察并从注入的抓取失败中自主恢复，而注入的视角失败会导致错误弃权。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在部分可观测检索任务中统一协调证据收集与选择性抓取承诺是可行的，同时揭示了视角故障与错误弃权等可靠性挑战。

Abstract: Language-guided object retrieval under partial observability requires deciding whether to gather more evidence, interact with the scene, grasp a candidate, or abstain. We present a closed-loop framework that coordinates these decisions for retrieving a target specified in relation to a reference container. The framework maintains a persistent joint belief over target identity, container relation, and presence through tracked-object, unobserved-target, and target-absent hypotheses. View-conditioned categorical VLM observations update this belief; conformal grasp eligibility and robot feasibility govern commitment, while finite-horizon belief-space planning selects information-gathering actions. Across five different scenarios, our proposed method succeeds in 19/25 simulation episodes versus 12/25 for the best-performing task-adapted baseline and is the only evaluated policy to achieve at least one success in each scenario. Ablations show that cross-view memory improves success under partial occlusion, while the full system does not consistently outperform simplified variants. Real-robot trials demonstrate closed-loop re-observation and autonomous recovery from injected grasp failures, while injected viewpoint failures end in false defer. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of coordinating evidence gathering and selective grasp commitment within a unified framework for retrieval under partial observability.

</details>


### [22] [Verti-WM: A Physics-Aided Exteroceptive World Model for Off-Road Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23118)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Verti-WM：一种物理辅助的外感知世界模型，用于越野导航强化学习。它递归融合冻结 Transformer（刚性地形）与神经符号地形力学模型（可变形地形），并利用地图查询的高程与语义信息进行条件化，实现无需额外仿真器访问的六自由度策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航强化学习依赖大量车辆-地形交互数据，而高保真仿真中采集成本高昂。世界模型可替代策略优化中的仿真器 rollout，但越野世界模型必须基于外感知地形信息而非仅本体感知来建模状态转移。此外，刚性地形与可变形地形需要不同建模方式，数据驱动方法与物理方法各有优势，需互补融合。

Method: Verti-WM 递归融合两类模型：用于刚性地形的冻结 Transformer，以及用于可变形地形的神经符号地形力学模型。在每个预测位姿处，从给定地图查询高程和语义观测，用作融合条件，从而支持六自由度 rollout 用于策略优化，且无需进一步访问仿真器。

Result: 与数据驱动基线和物理基线相比，Verti-WM 将预测误差分别降低 34.6% 和 21.7%。完全在 Verti-WM 中训练的策略达到相当的任务成功率，同时相比直接在高保真仿真器中训练减少 23.6 倍计算时间。使用真实世界数据验证后，可在学习到的真实世界运动动力学中进行策略优化，在 Verti-4-Wheeler 平台上达到 80% 成功率，而直接 sim-to-real 迁移为 40%。

Conclusion: Verti-WM 通过融合数据驱动与物理建模，并引入外感知地形条件，提升了越野世界模型的预测精度与策略优化效率。它减少了对高保真仿真器的依赖，并改善了真实世界迁移性能，为越野导航强化学习提供了一种有效且可扩展的世界模型方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for off-road navigation requires extensive vehicle-terrain interaction data, which are costly to collect in high-fidelity simulation. World models offer a promising alternative by replacing simulator roll-outs during policy optimization. However, an off-road world model must condition state transitions on exteroceptive terrain information, which proprioception alone does not provide. This challenge is further amplified by the need to model both rigid and deformable terrain, where data-driven and physics-based approaches offer complementary strengths. We propose Verti-WM, a physics-aided exteroceptive world model that recurrently fuses a frozen Transformer for rigid terrain and a neuro-symbolic terramechanics model for deformable terrain. Elevation and semantic observations queried from a supplied map at each predicted pose condition fusion, enabling six-degree-of-freedom rollouts for policy optimization without further simulator access. Verti-WM reduces prediction error by 34.6% and 21.7% over data-driven and physics-based baselines, respectively. Policies trained entirely within Verti-WM achieve comparable task success rates while reducing computation time by 23.6X relative to direct training in the high-fidelity simulator. We further validate Verti-WM using real-world data, enabling policy optimization within learned real-world kinodynamics and achieving a 80% success rate on the Verti-4-Wheeler platform, compared with 40% for direct sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [23] [Search, Ground, Plan: Functional Sufficiency for Task and Motion Planning under Incomplete Scene Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23113)
*Narendhiran Vijayakumar,Nav Singhal,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAB-TAMP 是一个基于基础模型的任务与运动规划框架：先搜索完成任务所需的场景实体，将功能角色接地到有效物理对象，并在完整联合分配确认功能充分性后才进行规划，以弥补不完整场景知识导致的“任务要求”与“物理可实现性”之间的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型已将任务与运动规划扩展到通过语言和视觉观察指定的操纵问题，但不完整的场景知识造成关键鸿沟：系统知道任务要求什么，却不一定知道物理场景能否真正实现它。

Method: 用功能角色、关系与分配约束表示任务；在需求仍未解决时增量检查场景，并通过语义、几何和关系检查验证候选对象；仅在完整联合分配建立功能充分性后开始规划。

Result: 在 Kitchen、Living Room、Workshop 三个领域的 32 个场景变体上评估；200 个可行试验中，端到端成功率为 54.0%，规划目标覆盖率为 67.3%；在同一执行设置下，相较三个基于基础模型的 TAMP 框架，端到端成功率比基线均值提高 25.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在规划前显式搜索场景实体、验证功能角色接地并确保功能充分性，可有效提升语言与视觉条件下 TAMP 在真实操纵场景中的可执行性和端到端成功率。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have expanded task and motion planning (TAMP) to manipulation problems specified through language and visual observations. However, incomplete scene knowledge leaves a critical gap between understanding what the task requires and knowing whether the physical scene can actually realize it. We introduce GRAB-TAMP, an FM-based TAMP framework that searches for scene entities required for task completion, grounds functional roles to valid physical objects, and plans only after a complete joint assignment establishes functional sufficiency. We represent the task through functional roles, relations, and assignment constraints, and incrementally inspect the scene while requirements remain unresolved, verifying candidate objects through semantic, geometric, and relational checks. We evaluate GRAB-TAMP across 32 scene variants spanning Kitchen, Living Room, and Workshop domains. Across 200 feasible trials, our approach achieves 54.0% end-to-end success with 67.3% plan goal coverage. Compared with three FM-based TAMP frameworks under the same execution setting, GRAB-TAMP improves end-to-end success by 25.7 percentage points over the mean baseline. Implementation and evaluation code: this https URL
