### [24] [DiagGen: Agentic Generation of Deformable Assets with Sim-based Diagnostics for Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23103)
*Guanxiong Chen,Yiduo Qu,Qianjun Xia,Pengyu Jing,Yixian Cheng,Bole Ma,Pengzhi Yang,Bingyang Zhou,Ziming Li,Shashwat Suri,Gongbo Sun,Chao Liu,Peter Yichen Chen,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DiagGen：一种智能体框架，可将单张野外图像通过“生成—仿真—诊断—精修”闭环转化为可用于仿真的可变形资产，并直接支持接触丰富的机器人抓放任务规划与仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形资产生成框架通常在生成完成后才评估物理合理性，未将物体在仿真中的响应作为反馈来修复上游生成错误；而仿真就绪的可变形资产对机器人操作任务至关重要。

Method: DiagGen 构建部件感知的几何与材质参数，并利用基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的智能体选择语义信息丰富的区域，在物理仿真器中探测这些区域、观察材质响应，并将有证据支持的修复线索路由到对应的生成阶段。

Result: 在 40 个资产上的实验表明，诊断能提供有用的修复线索，并适度提升生成可变形资产的质量；与视觉基础模型生成的资产可能不可仿真不同，DiagGen 生成的可变形资产可直接导入高保真物理仿真器，用于接触丰富的抓放任务规划与仿真。

Conclusion: 通过生成、仿真、诊断与精修的闭环反馈，DiagGen 提升了可仿真可变形资产生成质量，并验证了其在机器人操作仿真中的可用性。

Abstract: While simulation-ready deformable assets are essential for in-silico robotic manipulation tasks, existing generation frameworks typically assess physical plausibility after generation, leaving an object's simulated response unused as feedback for repairing upstream errors. We present DiagGen, an agentic framework that turns a single in-the-wild image into a simulation-ready deformable asset through a generate--simulate--diagnose--refine loop. DiagGen constructs part-aware geometry and material parameters, then uses a VLM (vision-language model)-based agent to select semantically informative regions, probe them in a physics simulator, observe material responses, and route evidence-backed repair cues to the responsible generation stage. Experiments on 40 assets show that diagnostics provides useful repair cues and can moderately improve the quality of generated deformable assets. Finally, we show that unlike assets generated from visual foundation models which may not be simulatable, DiagGen-generated deformables can be directly dropped into a high-fidelity physical simulator for the planning and simulation of contact-rich pick-and-place tasks. The project's website is this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [Splat-CBF: Safe Next-Best-View Control in 3D Gaussian-Splat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23100)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Splat-CBF，一种主动感知控制屏障函数，在硬安全约束下引导相机朝向下一最佳视角，从而同时解决未知环境中的导航与避障冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未建图环境中必须同时探索与移动，但两者相互冲突：最值得观测的区域恰是地图最不确定、碰撞裕度最不可信的区域。

Method: 用风险感知控制屏障函数将高斯场的平均风险价值（AVaR）转化为单一平滑硬约束以保障安全；用第二个屏障奖励机器人规划路径附近具有高期望Fisher信息增益的相机朝向。二者在二次规划中结合，安全为硬约束、感知为软约束，并采用随感知松弛次数和机器人接近不确定区域程度而自适应的松弛惩罚。

Result: 在室内仿真、Isaac Kinova机械臂和Ackermann驱动机器人实验中验证。相比仅安全基线和仅感知基线，机器人导航更快、收集信息更多、在线运行更快，并且仅在安全需要时放弃信息性运动。

Conclusion: Splat-CBF 有效解决了主动感知与安全避障之间的张力，以硬安全约束和软感知目标实现更快、更信息丰富且更安全的导航。

Abstract: Where to look and how to move? A robot navigating an unmapped environment must do both at once, and the two goals pull against each other. The regions most worth observing are the ones the map knows least about, and those are exactly where the robot cannot trust its collision margins. We resolve this tension by introducing Splat-CBF, an active perception control barrier function that steers the camera toward the next best view while collision avoidance is enforced as a hard constraint. Safety is enforced by a risk-aware control barrier function that turns the Average Value-at-Risk of the Gaussian field into a single smooth hard constraint. Perception is enforced by a second barrier that rewards camera orientations with high expected Fisher information gain near the robot's planned path. The two meet in a quadratic program where safety is hard and perception is soft, with a slack penalty that adapts to how often perception has already been relaxed and how close the robot is to an uncertain region. We verify the method in indoor simulations, a Isaac Kinova manipulator and in experiments on an Ackermann-drive robot. Our results assert that robot navigates faster, gathers more information, and runs faster online than safety-only and perception-only baselines, giving up informative motion only when safety requires it.

</details>


### [26] [On the Control of Mobile Ad-Hoc Agent Deployments in Partially Observed Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23060)
*Edwin Meriaux,Louis-Roy Langevin,Shuo Wen,Ndiamé Ndiaye,Gregory Dudek,Antonio Loría*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究未知正交环境中移动自组网的在线部署，证明 CADENCE 算法至多使用 n/2+h-2 个智能体即可实现全覆盖并保持可见图连通，同时通过部署顺序启发式降低实际智能体数量与部署时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动自组网在未知正交环境中的在线部署需要同时保证覆盖与连通，并尽量减少智能体数量和部署时间；为此将问题形式化为部分可观测协作守卫艺术画廊问题。

Method: 将在线部署问题形式化为 Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem；对 CADENCE 算法给出完整证明；并评估用于减少智能体数量和部署时间的部署顺序启发式。

Result: 完整证明 CADENCE 算法在具有 n 个拐角和 h 个孔洞的正交世界中，使用至多 n/2+h-2 个智能体即可实现全覆盖，并保持连通可见图；部署顺序启发式在实践中可减少智能体数量与部署时间。

Conclusion: CADENCE 为未知正交环境下移动自组网的在线部署提供了覆盖与连通性的理论保证，部署顺序启发式则能在实践中进一步提升部署效率。

Abstract: We study the online deployment of mobile ad hoc networks in unknown orthogonal environments, formalized as the Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem. We give a full proof that CADENCE algorithms achieve full coverage while maintaining a connected visibility graph using at most $n/2 + h - 2$ agents in orthogonal worlds with $n$ corners and $h$ holes. We further evaluate deployment-order heuristics that reduce agent count and deployment time in practice.

</details>


### [27] [Anatomy of a Closed-Loop Collapse: A Causal Case Study of a Compressed VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23048)
*Fengze Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对一个压缩操作策略通过离线评估却在闭环执行中崩溃的案例进行因果解剖：8层Octo-Base蒸馏保留86%参数并通过全部离线检查，但在WidowX拾放任务中0/72成功，教师为40/72。失败呈结构性，并可用动作轨迹中晚期加重的负z残差刻画；仅将一半训练流替换为部署分布教师rollout即可恢复与教师相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已确立压缩策略可能通过离线评估而在闭环失败；本文不重复该主张，而是剖析一个自然发生的具体案例，探究失败的结构、因果签名及标准补救措施为何无效，并检验能否通过数据分布干预修复。

Method: 对8层Octo-Base蒸馏策略进行系统评测：对比离线指标与闭环成功率；按任务阶段分解失败；用配对动作轨迹取证定位残差签名；在匹配控制下测试四种标准疗法（继续训练、域内离线数据、命令级补偿、命令通道钳制症状）；并做最小配对干预，将一半训练流替换为部署分布教师rollout，其余设置固定。

Result: 学生保留86%参数、离线教师比达0.996/1.000，但闭环0/72 vs 教师40/72。失败结构化：早期移动物体率为教师90%、抓取55%，而搬运到目标在全部训练变体中0%。签名是晚期加重的负z残差，约为修复后10倍，且跨基础蒸馏与两个续训分支持续。四种标准疗法均失败：继续训练和域内离线数据成功率仍为0（后者仅改善边际动作统计）；命令级补偿在任何偏移下均无恢复，尽管相同扰动会降低健康策略；命令通道钳制症状可保留抓取但成功率仍触底。最小配对干预恢复教师水平（18/36 vs 17/36留出），消除签名并恢复教师样扰动响应。

Conclusion: 本文主张存在性而非普遍性。操作上，离线门控（包括策略族自身验证指标）不足以作为压缩策略的验收测试；数十次闭环试验即可发现其遗漏的失败。

Abstract: Compressed manipulation policies can pass offline evaluation while failing in closed-loop execution; this dissociation is established in prior work and is not our claim. We contribute a causal anatomy of one naturally occurring case. An 8-layer distillation of Octo-Base retains 86% of parameters, passes every offline check we applied (0.996 and 1.000 teacher-ratios on the family's own validation metrics), and collapses in closed loop: 0/72 vs. the teacher's 40/72 on a simulated WidowX pick-and-place task. The collapse is structured, not diffuse: early task stages degrade gradually (the student moves the object at 90% of the teacher's rate and grasps at 55%), while transport-to-target fails categorically, at 0% in every training variant. Paired action-trace forensics isolate the signature: a negative, late-heavy $z$ residual, roughly 10x its post-repair magnitude, and persistent across the base distillation and both continuation branches. Four standard therapies fail under matched controls: continued training and in-domain offline data leave success at zero, even though the latter measurably improves marginal action statistics; command-level compensation recovers nothing at any offset, although the same perturbations degrade healthy policies; clamping the symptom in the command channel preserves grasping, yet success stays at floor. A minimal-pair intervention that substitutes half of the training stream with deployment-distribution teacher rollouts, with every other setting held fixed, restores parity with the teacher (18/36 vs. 17/36 held-out), eliminates that signature, and recovers a teacher-like perturbation-response profile. We claim existence, not universality. Operationally, offline gates, including a family's own validation metrics, are insufficient acceptance tests for compressed policies; a few dozen closed-loop trials sufficed to find what they missed.

</details>


### [28] [Fast and Robust Temporal Logic Planning via ADMM-based Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23037)
*Lukas Pries,Joris Verhagen,Jon Arrizabalaga,Jana Tumova,Markus Ryll,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向时序逻辑（TL）约束下安全连续时间运动规划的快速数值方法，将非凸安全与逻辑约束建模为凸集并集上的联合可行性图，并用ADMM解耦轨迹优化与离散约束处理，实现可靠收敛和平均4.7倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间运动规划需同时保证无碰撞安全性与时序/逻辑任务要求；此类非凸安全与逻辑约束使优化困难，现有方法在速度与可扩展性上存在不足。

Method: 将非凸安全与逻辑约束表示为凸集并集，并把相关离散决策编码到联合可行性图中；基于该图，将可行集上的欧氏投影与近端鲁棒性最大化分别转化为最短路径与最宽路径问题；进而提出基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的非凸分裂方法，解耦光滑时空轨迹优化与非光滑离散约束处理。

Result: 该算法在多个基准上表现出可靠收敛，并可扩展至大规模运动规划问题；在离散与连续时间逻辑问题上，相比当前最优方法取得平均4.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法为满足时序逻辑规范的安全连续时间运动规划提供了快速、可扩展的求解框架，能够生成无碰撞且鲁棒满足时序与逻辑任务要求的平滑连续轨迹。

Abstract: We present a fast numerical method for safe continuous-time motion planning under Temporal Logic (TL) specifications. The method generates smooth continuous trajectories that remain collision-free while robustly satisfying temporal and logical task requirements. A central component of our method is the formulation of nonconvex safety and logic constraints as unions of convex sets where associated discrete decisions are encoded in a joint feasibility graph. This graph representation allows Euclidean projection onto the feasible set and proximal robustness maximization to be reformulated as shortest- and widest-path problems, respectively. Building on this structure, we develop a nonconvex splitting method based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM), which decouples smooth spatio-temporal trajectory optimization from nonsmooth discrete constraint handling within the optimization. The resulting algorithm exhibits reliable convergence across benchmarks and scales to large-scale motion-planning problems, providing a 4.7x average speedup over the state of the art on discrete and continuous-time logic problems.

</details>


### [29] [Connectivity-Aware Exploration of Robotic Grasp Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22983)
*Maksim A Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究机器人抓取在 SE(3) 中可行抓取集合的连通结构，并提出连通性感知采样策略，以更高效地探索抓取空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抓取通常被建模为从候选抓取位姿空间中识别成功动作，但成功动作在该空间中的组织方式关注较少；作者希望探究可行抓取的多尺度结构能否用于提升探索效率。

Method: 使用大规模抓取数据集分析可行抓取集的连通性；提出 connectivity-aware sampling，增量探索当前观测到的抓取空间，优先采样组件间潜在桥接、结构前沿、边界扩展和几何新颖性；在受控重建实验中与随机采样和最远点采样比较，并评估隐藏抓取可行性下学习到的连通性对后续抓取发现的帮助，以及跨物体迁移结构经验的效果。

Result: 实验表明，成功抓取集在不同物体上呈现异质且可复现的连通结构；所提方法在恢复成功抓取集连通结构方面显著优于随机采样和最远点采样；连通性信息可改善后续抓取发现，且先前物体的结构经验可检索并迁移到未见物体。

Conclusion: 可行动作的空间组织包含超出单个候选动作可行性之外的有用信息；结构感知探索为利用机器人操作中可行动作空间的几何结构提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Robotic grasping is typically formulated as the problem of identifying successful actions from a space of candidate grasp poses. However, the organization of successful actions within this space has received less attention. We study the multiscale structure of viable robotic grasps in $SE(3)$ and investigate whether this structure can be exploited for more efficient exploration. Using a large-scale grasp dataset, we show that successful grasp sets exhibit heterogeneous and reproducible connectivity structure across objects. We then introduce a connectivity-aware sampling strategy that incrementally explores the currently observed grasp space by prioritizing potential bridges between components, structural frontiers, boundary extensions, and geometric novelty. In controlled reconstruction experiments, the method recovers the connectivity structure of successful grasp sets substantially more efficiently than random sampling and farthest-point sampling. We further evaluate whether connectivity acquired under hidden grasp viability can improve subsequent grasp discovery, and whether structural experience from previously explored objects can be retrieved and transferred to unseen objects. These results suggest that the spatial organization of viable actions provides information relevant to grasp-space exploration beyond the viability of individual candidate actions. More broadly, they motivate structure-aware exploration as a means of exploiting the geometry of viable action spaces in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [30] [A Horizon-slicing Approach to Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22974)
*Antony Thomas,Giulio Ferro,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Michela Robba,Marco Baglietto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究机器人运动规划中的最小障碍物位移规划问题：当初始无碰撞路径不存在时，通过移动可移动障碍物到达目标；证明多边形障碍物下问题为NP-hard，并给出精确解与可权衡路径长度和位移量的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人运动规划中，常需在无无碰撞路径时通过移动障碍物开辟路径，但障碍物位移会带来额外代价，因此需要研究最小化障碍物位移、并兼顾路径长度与计算复杂度的规划方法。

Method: 从机器人运动规划视角对最小障碍物位移规划进行建模，证明障碍物为平面多边形时问题NP-hard；提出最小障碍物位移问题的精确形式化及相应最优解，该形式化推广了文献中的其他问题；同时提出计算强度更低的近似解法，可在路径长度与障碍物位移量之间进行权衡。

Result: 证明了该问题在多边形障碍物模型下是NP-hard；给出了精确最优解；近似解与最优解的成本相差为最优成本的一个分数比例，且计算量更低，并能权衡路径长度与障碍物位移量。

Conclusion: 该问题具有较高计算复杂度；精确方法可求最优解，近似方法以较低计算代价获得有界偏差解，适用于需要灵活平衡路径长度与障碍物位移量的场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning problem from a robot motion planning perspective. The problem involves determining a feasible path to a goal location by displacing movable obstacles when no collision-free path initially exists. We show that this problem is computationally challenging and, in particular, NP-hard when obstacles are modeled as polygons in the plane. Besides an exact formulation of the minimum obstacle displacement problem generalizing other problems in the literature, and the associated optimal solution, this paper proposes an approximate solution that is less intensive from a computational standpoint, and differs from the optimal solution by a fraction of the optimal cost, being able to trade-off between path length and amount of obstacle displacements.

</details>


### [31] [An Empirical Study and Open Testbed for Federated Fine-Tuning of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22973)
*Zhekai Duan,Kevin Ziyang Xie,Xinyu Tan,Shikai Geng,Chengxu Zhou,Ramana Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的联邦微调，在LIBERO仿真与真实机器人任务上比较多种联邦参数范围、聚合算法和分布偏移下的表现，并总结关键经验与开放机会，同时开源测试平台DECENTVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练VLA适配到新机器人、环境或任务需要本地采集的演示数据，而这些数据常被丢弃。联邦学习有望利用分布式演示学习共享策略，但其能否适配大型预训练VLA仍属开放问题；同时，预训练VLA缺乏可复现基准和可复用训练框架，导致现有结果难以比较。

Method: 作者对三种现代预训练VLA策略进行系统性联邦微调研究：在LIBERO操作基准的40个仿真任务上，以及在两个真实机器人实验中的六个真实任务上评估，演示数据分别来自两个和三个站点。研究分析了该设定中的关键选择，涵盖多种联邦参数范围、三种聚合算法，以及分布偏移下的评估。

Result: 研究得出若干经验：联邦参数范围的选择比聚合算法选择更主导性能；在真实机器人上难以匹配集中式微调，因为跨站点异构性强于仿真所能体现。也发现机会：在异构数据上可匹配集中式微调；在分布偏移下至少与集中式微调一样鲁棒；可通过每个客户端只联邦部分策略、其余本地保留来实现个性化，在预训练较弱时有益，但不会留下可用的全局模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为联邦VLA学习提供了系统实证结论，并开源了模型与运行时无关的测试平台DECENTVLA，以促进未来研究和公平比较。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model to a new robot, environment, or task requires demonstrations that are collected locally and often discarded. Federated learning is a promising approach to exploiting such distributed demonstrations by learning a shared policy. However, whether it can adapt large pretrained VLAs remains an open question, and a lack of reproducible benchmarks for pretrained VLAs and reusable training frameworks makes existing results difficult to compare. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of federated fine-tuning of three modern pretrained VLA policies on the 40 simulated tasks of the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, and on six real-world tasks in two real-robot experiments, with demonstrations collected across two and three sites, respectively. Our study analyzes the key choices in this setting, spanning multiple federated parameter scopes, three aggregation algorithms, and evaluation under distribution shift. Based on the study, we derive a series of lessons, including the dominance of the federated scope over the choice of aggregation algorithm and the difficulty of matching centralized fine-tuning on physical robots, where cross-site heterogeneity is stronger than simulation captures. We also highlight opportunities for federated VLA learning, such as the ability to match centralized fine-tuning on heterogeneous data, to remain at least as robust as centralized fine-tuning under distribution shift, and to personalize, with each client federating part of the policy and keeping the rest local, which helps where the policy's pretraining is weak but leaves no usable global model. We open-source \decentvla{}, the model- and runtime-agnostic testbed behind the study, to facilitate future research and fair comparisons in federated VLA learning.

</details>


### [32] [Transferring the Intelligence of VLMs to Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22966)
*Meng-Hao Guo,Zhe-Han Mo,Jia-Jun Wang,Yi Zhang,Kejin Wang,Yi-Xuan Deng,Jia-Peng Zhang,Yongming Rao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 RoboDawn，将机器人控制暴露给智能体式 VLM，通过离散平移、旋转和夹爪命令构成的人机直观接口，让 VLM 闭环观察、推理、执行并适应；结合少量演示的上下文学习，无需任务特定机器人训练，在仿真与真实机器人上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可无缝适应物理与数字世界，尽管存在数字到现实的具身、环境和任务差距，人类智能本身似乎可以迁移。由此提出核心问题：视觉语言模型（VLM）的智能是否也能从数字世界泛化到物理世界，用于机器人控制？

Method: 提出 RoboDawn：一种人类直观接口，将机器人控制抽象为一组紧凑的离散平移、旋转和夹爪命令，并交由智能体式 VLM 控制。VLM 在闭环中工作：观察当前视觉状态，推理下一动作，执行动作，并根据结果状态调整后续决策。同时引入上下文学习（ICL）方案，用少量演示将 VLM 对齐到接口使用和任务求解策略。方法无需任务特定的机器人训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 C2R 和 RoboDojo 上评估：零样本 RoboDawn 优于多个在基准特定机器人数据上训练的强策略；加入一次上下文演示后性能显著提升并达到 SOTA。RoboTwin 2.0 C2R 成功率从零样本 53.2% 提升到单样本 73.6%，超过强基线 π0.5 的 46.0%。RoboDojo 成功率从零样本 35.67% 提升到单样本 47.17%。同一框架还迁移到真实机器人 Franka，完成 block-in-basket 和 block stacking 任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，VLM 的智能可从数字世界迁移到物理世界用于机器人控制；通过简洁离散接口和少量上下文演示，RoboDawn 无需任务特定机器人训练即可实现强泛化与 SOTA 性能，并能部署到真实机器人任务。

Abstract: Humans can seamlessly adapt to both physical and digital worlds, suggesting that while a digital-to-real gap exists in embodiment, environment and task, human intelligence itself may transfer across this gap. This naturally raises a fundamental question: can the intelligence of vision-language models (VLMs) similarly generalize from the digital world to the physical world for robotic control? We investigate this question through RoboDawn, a human-intuitive interface that exposes robotic control to an agentic VLM through a compact set of discrete translation, rotation, and gripper commands. Using this interface, the VLM controls a robot in a closed loop: it observes the current visual state, reasons about the next action, executes it, and adapts subsequent decisions to the resulting state. Furthermore, we introduce an in-context learning (ICL) scheme that uses a few demonstrations to ground the VLM in both interface usage and task-solving strategies. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 C2R and RoboDojo demonstrate that RoboDawn achieves strong performance without task-specific robot training. In the zero-shot setting, RoboDawn outperforms several strong policies trained on benchmarkspecific robot data, while a single in-context demonstration further yields substantial performance gains and establishes state-of-the-art (SOTA) results. On RoboTwin 2.0 C2R, the success rate increases from 53.2% zero-shot to 73.6% one-shot, exceeding the solid baseline {\pi}0.5 (46.0%). Similar gains are observed on RoboDojo, where success rate improves from 35.67% zero-shot to 47.17% one-shot. The same framework also transfers to real-world robots, performing block-in-basket and block stacking on Franka.

</details>


### [33] [Prescribed-Time Contracting-Boundary Control of a Tendon-Driven Flexible Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22963)
*Yi Lu,Chao Tang,Zhiji Han,Hongdu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预设时间性能塑造控制方法，用于三对拮抗腱驱动的单段柔性臂曲率跟踪。方法引入笛卡尔曲率表示和六腱显式运动学映射，设计三次性能边界与双重误差变换，保证边界不变、预设时间进入终端精度区并渐近收敛。仿真和降阶实验验证了有效性，终端曲率RMSE较匹配基线降低32.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单段柔性臂在直线构型下弯曲方向未定义，且腱驱动曲率跟踪需要同时处理运动学映射、误差约束和预设时间性能保证。现有方法难以在避免奇异性同时确保规定时间内进入终端精度区域。

Method: 采用笛卡尔曲率表示避免直线构型下弯曲方向未定义，并建立三对拮抗腱到六腱的显式运动学映射。设计三次性能边界，使其在预设时间内从初始容许误差界平滑收缩至非零终端精度界。基于该边界，构造静态对称误差缩放与时间变化行为塑造相结合的双重变换，将跟踪误差映射到固定单位盒，进而设计控制器以保证边界不变性、预设时间进入终端精度区域和后续渐近收敛。验证包括Python与OpenCR-MuJoCo数值评估，以及两段四通道平台上的监督降阶实验。

Result: 六次实验试验中未观察到违反规定性能边界的情况。与匹配基线相比，所提控制器将平均终端曲率RMSE降低32.5%，且进入终端精度带的时间与基线相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法在降阶实验设置中具有可行性，能够为腱驱动柔性臂曲率跟踪提供预设时间性能保证控制方案；但其完整适用性仍需在更高自由度或实际系统中进一步验证。

Abstract: This study develops a prescribed-time performance-shaping control method for curvature tracking of a single-segment flexible arm actuated by three antagonistic tendon pairs. A Cartesian curvature representation is introduced to avoid the undefined bending direction at the straight configuration and to establish an explicit six-tendon kinematic mapping. A cubic performance boundary contracts smoothly from an initially admissible error bound to a nonzero terminal accuracy bound within a prescribed time. Based on this boundary, a dual transformation combining static symmetric error scaling and time-varying behavior shaping maps the tracking error into a fixed unit box. The resulting controller guarantees boundary invariance, prescribed-time entry into the terminal accuracy region, and subsequent asymptotic convergence. Numerical evaluations with Python and OpenCR--MuJoCo, together with a supervised reduced-order experiment on a two-section, four-channel platform, provide complementary validation. Across six experimental trials, no violation of the prescribed boundary is observed, and the proposed controller reduces the mean terminal curvature RMSE by 32.5% relative to a matched baseline, with comparable terminal-band entry times. These results support the feasibility of the proposed approach in the reduced-order experimental setting.

</details>


### [34] [MIRA-PRM: Mission-Informed Reusable Roadmap Planning for Mobile Gas-Sensing Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22954)
*Gongsen Wang,Siyuan Wang,Xinyuan Wang,Yuhan Wen,Feng Yang,Zhen Tian,Hoi Leong Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向移动气体传感巡检的 MIRA-PRM，通过任务信息引导采样、局部自适应连接与证据门控细化，在有序多目标和有限节点预算下显著提升规划可靠性，但以较慢速度和略长路径换取高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动气体传感巡检要求移动平台可靠到达有序采样位姿，传统 PRM/PRM* 等概率路线图方法在任务约束、有限节点预算和重复查询场景下成功率与局部修复效率不足。

Method: 提出 MIRA-PRM，将几何采样、任务流采样与巡检条件采样结合，并采用局部自适应连接和证据门控细化。通过八个开发 campaign，共 142,448 次规划运行，评估任务扩展、重复查询适应、有限节点预算、目标分布、局部地图修复、消融、跨家族比较和参数敏感性；另在冻结 holdout 上验证。

Result: 有序目标从 2 增至 8 时，MIRA-PRM 保持 100% 种子级成功率；40 节点上限下达 97.67%，高于 PRM 的 67.17% 和 PRM* 的 86.50%。局部修复较重建降低事件耗时 79.9-81.9%、节点新增量 90.8-94.3%。冻结 holdout 含 12 张地图、48 个有序任务、每地图-任务单元 3 个种子：MIRA-PRM 为 96/144 验证成功，PRM 为 82/144，PRM* 为 89/144，LE-HG-PRM 为 70/144。在配对共同成功运行中，MIRA-PRM 实际墙钟时间较 PRM* 减少 16.89%、较 LE-HG-PRM 减少 81.01%，但较 PRM 慢 52.23%；路径长 0.54-1.86%。所有验证路径通过独立全段检查和净空非劣性检查。

Conclusion: MIRA-PRM 在结构化仿真中支持以可靠性为导向的权衡，显著提升有序巡检任务成功率和局部修复效率，但牺牲了相对 PRM 的速度且路径略长；后续需开展外部地图和物理移动传感验证。

Abstract: Mobile gas-sensing inspection requires a mobile platform to reliably reach ordered sampling poses. We present MIRA-PRM, a mission-informed probabilistic roadmap that combines geometry-, mission-flow-, and inspection-conditioned sampling with locally adaptive connectivity and evidence-gated refinement. Eight development campaigns comprising 142,448 planner runs evaluated mission scaling, repeated-query adaptation, finite node budgets, target distributions, local map repair, ablation, cross-family comparison, and parameter sensitivity. MIRA-PRM maintained 100% seed-level success as ordered targets increased from two to eight, and achieved 97.67% seed-level success under a 40-node ceiling, compared with 67.17% for PRM and 86.50% for PRM*. Local repair reduced event time by 79.9-81.9% and node additions by 90.8-94.3% relative to rebuilding. On a separate frozen holdout of 12 maps, 48 ordered missions, and three seeds per map-mission unit, MIRA-PRM produced 96/144 validated successes, versus 82/144 for PRM, 89/144 for PRM*, and 70/144 for LE-HG-PRM. On paired common-success runs, MIRA-PRM reduced wall time by 16.89% relative to PRM* and 81.01% relative to LE-HG-PRM, but was 52.23% slower than PRM; paths were 0.54-1.86% longer. All validated paths passed independent full-segment checks and clearance non-inferiority. The results support a reliability-oriented trade-off in structured simulations and motivate external-map and physical mobile-sensing validation.

</details>


### [35] [Embodied Snap: Octopus-Inspired Distributed Reach-and-Attach with a Speed-Limited Soft Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22926)
*Linxin Hou,Zhihang Qin,Heyang Zou,Qirui Wu,Peiyi Wang,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种具身弹射控制器（embodied snap controller），将慢速伺服预加载与快速弹性释放分离，使软体机械臂突破腱驱速度限制，实现动态 reach-and-attach。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机械臂配合被动吸盘进行 reach-and-attach 时需要精确瞄准并达到足够接触速度，但齿轮/腱驱执行器存在速度上限，仅靠改进轨迹跟踪无法克服。

Method: 提出具身弹射控制器，分阶段完成慢速伺服预加载与快速弹性释放；以章鱼生物学启发分段式组织先验，而非复现章鱼神经系统。学习到的策略在三个分段间共享，选择预加载、瞄准、腱松弛和释放时机，决定在哪里、如何以及何时加载与释放身体。策略在硬件验证的循环模型上离线优化，并受实验支持边界约束。

Result: 在 5 个优化种子和 400 个未见仿真目标中，5 cm 横向容差下附着成功率为 73±4%；共享策略在共同评估预算的中位 17% 时达到匹配的集中式控制器平均最终奖励。硬件表征达到 1.56-1.64 m/s 尖端速度，比直接腱驱释放至少高 108%。在 6 个放置位置的 18 次开环硬件试验中，17 次超过 1 m/s 弹射阈值，9 次取回物体，5 个放置位置成功取回。

Conclusion: 结果展示了控制问题中的实际职责分工：学习控制负责准备身体，被动身体力学负责执行动态 reach-and-attach 所需的快速运动。

Abstract: Reach-and-attach of soft robotic arms with passive suction requires accurate targeting and sufficient contact speed, yet geared actuators can impose a speed limit that improved trajectory tracking alone cannot overcome. This paper proposes an embodied snap controller that separates slow servo-driven preloading from rapid elastic release, enabling a compliant arm to move beyond its direct tendon-driven speed limit. Octopus biology motivates the controller's section-wise organizational prior, rather than reproduction of the octopus nervous system. A learned policy shared across three sections selects preloads, aim, tendon slack, and release timing, determining where, how, and when to load and release the body. The policy is optimized offline using a hardware-validated recurrent model within experimentally supported bounds. Across five optimization seeds and 400 unseen simulated targets, attachment success is $(73\pm4)\%$ at a $5\text{ cm}$ lateral tolerance, and the shared policy reaches the matched centralized controller's mean final reward after a median $17\%$ of the common evaluation budget. Hardware characterization achieves tip speeds of 1.56-1.64 m/s, at least $108\%$ above direct tendon-driven release. In 18 open-loop hardware trials across six placements, 17 exceed the 1 m/s snap threshold and nine retrieve the object, with successful retrieval at five placements. These results demonstrate a practical division of responsibility in the control problem: learned control prepares the body, and passive body mechanics execute the rapid movement needed for dynamic reach-and-attach.

</details>


### [36] [Learning Beyond What Humans Can Demonstrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24996)
*Yuchen Song,Aditya Mittal,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出GLIDE框架，面向“不可行演示”场景：当动态稳定性、接触时序或灵巧协调导致人类难以直接采集专家演示时，GLIDE推断任务特定失败模式，并将其转化为可执行护栏，用于数据采集与策略部署，从而提升策略学习可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作的行为克隆依赖专家演示，但一些任务要求动态稳定、精确接触时序或灵巧协调，人类操作者难以甚至无法采集有效数据。因此需要研究不可行演示条件下的策略学习问题。

Method: GLIDE（Guardrails for Learning from Infeasible Demonstrations Efficiently）根据任务描述和条件遥操作代码，自动生成护栏：利用系统状态过滤遥操作与策略指令、约束易失败动作，并基于轨迹反馈迭代改进。该方法旨在发现超越领域专家硬编码护栏的涌现式护栏，以改进数据采集和策略执行。

Result: 在三个任务中，GLIDE发现的涌现护栏优于朴素VR遥操作和领域专家硬编码护栏。经过细化后，数据采集成功率从0-10%提升至70-90%。策略执行阶段，混合数据训练并带护栏的策略在Tomato plate transfer、Marker handover and stand、Wine serving任务上分别达到70%、60%、60%的成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，GLIDE能够在直接演示不可行时支持策略学习，为不可行演示条件下的机器人操作数据采集与策略部署提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for robot manipulation relies on expert demonstrations. However, for tasks that require dynamic stability, precise contact timing, or dexterous coordination, human operators may find it hard or even impossible to collect data. We study this infeasible-demonstration regime and propose GLIDE: Guardrails for Learning from Infeasible Demonstrations Efficiently, a framework that infers task-specific failure modes and converts them into executable guardrails for data collection and policy deployment. Given a task description and the conditioning teleoperation code, GLIDE writes guardrails that use system states to filter teleoperation and policy commands, constrain failure-prone actions, and iteratively improve from trajectory feedback. Across three tasks, GLIDE discovers emergent guardrails that go beyond domain-expert hardcoded ones, improving data collection over naive VR teleoperation and domain-expert hardcoded guardrails. After refinement, GLIDE raises data-collection success from 0-10 percent to 70-90 percent across the three tasks. During policy execution, mixed-data guarded policies reach 70 percent, 60 percent, and 60 percent success on Tomato plate transfer, Marker handover and stand, and Wine serving tasks. These results show that GLIDE can support policy learning when direct demonstrations are infeasible. Project website: this http URL

</details>


### [37] [MIGU: Multimodal Instruction Grounding under Uncertainty for Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24995)
*Mingke Lu,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIGU：一个模块化多模态指令接地框架，融合语言与手势中的语义和几何证据及不确定性，将接地信念连接至机器人操作规划，并在真实世界基准上超越所有对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心环境中部署机器人，需要理解自然的人类指令。语言和手势提供互补但不确定的线索，因此必须显式建模多模态不确定性以提升指令接地与后续规划。

Method: MIGU通过眼-指几何传播视线方向和深度不确定性，并考虑手部方向估计误差，构建3D几何似然；利用视觉语言模型（VLM）提供候选物体和区域的语义先验；再经贝叶斯式融合形成统一接地信念。该信念支持行为规划，可直接进入下游规划或请求澄清。接地目标用于定义移动操作和桌面任务与运动规划（TAMP）的目标。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，MIGU优于所有评估基线；消融实验支持显式多模态不确定性建模带来的收益。项目网站：this http URL。

Conclusion: MIGU通过显式建模语言与手势的多模态不确定性，有效统一语义与几何证据，提升指令接地与机器人操作规划性能，并支持主动澄清机制。

Abstract: Understanding natural human instructions is crucial for deploying robots in human-centric environments. We study multimodal instruction grounding, where language and gesture provide complementary but uncertain cues. We present MIGU, a modular framework that combines semantic and geometric evidence into a unified grounding belief and connects it to manipulation planning. MIGU constructs a 3D geometric likelihood by propagating viewing-direction and depth uncertainty through eye-finger geometry while accounting for hand-direction estimation error. A vision-language model (VLM) provides semantic priors over candidate objects and regions, which are combined with the geometric likelihood through Bayes-inspired fusion. The resulting belief supports behavior planning to either proceed directly to downstream planning or request clarification. Grounded targets then define goals for mobile manipulation and tabletop task-and-motion planning. On a real-world benchmark, MIGU outperforms all evaluated baselines, while ablations support the benefit of explicit multimodal uncertainty modeling. Project website: this http URL

</details>


### [38] [DexTacWAM: A Visuo-Tactile World-Action Model for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24976)
*Haoran Yuan,Zekai Wang,Boning Shao,Haoran Lu,Trevor Darrell,Ismini Lourentzou,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexTacWAM 是一种视触觉世界-动作模型，将逐指尖触觉特征经手指与姿态感知压缩器注入视频扩散世界模型，实现视触觉联合建模，在六项接触丰富双手灵巧操作任务上平均得分70.6，远超最强基线38.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖接触动力学，而视觉只能部分观测；现有 World-Action Models 大多以视觉为中心，无法直接建模接触动力学。

Method: 独立编码每个指尖触觉，使用手指与姿态感知的触觉压缩器聚合特征，并将触觉隐变量注入视频扩散世界模型，进行视触觉联合世界建模。

Result: 在22自由度双臂平台的六项接触丰富任务中，每项均取得最高分，平均70.6对最强基线38.0；消融显示，将接触演化作为预测世界状态的一部分而非仅做触觉条件，四任务均值从74.7降至26.6（移除触觉世界建模）；用冻结预训练视觉VAE、约每任务100条演示、4小时触觉编码器适配，无需触觉中训即可将预训练视频模型扩展到触觉，视觉预测质量保持在纯视觉对应方法0.5 dB以内；压缩器保留89.4%融合前接触召回率，同时训练快2.26倍、推理快1.29倍。

Conclusion: 预训练视频先验可以数据与计算高效地扩展到分布式多指接触动力学建模。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact dynamics that are often only partially observable from vision. Recent World-Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive video world modeling with action generation, but remain largely vision-centric and therefore cannot directly model these contact dynamics. We present DexTacWAM, a visuo-tactile WAM that encodes each fingertip independently, aggregates the resulting features through a finger- and pose-aware tactile compressor, and injects the tactile latent into a video diffusion world model for joint visuo-tactile world modeling. Across six contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks on a 22-DoF bimanual platform, DexTacWAM achieves the highest score on every task, averaging 70.6 versus 38.0 for the strongest baseline. Ablations attribute the gain to modeling contact evolution as part of the predicted world state rather than tactile conditioning alone: removing tactile world modeling reduces the four-task mean from 74.7 to 26.6 while keeping the same tactile features and action expert. After four hours of tactile-encoder adaptation with a frozen pretrained vision VAE, our continual vision-to-touch learning extends the pretrained video model to touch using roughly 100 demonstrations per task without tactile midtraining, while retaining visual prediction quality within 0.5 dB of vision-only counterparts. The compressor retains 89.4% of pre-fusion contact recall while enabling 2.26x faster training and 1.29x faster inference. Together, these results show that pretrained video priors can be extended to distributed multi-finger contact dynamics in a data- and compute-efficient manner.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星自主导航要求车辆在多样且视觉挑战环境中区分可通行地形，但面向地外环境的基于学习导航受限于缺乏大规模数据集。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模火星导航数据集和不确定性感知可通行性估计方法，推动了地外环境基于学习的自主导航，并展示了向实际路径规划系统部署的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation on Mars requires vehicles to distinguish between traversable terrains across diverse and visually challenging environments. However, progress in learning-based navigation for off-world environments has been limited by the lack of large-scale datasets. Since landing in Jezero Crater, the Mars 2020 Perseverance rover has traversed terrain ranging from sandy dunes, rocky patches, and flat bedrocks. As a result, this paper presents a dataset spanning 500 sols and 45km of trajectories driven by both human operators and the onboard planner, ENav. Our dataset contains grayscale stereo image pairs, poses, accelerometer readings, rocker-bogie angles, and estimates of tilt and wheel slip. Building on this dataset, we introduce an uncertainty-aware traversability-estimation framework that learns terrain representations from multimodal driving experience. We compare our proposed method against existing approaches on the Mars 2020 dataset and show that our method achieves an AUROC of 0.874 and an F1 score of 0.758, outperforming the strongest baseline by 0.058 and 0.156, respectively, while also achieving the highest average precision and recall. Finally, we show that the visual representations can be integrated into path planners, such as ENav, on a physical rover test bed. Videos, code, and the M2020 dataset will be available at this https URL .

</details>

*Abhinav Jain,Cindy Grimm,Stefan Lee*

TL;DR: 提出端到端流水线，用仿真与合成数据训练闭环视觉运动控制器，实现平面果园树形（V-Trellis苹果、UFO樱桃）机器人修剪，并在真实果园零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 休眠期修剪劳动密集但对现代高产果园至关重要；现有机器人修剪在杂乱枝条环境中定位剪点与工具姿态困难，且依赖真实数据或完整3D重建。

Method: 针对平面化树形系统，合成生成平面果园树网格，构建物理果园仿真器，经运动规划自动采集成功修剪轨迹，再用结合离线示范与在线仿真rollout的混合强化学习训练策略；控制器以腕部相机光流为输入，无需完整3D重建，闭环引导切割器穿过杂乱枝条到达指定剪点并保持正确工具朝向。

Result: 在3000个剪点的穷举仿真任务空间评估中，V-Trellis苹果成功率49.9%，UFO樱桃46.0%；在38次物理试验（28次商业/实验果园户外田间试验和10次室内实验室测试）中验证了零样本sim-to-real迁移；实验室硬件上优于经典RRT-Connect基线。

Conclusion: 表明完全由仿真和合成数据训练的闭环视觉运动策略可零样本部署于真实平面果园修剪，并优于经典运动规划基线。

Abstract: Dormant tree pruning is labor-intensive yet essential for maintaining modern high-productivity fruit orchards. In this work, we focus on pruning of modern planar tree training systems - V-Trellis apples and UFO cherries - where trunks and primary branches are trained into approximately planar walls. We introduce an end-to-end pipeline to learn a closed-loop visuomotor controller for robotic pruning. This controller is trained entirely using simulation and synthetically generated data and deployed in real orchards in a zero-shot manner. The pipeline comprises synthetic generation of planar orchard tree meshes, construction of a physics-based orchard simulator, automated collection of successful pruning trajectories via motion planning, and policy learning with a novel hybrid reinforcement-learning algorithm that combines offline demonstrations with online simulated rollouts. The controller uses optical-flow inputs from a wrist-mounted camera - avoiding the need for full 3D-reconstruction - and continuously guides the cutter through cluttered branch environments to a specified cutpoint with correct tool orientation. In exhaustive simulated task-space evaluations over 3,000 pruning points, the policy attains 49.9% success on V-Trellis apples and 46.0% on UFO cherries. We validate the learned controller across 38 physical trials - comprising 28 outdoor field trials in commercial and experimental orchards and 10 indoor laboratory tests - demonstrating zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. The learned policy also outperforms a classical RRT-Connect baseline on physical hardware in laboratory trials.

</details>


### [41] [Steerable and Reactive Grasping Through Modular Design with a Three-Point Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24896)
*Andrew Nguyen,Yonghyeon Lee,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将全局几何推理与局部接触控制解耦的模块化灵巧抓取框架：先从抓取可供性热力图选择三点接触，再由模型控制器导引，最后用仅依赖本体感知的RL策略稳定抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需同时解决抓哪里、如何到达、如何保持稳定接触；端到端方法难以泛化且对到达与感知误差敏感，因此需要模块化地结合几何先验与局部反馈控制。

Method: 给定物体几何和可选语言指令，框架从预计算抓取可供性热力图中采样三点接触；模型驱动反应式控制器跟踪物体、避碰并引导手到达接触点；在最后几厘米，RL策略仅观察手指关节状态和近期动作，不使用目标点、视觉观测或物体几何，以精调并稳定抓取。

Result: 仿真中与挤压及端到端基线比较抓取-提升成功率，分析到达收敛性并展示抓取引导；硬件在两个训练物体和一个未见物体上验证了完整流程。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架用几何引导到达、用局部反馈确保抓取，单一RL策略可跨物体和抓取构型共享，实现鲁棒的灵巧抓取。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping requires deciding where to grasp, reaching the target, and maintaining stable contact. We connect these stages through a compact three-point interface that separates global geometric reasoning from local contact control. Given object geometry and optional language commands, our framework samples contact triples from a precomputed grasp-affordance heatmap. A model-based reactive controller tracks the object, avoids collisions, and guides the hand toward the selected contacts. In the final centimeters, a Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy uses proprioceptive feedback to refine and stabilize the grasp despite reaching and perception errors. It observes only finger joint states and its recent actions, with no target points, visual observations, or object geometry, so a single policy is shared across objects and grasp configurations. In simulation, we compare grasp-and-lift success against squeeze and end-to-end baselines, characterize reaching convergence, and demonstrate grasp steering; hardware demonstrations on two training objects and one unseen object illustrate the full pipeline. Our modular framework uses geometry to guide the reach and local feedback to secure the grasp.

</details>


### [42] [DualWAM: Dual-System World Action Models for Asynchronous Global Planning and Local Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24868)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yuntian Deng,Kai Liu,Yizhou Zhou,Yizhou Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao,He Wang,Zhizheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双系统世界动作模型（WAM），通过解耦全局规划与局部精化，在保持长时域世界-动作生成的同时实现高频闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型联合生成机器人动作并预测未来世界状态，可将视频预训练先验迁移到机器人控制，但未来视觉预测计算昂贵；现有方法常依赖长动作块摊薄推理成本，牺牲了闭环响应性。

Method: 所提方法采用双系统异步共享去噪轨迹：全局系统周期性地对较大世界-动作块进行高噪声双向去噪以建立全局规划；仅用腕部观测的局部系统从中间去噪状态提取时间对齐的短窗口，并结合最新腕部观测完成低噪声精化，提供局部几何、运动和接触等动作对齐线索。每个全局规划可复用多次局部更新，局部系统持续引入新鲜交互反馈。

Result: 在Franka和Galbot的零样本操作任务中，平均成功率比最强基线提升4.5个百分点，关键路径加速16.6倍；角色匹配的egocentric与UMI数据使成功率提升14个百分点；该解耦设计还支持边缘-云部署，通信开销显著低于基线。

Conclusion: 通过解耦全局规划与局部精化，该方法缓解了长时域世界-动作生成与高频闭环控制之间的权衡，提升了操作成功率和推理效率，并利于边缘-云部署。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate robot actions and predict future world states, transferring priors from video pretraining to robot control. However, future visual prediction is computationally expensive, so existing WAMs often rely on long action chunks to amortize inference cost across control steps, at the cost of closed-loop responsiveness. We present \method, a dual-system WAM that preserves broader-horizon world-action generation while enabling high-frequency closed-loop action updates by decoupling global planning and local refinement. \systwo periodically performs high-noise bidirectional denoising over a broader world-action chunk to establish a global plan, while wrist-only \sysone extracts a temporally aligned short window from the intermediate denoising state and completes low-noise refinement using the latest wrist observations, which provide action-aligned cues about local geometry, motion, and contact during interaction. The two systems operate asynchronously along a shared denoising trajectory: each global plan is reused across multiple local updates, while \sysone repeatedly incorporates fresh interaction feedback. Across zero-shot manipulation tasks on Franka and Galbot, \method improves success over the strongest evaluated baseline by 4.5 percentage points on average, while achieving a 16.6$\times$ critical-path speedup. Further studies show that role-matched egocentric and UMI data improve success by 14 percentage points, and that the decoupled design naturally supports edge--cloud deployment with substantially lower communication overhead than the baseline.

</details>


### [43] [SE(3) Neural Potential Fields for 6-DoF Trajectory Planning Directly from Images Without Explicit 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24864)
*Jeffrey Eiyike,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种SE(3)神经势场，直接从带位姿RGB图像学习6-DoF抓取的无碰撞轨迹，并用导航函数（自由空间中到抓取的测地距离）监督，消除传统人工势场的梯度抵消、贴障和停滞问题；在UR10上实现高避障裕度与快速部署规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱场景中的6-DoF抓取需要无碰撞轨迹。传统方法先重建3D场景再规划，受重建精度与计算开销限制；直接从图像学习势场虽避免重建，却继承经典人工势场弱点：吸引与排斥梯度抵消时，下降路径会擦过障碍而非绕行，并可能在到达目标前停滞。

Method: 学习SE(3)神经势场，输入为带位姿RGB图像；训练时以导航函数监督，即从同一批图像恢复自由空间中到抓取位姿的测地距离。该监督信号使势场同时避免局部极小和贴障下降，并可直接用于生成机械臂轨迹。

Result: 在两个桌面场景中，UR10从被障碍阻挡的起点执行：所有起点均收敛到抓取3 cm以内，且所有执行路径相对真值几何均无碰撞，而仅图像监督下无碰撞率为25%和0%；平均间隙从不足1 cm提升到8.6-8.8 cm，臂杆接触从执行构型的20.6-50.4%降至2.7-5.5%。两个场景的执行抓取成功率为90.0%和40.0%，剩余失败源于笛卡尔执行器拒绝而非势场。规划约2 s，而基于同图像重建的RRT*需67-133 s；在统一离线测试框架下两者可比，部署时的差距主要来自稠密重建的碰撞检测成本，而非规划器复杂度。

Conclusion: 用导航函数/测地距离监督从图像学习SE(3)神经势场，可有效生成无碰撞6-DoF抓取轨迹，避免显式重建，显著提高避障安全性与部署效率；其部署优势来自免除稠密重建碰撞检测，而非规划算法本身。

Abstract: Reaching a 6-DoF grasp pose in clutter requires a collision-free trajectory, conventionally obtained by reconstructing the scene in 3D and planning inside that reconstruction, at the cost of its accuracy and compute. Potential fields learned directly from images remove that dependency but inherit the classical weakness of artificial potential fields: where attractive and repulsive gradients cancel, the descent grazes the obstacle instead of going around it, and can stall short of the goal. We present an SE(3) neural potential field learned from posed RGB images and supervised with a navigation function, the geodesic distance to the grasp through free space recovered from those same images during training, which removes both failures. On two tabletop scenes, from obstacle-blocked starts executed on a UR10, the field converges within 3 cm of the grasp from every start and every path it executes is collision-free against the ground-truth geometry, against 25% and 0% under image supervision alone; mean clearance rises from under a centimeter to 8.6-8.8 cm and arm-link contacts fall from 20.6-50.4% to 2.7-5.5% of executed configurations. Executed grasp success is 90.0% and 40.0% on the two scenes, the residual failures being refusals of the Cartesian executor rather than of the field. Planning takes about 2 s against 67-133 s for RRT* on a reconstruction of the same images, though under a common offline harness the two are comparable: the deployed margin is the cost of collision-checking a dense reconstruction, not planner complexity.

</details>


### [44] [Range-Aided SLAM Initialization Exploiting Accurate Heading Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24846)
*Isabel Lougheed,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于距离辅助同步定位与建图（RA-SLAM）的新型初始化方法，利用高精度航向信息，通过两步法求解应答器与AUV位置，为非线性RA-SLAM提供可靠初值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用SLAM问题在已知机器人航向时具有可分离结构，可线性求解路标与机器人位置；而AUV导航中通常可获得高精度航向信息，因此需要一种利用该信息进行RA-SLAM初始化的方法。

Method: 该方法分为两步：首先，在给定非线性距离测量的条件下，利用广义信赖域子问题（GTRS）求解应答器相对于AUV的位置；其次，结合应答器相对位置与AUV已知航向，通过线性最小二乘估计应答器和AUV的位置。

Result: 所得到的应答器与AUV位置估计，结合高精度航向信息，可为一般非线性RA-SLAM问题提供可靠初始化；并在真实AUV数据集上进行了验证，该数据集包含长基线（LBL）距离测量和惯性导航系统（INS）提供的高精度航向信息。

Conclusion: 实验表明，该初始化方法能够有效利用高精度航向与距离测量，为RA-SLAM提供可靠的初始估计，适用于AUV导航场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel initialization method for range-aided simultaneous localization and mapping (RA-SLAM). The general SLAM problem has a well-known separable structure where landmark and robot positions can be solved for in a linear fashion given known robot headings. This paper considers the case where highly accurate heading information is available, which is typical of autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) navigation, to solve for the range transponder and robot positions. The proposed approach consists of two steps. First, using a generalized trust region subproblem (GTRS), the positions of the transponders relative to the AUV are solved for given the nonlinear range measurements. Second, the relative transponder positions and the known heading of the AUV are used to estimate the transponder and AUV positions by solving a linear least-squares problem. These transponder and AUV position estimates, combined with the highly accurate heading information, provide a reliable initialization method for the general nonlinear RA-SLAM problem. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is tested on a real-world AUV dataset where long baseline (LBL) range measurements are provided in concert with highly accurate heading information provided by an inertial navigation system (INS).

</details>


### [45] [CAST: Collision-Aware Assembly with Construction Robots using Simultaneous Trajectory Estimation and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24841)
*Karthik Shaji,Chisung Kim,John D'Amato,Edvard Bruun,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单因子图的多机器人轨迹估计与规划框架，融合实测状态、显式碰撞与学习到的缆绳约束，实现建筑场景中的同步高维运动规划并完成木结构搭建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在建筑中可高精度作业并减少人员暴露于危险，但环境配置空间高维、碰撞约束复杂（其他机器人、装配对象、工作空间边界），且重型机器人存在刚度与振动诱导的不确定性，工作空间也会变化。

Method: 使用单一因子图统一轨迹估计与规划，纳入机器人实测状态、显式碰撞约束和学习到的缆绳约束；支持变化工作空间与同步高维机器人运动规划，并考虑重型机器人系统的振动不确定性。

Result: 在柱-过梁（post-and-lintel）木结构搭建中验证：一台机械臂作为木材抓取器，另一台作为打钉紧固器，框架成功完成该任务。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效处理高维、多约束、动态建筑环境中的多机器人协同规划与执行，适用于实际建筑装配任务。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems have shown increasing viability in construction due to their ability to execute high-precision actions while reducing human exposure to hazardous tasks. However, these environments have high-dimensional configuration spaces and possess substantial collision-avoidance constraints, which include other robots, assembly objects, and workspace boundaries. We utilize a single factor graph for trajectory estimation and planning that incorporates measured robot states together with explicit collision and learned cable constraints. This supports changing workspaces and enables synchronized, high-dimensional robot motion planning while accounting for the stiff, vibration-induced uncertainty of heavy robotic systems. We demonstrate the success of our framework on the construction of a post-and-lintel structure using one robot arm as a timber gripper, and a second robot as a nail-fastener.

</details>


### [46] [PredActor: Predictive Action Diffusion for Steerable Onboard Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24840)
*Lei Ye,Haibo Gao,Yitang Li,Peng Xu,Zetong Jing,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Ziqi Han,Xue Wang,Jianhua Sun,Cewu Lu,Hao Zhao,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredActor 是一种预测式动作扩散策略，仅用本体感觉观测，联合生成可执行动作与内部未来状态轨迹，并通过引导实现文本条件行为与测试时运动目标控制；在仿真和 Unitree G1 上验证，实时性满足 20 ms 控制周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型运动生成灵活，但用于反馈响应式人形控制仍困难：分层系统依赖参考轨迹，可能超出独立跟踪器能力；动作扩散直接生成动作但缺乏显式未来状态轨迹；联合状态-动作扩散虽提供该表示，却常依赖特权全身状态，且行为选择与测试时运动引导的支持较为碎片化。

Method: 以本体感觉历史与可选任务上下文为条件，联合扩散生成动作和未来状态轨迹；推理时仅执行动作，无需独立运动参考跟踪器或外部估计全身状态作为策略输入；采用无分类器引导增强文本条件行为，采用分类器引导将预测状态导向测试时目标；并通过滚动去噪与计算保持的运行时优化实现板载部署。

Result: 仿真中到达全部 15 个目标，文本检索得分 0.580，高于条件动作扩散的 0.373，扰动存活率相近；Jetson Orin NX 上完整回调中位数 16.790 ms、p95 19.383 ms，均低于 20 ms 控制周期；部署于 Unitree G1，仿真与硬件评估展示文本条件运动、扰动响应、手柄控制和语义插值。

Conclusion: PredActor 将文本条件、测试时引导、扰动响应与直接执行统一在单一预测式动作扩散策略中，并在真实人形机器人上实现满足控制周期约束的板载运行。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer flexible motion generation, but translating this flexibility into feedback-responsive humanoid control remains challenging. Hierarchical systems steer motion through references that may exceed a separate tracker's capabilities, leaving recovery and physical execution largely to the tracker. Action-only diffusion generates actions directly but lacks an explicit future-state trajectory for test-time motion objectives. Joint state-action diffusion provides this representation, yet representative controllers often depend on privileged full-body states, and support for learned behavior selection and test-time motion steering remains fragmented. We present PredActor, a predictive action diffusion policy that brings these complementary steering capabilities into one directly executed policy using proprioceptive observations. Conditioned on proprioceptive history and optional task context, PredActor jointly generates executable actions and an internal future-state trajectory. Classifier-free guidance strengthens text-conditioned behavior, while classifier guidance steers predicted states toward test-time objectives. Only actions are executed, without a separate motion-reference tracker or externally estimated full-body states as policy inputs. In simulation, PredActor reaches all 15 destination targets and achieves a text retrieval score of 0.580, compared with 0.373 for conditional action diffusion, with similar observed disturbance survival. To make this guided policy practical onboard, rolling denoising and computation-preserving runtime optimizations reduce the complete callback to 16.790 ms median and 19.383 ms p95 on a Jetson Orin NX, both below the 20 ms control period. We deploy PredActor on a Unitree G1; evaluations across simulation and physical hardware demonstrate text-conditioned motion, disturbance response, joystick control, and semantic interpolation.

</details>


### [47] [Minimum Time Trajectories for a Car-Like Mobile Robot Moving with Rigid Wheels Under Non-Sliding Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24832)
*Joseph Ben-Asher,Elon Rimon,Leeor Ravina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无障环境中受前轮加速度有界与前轮转向速率受限、可前进/后退的类车移动机器人最小时间轨迹，并在运动学模型基础上引入由动力学导出的非滑动约束及其新的时间最优路径基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有类车机器人时间最优轨迹研究多基于运动学模型，但该模型假设车轮与地面接触处纯滚动，要求前后轮满足非滑动约束；这一约束只能由机器人动力学处理，因此需要扩展原有运动学结果。

Method: 基于机器人动力学建立非滑动约束，并将其加入运动学时间最优路径基元中，新增三种与非滑动约束相关的路径基元；这三种非滑动路径基元与原运动学模型的十二种路径基元共同构成类车机器人时间最优轨迹，同时给出非滑动路径基元的近似解析解。

Result: 得到类车机器人时间最优轨迹由三种非滑动路径基元和十二种运动学路径基元组成，并提供非滑动路径基元的近似解析解；代表性机动示例表明非滑动约束会影响类车机器人的时间最优轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究将传统运动学时间最优规划扩展到考虑动力学非滑动约束的情形，有助于生成更符合真实约束的类车机器人最小时间机动轨迹。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum time trajectoriesvof a car-like mobile robot navigating in an obstacle free environment. The robot, with forward and backward speeds, is controlled by bounded front-wheels acceleration and limited front-wheels steering rate. The paper extends previous results which solved this problem for the kinematic car-like robot. However, the kinematic model assumes pure rolling at the wheels ground contacts. This assumption requires non-sliding constraints for the front and rear wheels that can only be handled by the robot dynamics. This paper formulates the non-sliding constraints based on the robot dynamics then augments the kinematic model time-optimal path primitives with three new path primitives associated with the non-sliding constraints. The three non-sliding path primitives together with the kinematic model twelve path primitives form the car-like robot time optimal trajectories. Approximate analytic solutions for the non-sliding path primitives are also provided. Examples study the time-optimal path primitives along representative maneuvers, illustrating how the non-sliding constraints influence the time optimal trajectories of the car-like robot.

</details>


### [48] [Uranus: Building the Next-Generation Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24815)
*Wenkang Qin,Yukun Zhou,Noah Shen,Jisong Cai,Dongxiao Mao,Baicheng Li,Yue Zhang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uranus：基于关节轨迹条件自回归扩散模型的数据驱动机器人模拟器，支持流式开放式推演、24 FPS 低延迟生成与跨本体多视角同步控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展仿真是机器人数据生成、策略训练、评估与安全迭代的关键，但真实交互成本高昂，传统模拟器构建需大量人工。

Method: 提出 Uranus，以关节轨迹为条件的自回归扩散模型为核心的数据驱动机器人模拟器，具备三项能力：(1) 流式开放式推演，在线接收未来关节位置轨迹并逐步自回归生成潜在帧，每步对应四帧 RGB，无固定视界；(2) 经推理优化后达 24 FPS 低延迟生成；(3) 统一接口支持跨多种机器人本体与相机配置的同步多视角生成，具可扩展性。

Result: 在分布内与分布外数据上进行全面定量与定性评估，客观评估 Uranus 性能并明确指出当前局限，同时开源代码与模型权重。

Conclusion: Uranus 为社区提供可扩展的数据驱动仿真工具与实用洞见，助力机器人数据生成、策略训练与评估等研究。

Abstract: Scalable simulation is essential for robot data generation, policy training, evaluation, and safe iteration, yet real-world interaction is costly and conventional simulators require labor-intensive construction. We present Uranus, a data-driven robot simulator built around a joint-trajectory-conditioned autoregressive diffusion model. Uranus offers three key capabilities: (1) streaming, open-ended rollout, which receives future joint-position trajectories online and autoregressively generates one latent frame per step, corresponding to four RGB frames, without a fixed horizon; (2) low-latency generation, achieving 24 FPS after inference optimization; and (3) scalable, extensible robot control, providing a unified interface for synchronized multi-view generation across diverse robot embodiments and camera configurations. We conduct comprehensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data, providing an objective assessment of Uranus and clearly identifying its current limitations. We release the code and model weights to empower the community with practical tools and insights.

</details>


### [49] [H2RBench: A Real-to-Sim Benchmark for Evaluating Human-to-Robot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24778)
*Chuyang Xiao,Haotian Zhan,Sriram Krishna,Peilin Meng,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 H2RBench，一个 Real2Sim 基准，用于标准化评估从人类视频演示学习机器人操作策略的人到机器人（H2R）迁移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 H2R 迁移方法在不同任务套件、场景布局、物体实例和机器人监督量下评估，难以进行公平、可复现的比较。

Method: H2RBench 基于真实人类视频演示与仿真机器人演示构建标准化协议，包含四个覆盖多样交互需求的操作任务；评估多种采用不同跨具身桥接策略的代表性 H2R 迁移方法，并系统分析其随人类演示量增加的性能扩展规律。

Result: 不同方法利用额外人类数据的能力差异显著；仿真性能对真实机器人性能具有总体预测性，跨方法-任务配置的 Pearson 相关系数为 r=0.89，Spearman 相关系数为 rho=0.85，Mean Maximum Rank Violation (MMRV) 为 0.06。

Conclusion: H2RBench 是实用且可扩展的 H2R 比较评估基准，可在真实世界部署前用于方法筛选与评估。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies from human video demonstrations constitutes a promising avenue for scalable robot learning. However, comparing different human-to-robot (H2R) transfer methods remains challenging, as existing approaches are evaluated under different settings, including differing task suites, scene layouts, object instances, and amounts of robot supervision. To address this challenge, we present H2RBench, a Real2Sim benchmark for evaluating H2R transfer methods. H2RBench provides a standardized protocol built on real human video demonstrations and simulated robot demonstrations, and includes four manipulation tasks spanning diverse interaction requirements. We evaluate multiple representative H2R transfer methods, each adopting a different strategy for bridging the embodiment gap. Using H2RBench, we systematically characterize how each method scales with the amount of human demonstrations, revealing that methods differ substantially in their ability to leverage additional human data. We further show that simulation performance is broadly predictive of real-world robot performance, with an overall Pearson correlation of r = 0.89, Spearman correlation of \r{ho} = 0.85 and Mean Maximum Rank Violation (MMRV) of 0.06 across method-task configurations. These results establish H2RBench as a practical and scalable benchmark for comparative H2R evaluation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [50] [A Switched Adaptive Control Framework for Aerial Manipulators Under Dynamic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24761)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Saksham Gupta,Amitabh Sharma,Sarthak Mishra,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy,Simone Baldi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于切换的自适应控制框架，用于空中机械臂，无需飞行器-机械臂耦合和状态相关不确定性的先验知识，并可保证动态过渡期间的稳定操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在执行复杂交互任务时会发生动态过渡，系统动力学出现突变或不连续，适合采用切换控制；但现有空中操控方法并非针对切换工况设计，且难以处理飞行器与机械臂之间的紧耦合及状态相关不确定性。

Method: 构建基于切换的自适应控制框架，不依赖飞行器-机械臂耦合与状态相关不确定性的先验知识；为保证系统动力学变化下的稳定操控，给出一类切换信号，刻画可保证系统稳定的过渡阶段。

Result: 通过对比实验验证了所提出的基于切换的框架相较于现有先进方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够应对空中机械臂任务执行中的切换动态与建模困难，实现稳定操控，并优于现有方法。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators represent the forefront of aerial robotics. Although potentially capable of complex interaction tasks, controlling aerial manipulators throughout the dynamic transitions occurring during task execution presents significant challenges. Abrupt or discontinuous changes in system dynamics generated by the transitions suggest the use of a switched approach, yet the available aerial manipulation methods are not designed for coping with switched regimes. In addition, most available methods fall short in coping with the tight couplings between the aerial vehicle and the manipulator, as well as in coping with the state-dependent uncertainties arising from the difficulty in modeling such couplings. We propose a switched-based adaptive control framework for aerial manipulators not relying on a priori knowledge of the vehicle-manipulator couplings and of state-dependent uncertainties. To guarantee stable manipulation despite changes in system dynamics, the framework provides a class of switching signals characterizing those transition phases for which the system is guaranteed to remain stable. Comparative experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed switched-based framework over the state of the art.

</details>


### [51] [D-JEPA: A Decision-Aligned Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24749)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chengyu Wu,Qifu Wen,Feiyang You,Chenglong Zhang,Shuyang Hao,Xi Lin,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-JEPA是一种决策对齐的潜在世界模型，通过从执行结果中学习候选未来之间的决策相关关系，弥合预测精度与决策成功之间的差距，从而提升动作选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型能预测动作后果，但预测准确并不保证潜在距离能反映哪个候选未来将成功执行。作者识别出“决策局部预测差距”：在少数竞争执行的未来中，预测更接近目标的候选可能产生比现有替代方案更差的真实结果。

Method: 提出D-JEPA：使用有界、置换等变算子联合推理目标相对预测特征与序数证据，在最影响动作选择处精炼预训练预测几何；通过受限预测器适配和共享序数接口跨互补预测几何扩展对齐；在JEPA兼容的未来表示中实现学习到的决策结构，支持原生潜在距离规划。

Result: 在潜在控制、操作、预训练动作生成模型、物理机器人及自动驾驶等任务上评估，动作选择均有改善：PushT成功率达87.89%，RoboTwin平均提升15.04点，物理机器人任务提升17点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，决策相关的关系结构可作为预测世界建模与有效控制之间的直接桥梁。

Abstract: Latent world models predict the consequences of actions, but accurate prediction does not guarantee that latent distance reflects which candidate will execute successfully. We identify a decision-local prediction gap: among the few futures competing for execution, a candidate predicted closer to the goal can produce a worse realized outcome than an available alternative. We introduce D-JEPA, a decision-aligned latent world model that learns decision-relevant relations among candidate futures from executed outcomes. A bounded, permutation-equivariant operator jointly reasons over goal-relative predictive features and ordinal evidence, refining pretrained predictive geometry where action choices are most consequential. Restricted predictor adaptation and a shared ordinal interface extend this alignment across complementary predictive geometries. D-JEPA further realizes the learned decision structure in JEPA-compatible future representations, enabling deployment through native latent-distance planning. Evaluations across latent control, manipulation, pretrained action-producing models, physical robots and autonomous driving demonstrate improved action selection, including 87.89% success on PushT, a 15.04-point average gain on RoboTwin, and a 17-point gain on physical robot tasks. These results establish decision-relevant relational structure as a direct bridge between predictive world modeling and effective control.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Visual Quality: A Study of Test-Time Planning with World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24745)
*Jianhao Yuan,Yu Yuan,Benjamin Ramtoula,Lukas Vierling,Paul Newman,Lars Kunze,Philip Torr,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实证检验世界动作模型（WAM）利用想象未来进行动作选择规划的潜力，发现基于生成预测的选择器虽偶有提升，但远未恢复候选动作中可用的选择收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM可同时生成动作及其视觉后果预测，从而支持从同一状态采样多个动作、比较想象结果并选择最优动作；然而，如何利用想象未来指导动作选择仍不明确，因此需要评估其规划潜力。

Method: 作者先通过选择实际结果最佳的候选动作估计选择性能的神谕上界，并进行同状态受控分析；随后以视觉质量、物理一致性和任务进展等指标构建选择器作为干预；进一步分析采样动作是否产生不同结果、这些差异是否体现在预测中以及评分能否识别差异，并通过同状态反事实分支和跨轨迹阶段完整执行进行评估。

Result: 神谕选择将成功率从均匀随机选择的68.9%提升至79.2%；测试的选择器部分可提高观测成功率，但增益不均衡，匹配条件下仍留下大量可恢复机会。反事实分支表明选择机会集中在初始候选集中的少数决策；动作分布与结果覆盖未必同步增加；跨轨迹阶段评估中，测试评分仍只能恢复很少可用提升，尽管学习价值带来小幅增益。

Conclusion: 研究区分了“产生有后果的动作选择”与“在生成未来中识别这些选择”的能力，主张应通过预测对决策的有用性而非仅凭视觉质量来评估WAM预测。

Abstract: World action models generate actions together with visual predictions of their consequences. These paired outputs create the potential for planning by sampling multiple actions from one state, comparing their imagined outcomes, and choosing the action with the most promising predicted outcome. However, how to use imagined futures to guide action selection remains unclear. We examine this planning potential empirically. First, we estimate an oracle upper bound on selection by choosing the sampled candidate whose realised outcome is best. In a controlled same-state analysis, this choice raises success from 68.9% under uniform random selection to 79.2%. We then test selectors based on visual quality, physical consistency, and task progression as controlled interventions. Some tested selectors yield higher observed success, but the gains are uneven and the matched selectors leave much of the measured opportunity unrecovered. To investigate this gap, we examine whether sampled actions lead to different outcomes, whether these differences are visible in the predictions, and whether a score recognises them. Counterfactual branching from the same states shows that selection opportunity is concentrated in relatively few decisions in the initial candidate sets. Action spread and outcome coverage need not increase together. In a further evaluation across trajectory phases with complete action execution, the tested scores again recover little of the available improvement despite a small gain from learned value. These findings distinguish producing consequential action choices from recognising them in generated futures, motivating the evaluation of WAM predictions through their usefulness for decisions rather than visual quality alone.

</details>


### [53] [LLM-based Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for MyBuddy Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24742)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个面向Raspberry Pi驱动的13轴MyBuddy人形机器人的基于LLM的多功能对话AI知识助手，集成语音识别、LLM推理、互联网知识检索、对话管理与语音合成，以实现多轮连续对话和情感支持型人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则、预定义响应和有限知识库的对话系统限制了人形机器人在智能对话和自然交互式知识辅助方面的能力；LLM为自然、自适应、上下文感知的人机交互提供了新基础。

Method: 在MyBuddy人形机器人上构建LLM驱动的对话AI知识助手，整合LLM语言理解与AI推理、实时语音识别、通过Wikipedia和arXiv等互联网引擎的可扩展知识检索、灵活对话管理以及自然语音合成。

Result: 该系统实现了更智能的多轮连续对话和高级情感支持型人机交互，提升了机器人的自然语音理解、复杂查询推理、对话上下文维护和知识丰富响应生成能力。

Conclusion: 将LLM与语音、检索、对话管理和语音合成相结合，可有效突破传统人形机器人对话系统的局限，为智能知识辅助和情感支持型HRI提供可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly being popular and developed for human-centered applications, yet their ability to provide intelligent conversations and natural interactive knowledge assistance remains constrained by traditional rule-based dialogue systems, pre-defined responses and limited knowledge repositories. Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful foundation for enabling natural, adaptive, and context-aware Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), which provides a significant opportunity to address such limitations by enabling robots to understand natural speech language, reason over complicated queries, maintain high-quality conversational context, and generate knowledge-rich responses. In this work, we originally present and implement an LLM-based versatile Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for the Raspberry-Pi-powered 13-Axis MyBuddy humanoid robot, which integrates LLM-driven language understanding and AI reasoning with real-time speech recognition, knowledge retrieval via extensible access of internet engines (e.g., Wikipedia, arXiv), flexible dialogue management, and natural speech synthesis to enable much more intelligent multi-turn continuous conversations and advanced emotional-support Human-Robot Interaction.

</details>


### [54] [SPARSER: Sparse Variable Projection by Exploiting Separable Structure in Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24708)
*Nikolas R. Sanderson,Andrew Fishberg,Haoyu Han,Heng Yang,Jonathan P. How,Hanumant Singh,Michael Everett,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARSER 是一种面向 gauge 对称可分离机器人感知问题的变量投影（VarPro）框架，通过保持稀疏性的解析消元，在 SLAM、SNL、SfM 等任务上显著加速非线性最小二乘求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知常需求解大规模非线性最小二乘问题。现有方法多利用稀疏性，但常忽略可分离结构：视觉路标等变量在残差中线性出现，固定位姿等变量后可闭式求解。变量投影可利用该结构，但受全局平移/旋转等 gauge 对称性限制，难以直接用于机器人感知。

Method: 提出 SPARSER（Sparsity Preserving Analytic Reduction for Separable Robotic Perception），一种面向 gauge 对称问题的 VarPro 框架，联合利用可分离性与稀疏性。构造无矩阵 Schur 补算子，高效计算约化代价、梯度与 Hessian-向量积，并可接入迭代 NLS 求解器；刻画适用问题类别，指出可进一步解析简化的常见情形，并证明基于 IRLS 的鲁棒代价可保留大部分可利用结构。

Result: 在合成与真实 SLAM、SNL、SfM 基准上，SPARSER 在 CPU 和 GPU 上平均比 SOTA 基线快 5–7 倍，个别数据集加速超过 40 倍；在含外点的多机器人 SLAM 数据上，鲁棒变体比 SOTA GNC 求解器快 2–16 倍。作者开源 C++ 代码与全部数据集。

Conclusion: SPARSER 有效解决了 gauge 对称下可分离 NLS 的 VarPro 应用难题，兼顾稀疏性与解析消元，为机器人感知提供了高效、可扩展且支持鲁棒代价的求解方案。

Abstract: Robotic perception often requires solving large nonlinear least-squares (NLS) problems. While sparsity has been widely exploited to scale solvers, a complementary and underused structure is \emph{separability}: some variables, such as visual landmarks, appear linearly in the residuals and admit a closed-form solution once the remaining variables, such as poses, are fixed. Variable projection (VarPro) exploits this structure by analytically eliminating the linear variables, yielding a reduced problem with favorable computational properties. However, its use in robotic perception has been limited by gauge symmetries, such as invariance to global translations and rotations, which introduce challenges for standard VarPro methods. We present SPARSER (\textbf{S}parsity \textbf{P}reserving \textbf{A}nalytic \textbf{R}eduction for \textbf{S}eparable \textbf{R}obotic \textbf{P}erception), a VarPro framework for gauge-symmetric problems that jointly exploits separability and sparsity. Our method constructs a \emph{matrix-free Schur complement operator} for efficient evaluation of reduced costs, gradients, and Hessian-vector products, enabling integration with iterative NLS solvers. We characterize the applicable problem class, identify common cases admitting further analytical simplifications, and show that IRLS-based robust costs preserve most of the exploitable structure. Across synthetic and real SLAM, SNL, and SfM benchmarks, SPARSER is on average $5\times$--$7\times$ faster than state-of-the-art baselines on CPU and GPU, with gains exceeding $40\times$ on individual datasets. On outlier-corrupted multi-robot SLAM data, the robust variant is $2\times$--$16\times$ faster than a state-of-the-art GNC solver. We release open-source C++ code and all datasets.

</details>


### [55] [AC-DC: Adaptive Communication for Scalable Dynamic Average Consensus in Multi-Robot Ergodic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24702)
*Robin Inho Kee,Begum Cannataro,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AC-DC，一种面向多机器人系统的可扩展点对点动态平均共识方法，通过自适应选择通信对象、时机与状态分量，在有限通信条件下实现高效协同，并显著降低通信量与搜索不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限通信距离、有限速率和干扰约束下，多机器人系统难以高效实现可扩展的点对点动态平均共识；传统方法通常需要等待完整通信轮次，且随团队规模增大通信负担加重。

Method: 提出AC-DC：利用局部输入和成功接收的邻居信息，联合自适应决定“与谁通信、何时通信、通信共识状态的哪些部分”；机器人对无需等待所有机器人完成通信轮次即可更新，所选状态消息的坐标维度不随团队规模增长。将其应用于动态优先级多机器人遍历搜索：一个共识流估计团队访问分布以协调运动，另一个融合区域测量信息以更新不确定性地图和搜索目标。

Result: 在多达80个机器人、12种设置、每种20次配对试验中，AC-DC在归一化协方差迹曲线下面积（AUC）和尝试建模通信负载上的均值最低；相较最先进基线，平均AUC降低27.5%，通信流量减少8.7倍。在固定600×600米、120个机器人的扩展测试中，AC-DC通信量为19.3 MB，接近理想集中式基线的19.2 MB，同时保持点对点架构。

Conclusion: AC-DC能在有限通信约束下实现可扩展的点对点动态平均共识，并在多机器人遍历搜索中同时提升协调性能与通信效率；随着机器人数量增加，其通信负载趋近理想集中式基线。

Abstract: We study scalable peer-to-peer dynamic average consensus (DC) for multi-robot systems under finite-range, finite-rate, and interference-constrained communication. We introduce Adaptive Communication for Dynamic Average Consensus (AC-DC), which jointly adapts Who communicates with whom, When, and over What parts of the consensus state, using local inputs and successfully received neighbor information. Each robot's consensus state estimates the current average of the robots' local inputs. AC-DC updates these estimates as local inputs change and averages the values exchanged between robot pairs. In AC-DC, robot pairs update without waiting for every robot to complete a communication round, and the selected-state messages carry consensus state coordinates independent of team size for a fixed state representation. We apply AC-DC to dynamic-priority multi-robot ergodic search: one consensus stream estimates team visitation for motion coordination, while the other fuses regional measurement information to update uncertainty maps and search targets. Across twelve settings with up to 80 robots and 20 paired trials per setting, AC-DC has the lowest mean (i) normalized covariance-trace area under the curve (AUC) and (ii) attempted modeled communication payload among the compared decentralized methods. Averaged across settings, AC-DC achieves paired AUC reductions of 27.5% relative to state-of-the-art baselines, with 8.7x less communication traffic. As the number of robots increases, we observe that AC-DC's communication payload approaches that of the ideal centralized baseline (one ground compute-station communicating directly with all robots): with 120 robots in a fixed 600 x 600 m scaling test, AC-DC uses 19.3 MB versus 19.2 MB for the ideal centralized baseline, while remaining peer-to-peer.

</details>


### [56] [Think Like a World Model, Act Like a VLA: Distilling World-Model Representations into Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24682)
*Trung Dao,Sankalp Yamsani,Jaden Park,Joohyung Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过将世界模型的内部表征对齐到VLA策略中，可在不增加推理开销的情况下提升机器人操作的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型仅以观测到动作的映射为目标，缺少对世界如何响应的建模，因此鲁棒性受数据覆盖限制；世界模型具备该目标且表征更接地，但未来rollout耗时高，难以进入控制回路。

Method: 将世界模型所学的物理场景知识归因于其内部特征，而非生成过程。冻结世界模型对训练帧推理一次并缓存特征，在常规VLA训练中加入一个特征对齐项，使学生策略与缓存特征一致。训练时不再加载教师，之后丢弃投影器；部署策略与未蒸馏基线完全相同，在RTX 5090上耗时32 ms、占用1.86 GB。增益因此归因于表征而非额外容量或测试时算力。

Result: 0.8B学生模型在LIBERO上达到97.9%；在RoboCasa-GR1人形操作上从48.2%提升至50.5%；同一目标迁移到真实硬件，包括单臂和双臂平台。增益在不同学生规模、骨干网络、对齐层和教师模型下均保持，表明其是广泛的表征先验而非特定网络间的脆弱对齐。

Conclusion: 世界模型的接地能力可与其生成机制解耦并被VLA继承；简单的特征对齐即可在极低部署开销下持续提升策略性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map observations to actions with no objective that accounts for how the world responds, so their robustness is bounded primarily by data coverage. World models carry precisely that missing objective and are better grounded for it, yet rolling the future forward costs seconds per decision and rules them out of the control loop. We show the two can be separated. What a world model knows about physical scenes lives in its \emph{internal features}; generating the future is merely the objective that produced them, so the grounding can be inherited while the generative machinery is left behind. We add one feature-alignment term to ordinary VLA training: a frozen world model is run over the training frames once and cached, and the student learns to agree with that cache. No teacher is loaded during training, the projector is discarded after it, and the deployed policy is identical to the undistilled baseline, running in $32$~ms and $1.86$~GB on a consumer RTX~5090, so every gain is attributable to the representation rather than to added capacity or test-time compute. A $0.8$B student reaches $97.9\%$ on LIBERO, improves from $48.2\%$ to $50.5\%$ on RoboCasa-GR1 humanoid manipulation, and the same objective carries over to real hardware, on both a single-arm and a bimanual platform. The gain survives changes of student scale, backbone, alignment layer, and teacher, indicating a broad representational prior rather than a fragile alignment between two particular networks. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Touch2Robot: Robot Touch in the Human Demonstration Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24660)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiaoyang Cheng,Hong Ying,Xiaoying Zhou,Jiaming Jiang,Haoran Guo,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Touch2Robot 框架，在人类演示过程中引入目标机器人手的接触反馈，从而提升灵巧操作数据采集的质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可扩展地采集操作数据，但其接触模式迁移到机器人手时可能不稳定或不可行；直接在目标机器人上采集虽避免不匹配，却显著增加成本。因此需要兼顾低成本人类演示与目标机器人接触可行性的方法。

Method: 采集人类手部运动、触觉手套测量和物体运动；以此指导物体特定的强化学习策略复现演示物体运动，并偏好与人类触觉一致的接触。将学习行为蒸馏为统一的实时重定向器，把人类观测与物体几何映射为目标机器人手构型。采集时，在仿真中同步预测的机器人构型与跟踪的物体位姿，重建机器人-物体接触并可视化，帮助演示者调整后续交互以适应目标手。

Result: 在四个真实世界任务中，相比仅视觉反馈，Touch2Robot 将真实机器人重放完成率从 37.9% 提升至 72.1%，并将每次重放成功演示的采集时间从 58.6 秒降至 18.2 秒。重建的目标手接触与真实机器人触觉测量达到 44.2% F1；用 Touch2Robot 演示训练的策略使下游 Diffusion Policy 性能比仅视觉反馈提高 29.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将机器人触觉引入人类演示闭环，可同时提升可扩展灵巧数据采集的质量与效率。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer a scalable way to collect manipulation data, but their contacts may be unstable or infeasible when transferred to a robot hand. Collecting demonstrations directly on the target robot avoids this mismatch, but substantially increases the cost of data collection. To address this trade-off, we present \textbf{Touch2Robot}, a framework that lets humans collect demonstrations while seeing how the target robot hand would contact the object. We capture human hand motion, tactile-glove measurements, and object motion during human manipulation. These recordings guide object-specific RL policies to reproduce the demonstrated object motion while favoring contacts consistent with the recorded human touch. We distill the learned behaviors into a unified real-time retargeter that maps incoming human observations and object geometry to robot hand configurations. During collection, the predicted robot configuration is synchronized with the tracked object pose in simulation to reconstruct robot-object contacts, which are visualized to help the demonstrator adapt subsequent interactions to the target hand. Across four real-world tasks, Touch2Robot improves average real-robot replay completion from 37.9\% to 72.1\% over visual-only feedback, while reducing the collection time per replay-successful demonstration from 58.6~s to 18.2~s. Reconstructed target-hand contacts achieve 44.2\% F1 against real-robot tactile measurements, and policies trained on Touch2Robot demonstrations improve downstream Diffusion Policy performance by 29.1 percentage points over visual-only feedback. These results show that bringing robot touch into the human demonstration loop improves both the quality and efficiency of scalable dexterous data collection. \textit{Project webpage: \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.}

</details>


### [58] [Feasibility Distance Fields for Heterogeneous Constraints in Robot Configuration Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24632)
*Xijing Cui,Huayan Pu,Jun Luo,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出可行性距离场（FDF），在固定正定关节空间度量下，将多种机器人约束统一表示为到不可行构型集并集的距离，并用于约束监测、组合与神经近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人约束指标的单位与梯度尺度不可比，无法统一衡量构型空间运动在违反约束前还剩多少余量。

Method: 定义 FDF 为固定正定关节空间度量下到不可行构型集并集的距离；利用距离集理论给出 1-Lipschitz、几乎处处可微及最近投影唯一处的单位对偶梯度范数。通过可容许性分析，为外/自碰撞、关节限位、灵巧度、笛卡尔/任务投影柔顺性、载荷下关节力矩和动态可操作度推导闭集形式。各场共享同一度量，故异质约束可按逐点最小值组合；条件约束保持固定距离空间；多机器人约束产生块稀疏梯度以识别需反应的机器人。生成基于投影标签，并用距离损失与 Eikonal 惩罚训练神经网络近似。

Result: 在 UR5e 与双臂单元上评估七个场，进行值、投影、符号、梯度、组合和移动障碍物诊断。8,000 个构型中，最大可行侧割线比为 0.920，学习梯度范数均值 0.994–0.998，投影残差 0.011–0.034 rad。24 条随机障碍路径中，外部碰撞场与组合碰撞场在 3 cm 内成功率分别为 90.4% 和 91.6%，符号错误率低于 2%。

Conclusion: 结果支持使用统一构型空间余量，并指出近似误差主要出现在中轴附近和边界采样稀疏处。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are monitored by constraint-specific indicators whose units and gradient scales are not comparable, so they do not provide a common measure of the configuration-space motion remaining before violation. We define the feasibility distance field (FDF) as the distance, under a fixed positive-definite joint-space metric, to the union of infeasible configuration sets. Classical distance-to-set theory gives 1-Lipschitz continuity, almost-everywhere differentiability, and unit dual-gradient norm wherever the nearest projection is unique. The robotics contribution is an admissibility analysis showing when practical constraints define non-empty closed sets. We derive admissible formulations for external and self-collision, joint limits, dexterity, Cartesian and task-projected compliance, joint torque under payload, and dynamic manipulability. Since every field uses the same metric, heterogeneous constraints compose by a pointwise minimum, conditioned constraints retain a fixed distance space, and multi-robot constraints produce block-sparse gradients that identify which robots must react. We generate projection-based labels and train neural approximations with a distance loss and an Eikonal penalty. Simulations on a UR5e and a dual-arm cell evaluate seven fields using value, projection, sign, gradient, composition, and moving-obstacle diagnostics. Across 8,000 configurations, the largest feasible-side secant ratio is 0.920, mean learned gradient norms range from 0.994 to 0.998, and projection residuals range from 0.011 to 0.034 rad. Across 24 random obstacle paths, the external and composed collision fields achieve 90.4% and 91.6% success within 3 cm, with sign-error rates below 2%. The results support a common configuration-space margin and identify approximation errors near medial axes and sparsely sampled boundaries.

</details>


### [59] [From Semantic Decisions to Feasible Trajectories: Self-Evolving LLM-Guided Optimal Control for Narrow-Space Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24631)
*Zhengbao Yao,Yuanfu Luo,Kehan Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SE-LLM-OCP：LLM 负责高层离散机动决策，最优控制负责底层动力学与避碰约束，通过在线稀疏机动规划与失败驱动重规划、离线知识库自演化，在狭窄非凸环境中实现更安全自主泊车，并跨运动学平台迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸狭窄环境中的自主泊车仍具挑战。最优控制方法虽能显式施加车辆动力学与碰撞约束，但非凸性会损害求解器鲁棒性并导致失败；LLM 具强语义推理能力，但直接生成稠密轨迹难以保证物理可行性。

Method: 提出统一框架 SE-LLM-OCP：LLM 做出高层离散机动决策，最优控制模块保障底层车辆动力学与碰撞约束。在线阶段，LLM 提出稀疏机动计划，将泊车任务分解为一系列短时域轨迹优化问题，由底层求解器顺序求解 OCP；若求解失败，LLM 聚合来自求解器与验证阶段的失败证据以引导重规划。离线阶段，SE-LLM-OCP 由累积的在线失败驱动，从零自动演化结构化决策知识库。
