Result: 在类车模型与差速驱动机器人上进行仿真验证。实验结果表明，SE-LLM-OCP 能在狭窄场景中实现更安全的自主泊车，并证明相同机动表示可迁移至不同运动学平台。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 的高层语义决策能力与最优控制的底层物理可行性保障相结合，并通过在线失败反馈与离线知识演化提升非凸狭窄泊车鲁棒性，且具备跨平台迁移能力。

Abstract: Autonomous parking in nonconvex and narrow environments remains challenging. Although optimal-control methods can explicitly enforce vehicle dynamics and collision constraints, nonconvexity compromises solver robustness and can cause failures. Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic reasoning capabilities, but directly generating dense trajectories makes it difficult to guarantee physical feasibility. We introduce SE-LLM-OCP, a unified framework in which LLMs make high-level discrete maneuver decisions, while an optimal-control module enforces low-level vehicle dynamics and collision constraints. Online, the LLM proposes sparse maneuver plans, decomposing the parking task into a sequence of short-horizon trajectory-optimization problems. A low-level solver then sequentially solves optimal-control problems. If the solver fails, the LLM aggregates failure evidence from the solver and validation stages to guide replanning. Offline, SE-LLM-OCP automatically evolves a structured decision-making knowledge base from scratch, driven by accumulated online failures. We validate our proposed framework in simulation on a car-like vehicle model and on a differential-drive robot. Our experimental results show that SE-LLM-OCP enables safer autonomous parking in narrow scenarios and demonstrates transfer of the same maneuver representation to a different kinematic platform.

</details>


### [60] [Learning tactile perception from high-bandwidth single-point sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24621)
*Joseph Rigal,Emmanuel Virot,Caroline Pascal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpectRobot框架，将单点触觉信号转换为紧凑的时频谱图，使其可由标准视觉编码器处理，从而利用稀疏高带宽单点测量的丰富动态信息，而非依赖空间分布式触觉阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型机器人操作多依赖空间分布式触觉传感器，但增加空间密度并非唯一路径；单点高带宽触觉信号中包含的时序与频率动态信息尚未被充分利用。

Method: 将单点触觉信号编码为固定尺寸、类图像的时频谱图，并接入原本面向视觉的学习流程；传感器远离接触面但与接触面机械耦合，以减少磨损；该表示可跨加速度、力或应变等不同触觉传感技术使用。

Result: 实验表明：机器人可利用单点振动信号完成视觉遮挡下的操作任务；时间历史显著影响策略性能，传感带宽决定可用频谱信息，测量可达100 kHz；同一表示适用于不同触觉传感技术；现成商用硬件也可实现此前需研究级仪器才能获得的能力。

Conclusion: 更易获取的高带宽触觉传感有助于将接触动力学融入具身学习系统，并在某些任务中成为增加触觉空间密度的替代方案或补充手段。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is increasingly being incorporated into learning-based robotic manipulation, yet many existing approaches rely on spatially distributed sensors. Here we introduce SpectRobot, a framework that transforms single-point tactile signals into compact time-frequency spectrograms. These spectrograms encode high-bandwidth tactile histories as fixed-size image-like representations. They can be processed by standard vision encoders and integrated into learning pipelines originally developed for vision, while preserving temporal and frequency information unavailable to conventional cameras. Rather than increasing spatial density through arrays of tactile elements, SpectRobot exploits the rich dynamics contained in sparse, high-bandwidth single-point measurements. In our implementation, the sensors are mounted away from the contact surface while remaining mechanically coupled to it, reducing direct exposure to wear and potentially improving robustness in harsh environments and for long-term deployment on dexterous robots. Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) a robot can exploit single-point vibration signals to solve a visually occluded manipulation task; (2) temporal history strongly influences policy performance, while sensing bandwidth controls the spectral information available, with measurements extending to 100~kHz; and (3) the same representation can be used across different tactile sensing technologies mediated by acceleration, force, or strain. We further show that capabilities previously associated with research-grade instrumentation can be accessed using readily available, off-the-shelf hardware. We believe that broader access to high-bandwidth tactile sensing could facilitate the integration of contact dynamics into embodied learning systems and, for some tasks, offer an alternative or complement to increasing the spatial density of tactile sensing.

</details>


### [61] [What do VLM-Based Vision-Language Navigation Models Rely on: Interpreting and Steering Policy Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24576)
*Débora Oliveira Makowski,Samiran Gode,Abhijeet Nayak,Marco Hutter,Cordelia Schmid,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言导航（VLN）模型的可解释性与可操控性，利用干预指标揭示视觉观察、指令和视觉记忆对导航决策的因果影响，并证明可通过内部激活实现概念级操控和零样本行为迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLN模型多依赖预训练大型视觉语言模型（VLM）预测导航动作。尽管这种语言指令与视觉观察的融合支持多模态推理，但信息如何在模态间路由、何种机制驱动导航决策仍不透明；因此尚不清楚VLN模型是否基于相关语义线索进行预测，或能否跟踪任务进度。

Method: 使用基于干预的度量，量化视觉观察、指令和视觉记忆对导航决策的因果影响；分析模型内部激活以检验导航进度编码和VLM骨干保留的语义结构；提取抽象行为的激活向量，并将其零样本迁移到分布外（OOD）真实场景。

Result: 结果表明，导航策略对所有输入模态均敏感，并不依赖单一模态；智能体能够编码导航进度并保留VLM骨干的语义结构，从而支持通过内部激活进行概念级操控；抽象行为的激活向量可零样本迁移至OOD真实场景，无需额外微调即可提升性能。

Conclusion: VLN模型具有可解释且可操控的内部表征，其决策受多模态信息因果影响，并可通过激活干预与抽象行为迁移改善泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) models rely mostly on pre-trained large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to predict navigation actions. While this fusion of language instructions and visual observations allows multimodal reasoning, it obscures how information is routed across modalities or what mechanisms drive navigation decisions. Thus, it remains unclear whether VLN models ground their predictions in relevant semantic cues or can track task progress. In this work, we study the interpretability and steerability of VLN models. We use intervention-based metrics that measure how visual observations, instructions, and visual memory causally influence navigation decisions. Our results show that these navigation policies are sensitive to all input modalities and do not depend on a single one. We further show that these agents encode navigation progress and retain semantic structure from their VLM backbones, enabling concept-level steering through internal activations. Finally, we extract activation vectors for abstract behaviors to transfer them zero-shot to out-of-distribution real-world scenarios, improving performance without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [62] [ARSTAG: An Agentic Real2Sim2Real System for Task-Specific Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24563)
*Bowei Li,Yuner Zhang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARSTAG 是一个由语言智能体驱动的 Real2Sim2Real 系统，可将单张 RGB 图像与自然语言指令自动转化为机器人策略学习数据，并在七项操作任务上实现双机械臂的 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉运动策略适配到新操作任务通常依赖大量人工工程或遥操作数据采集；仿真虽可规模化提供任务数据，但场景构建、专家行为设计与数据生成配置仍需逐任务投入。

Method: ARSTAG 采用分层语言智能体：构建任务限定的仿真场景、生成机器人可行的演示，并通过任务一致性随机化扩展训练分布；协调智能体负责跨阶段反馈与失败恢复。输入为单张 RGB 图像和自然语言指令。

Result: 在抓取、放置与堆叠等七项操作任务中，ARSTAG 生成演示使三种视觉运动策略架构成功迁移至双机械臂；pi0.5 平均真实世界成功率达 74.6%。消融表明任务一致性随机化显著提升鲁棒性，且策略性能随生成数据集规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: ARSTAG 展示了以智能体自动化 Real2Sim2Real 流程、降低人工工程与数据采集成本、并生成可扩展机器人策略学习数据的可行性。

Abstract: Adapting visuomotor policies to new manipulation tasks often requires substantial manual engineering or teleoperated data collection. Simulation can provide task-specific data at scale, but constructing the scene, designing expert behavior, and configuring data generation still require significant per-task effort. We present ARSTAG, an agentic Real2Sim2Real system that turns a single RGB image and a natural-language instruction directly into robot policy-learning data. A hierarchy of language agents constructs a task-scoped simulation scene, generates robot-feasible demonstrations, and expands the training distribution through task-consistent randomization, while a coordinator agent manages cross-stage feedback and recovery. Across seven manipulation tasks spanning grasping, placement, and stacking, the ARSTAG-generated demonstrations enable sim-to-real transfer of three visuomotor policy architectures to a dual-arm robot, with pi0.5 achieving an average real-world success rate of 74.6%. Ablations show that task-consistent randomization substantially improves robustness, and policy performance increases with generated dataset size. Project webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [Smoothness as a Constraint for Stable Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24552)
*Utsav Panchal,Denis Kleyko,Unal Artan,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DeCap：一种将全身平滑性解耦为上、下半身约束组的约束强化学习算法，用于真实人形机器人全身控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习通常通过奖励函数中的辅助平滑项来约束全身运动，这会与任务目标竞争，且将身体视为均一整体，无法直接控制决定平滑性的物理量；而真实部署需要下半身保持反应性、上半身严格稳定。

Method: DeCap 将全身平滑性解耦为上半身和下半身两个独立约束组，把平滑性显式建模为对物理运动极限的约束；并引入有界障碍惩罚，当接近约束边界时主动激活，且在约束极限处保持有界，以提升可行性边界附近的约束满足度。

Result: 在真实人形机器人全身控制任务上，相比基于奖励的平滑策略，DeCap 将上半身动作速率降低 2.50 倍、加速度降低 2.18 倍，同时改善下半身平滑性并减少瞬态运动。

Conclusion: 固定的一组平滑性约束可跨多种地形迁移，减少大量奖励调参需求，表明解耦约束式方法更适合真实人形机器人全身控制中的平滑性与任务响应性平衡。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, particularly humanoid robots deployed in real world scenarios require whole-body control policies that are both task-responsive and physically smooth. However, smoothness is not uniform across the body: lower body must remain sufficiently reactive, while the upper body must be tightly regulated to preserve stability. Existing reinforcement learning approaches typically impose smoothness through auxiliary terms in the reward function, which compete with task objectives, treating the body as uniform and provide no direct control over the physical quantities responsible for smooth behavior. We introduce DeCap (Decoupled Constraint-aware policy), a constrained reinforcement learning algorithm that decouples whole-body smoothness into separate upper- and lower-body constraint groups, each formulates smoothness as explicit constraints on physical motion limits. To improve constraint satisfaction near feasibility boundaries, DeCap incorporates a bounded barrier penalty that activates proactively as limits are approached and remains bounded at the constraint limit. On real-world humanoid whole-body control task, DeCap reduces upper-body action rate by 2.50x and acceleration by 2.18x relative to reward-based smoothness policies, while also improving lower-body smoothness and reducing transient motion. We demonstrate that a fixed set of smoothness constraints transfers across diverse terrains, alleviating the need of extensive reward tuning.

</details>


### [64] [MIRA: Real-Time Full-Duplex Human-Robot Interaction for Embodied Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24547)
*Lijian Lin,Ye Zhu,Fan Zhang,Yunfei Liu,Baofeng Li,Xianwen Zeng,Jianan Wang,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MIRA，一个用于全双工具身陪伴交互的统一框架，在流式语音下预测回复文本与具身线索，并生成可中断的共语动作，部署于Astribot S1人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将对话编排与手势合成解耦，依赖完整音频的离线动作生成，难以在增量输入和不确定轮次边界下动态同步回复内容、韵律时序与物理安全。

Method: MIRA以流式用户语音、对话历史与语音情感为输入，同时预测回复文本和显式具身线索；离散社交行为（如倾听、问候）映射到已验证的机器人轨迹，开放式说话则配对流式共语动作。生成动作由“预测多于提交”的滑动窗口控制，提供时序前瞻以保持动作连续性，同时仅承诺短小可取消的前缀。CORTEX双时间尺度交互策略管理低延迟流式处理与深思熟虑的轮次决策，机器人侧执行层以控制频率强制物理安全约束。

Result: 定量评估表明，与最先进的动作生成基线相比，MIRA在音频-动作对齐上具有竞争力；真机部署测量刻画了流式响应性与中断处理能力。

Conclusion: MIRA为实现内容、韵律时序与物理安全动态同步的全双工具身陪伴交互提供了可行框架，并在人形机器人上验证了其实时性与可中断性。

Abstract: Real-time embodied companion interaction requires a robot to infer user intent from streaming speech, generate timely responses, and execute expressive, interruptible motions. Existing systems typically decouple dialogue orchestration from gesture synthesis, relying on offline motion generation from complete audio. This separation leaves open how a deployed robot can dynamically synchronize response content, prosodic timing, and physical safety under incremental inputs and uncertain turn boundaries. We present MIRA, a unified framework for full-duplex embodied companion interaction. Given streaming user speech, dialogue history, and vocal affect, MIRA predicts both the response text and an explicit embodiment cue. Discrete social behaviors (\eg listening, greeting) are mapped to validated robot trajectories, while open-ended speaking is paired with streaming, co-speech motion. This generative motion is governed by a predict-more-than-commit sliding window that provides temporal look-ahead for motion continuity while limiting physical commitment to a short, cancellable prefix. Crucially, we design CORTEX, a dual-timescale interaction policy that manages low-latency streaming and deliberative turn decisions, backed by a robot-side execution layer that enforces physical safety constraints at the control rate. We deploy MIRA on an Astribot S1 humanoid robot. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate competitive audio-motion alignment relative to state-of-the-art motion-generation baselines, while real-robot deployment measurements characterize streaming responsiveness and interruption handling.

</details>


### [65] [Estimation and Control of Tensegrity Manipulator Kinematics based on Strut Inclination Angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24535)
*Tufail Ahmad Bhat,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于IMU与几何约束的降阶模型，用于张拉整体连续机械臂的形状估计与姿态控制，并完成全尺寸实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性连杆机器人由离散关节定义，而连续机器人具有复杂连续弯曲构型，难以建模并用于闭环控制；现有连续机器人建模方法多假设连续弹性骨干，无法直接适用于由刚性杆和受拉索网络构成的张拉整体机械臂。

Method: 将张拉整体连续机械臂建模为串联并联连杆机构；结合张拉整体结构的几何约束与嵌入杆件中的IMU传感器信息，构建优化问题以估计形状；采用简单PI控制器实现姿态控制。

Result: 首次在全尺寸张拉整体机械臂上实验演示实时IMU形状估计方法；方法可从任意静态构型估计单模块张拉整体结构和多模块张拉整体机械臂的形状，并实现期望姿态。

Conclusion: 所提降阶模型可用于张拉整体连续机械臂的形状估计与姿态控制，为这类连续机器人闭环控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Unlike conventional rigid-link robots defined by discrete joints, continuum robots pose a fundamental challenge for expressing their complex continuous bending configurations for closed-loop control. Several modelling approaches have been proposed for conventional continuum robots, but tensegrity-based continuum robots remain largely open. Moreover, many of these approaches assume a continuous elastic backbone and are therefore not directly applicable to tensegrity manipulators, whose bodies are networks of rigid struts and tensioned cables. This work presents a reduced-order model for shape and posture control of a tensegrity-based continuum manipulator. The manipulator is modelled as a serially connected parallel-link mechanism. The proposed method is formulated as an optimization problem that uses geometric constraints of the tensegrity structure together with information from the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors embedded in the strut elements. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first experimental demonstration of a real-time IMU-based shape estimation method on a full-scale tensegrity manipulator and demonstrates posture control using a simple Proportional-Integral (PI) controller. The results show that the proposed method can estimate the shape of both single-module tensegrity structures and multi-module tensegrity manipulators from arbitrary static configurations and achieve desired postures.

</details>


### [66] [Bridge3D: Enabling Vision-Language-Action Models to See and Act in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24525)
*Haoxuan Li,Sixu Yan,Lianghui Zhu,Xuanlai Tang,Shikang Wang,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge3D 将隐式与显式 3D 几何引导注入预训练 2D VLA 模型，使机器人操作能够在 3D 中“看”和“动”，并在仿真与真实任务中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要基于 2D 观测训练，空间精确操作能力受限；已有方法虽引入隐式空间先验，但仍缺乏显式几何指导。

Method: 提出 Bridge3D，包含两种策略：1）Implicit Fusion，用 3D 基础模型特征增强视觉 token 以改善 3D“看”；2）Explicit Conditioning，将动作去噪与显式 3D 语义场结合以实现 3D“动”。同时采用 layer-wise linear probing 提升学习效率。

Result: 实验表明 Bridge3D 优于 SOTA。在 RoboTwin 2.0 上比 π0 高 14.0 个百分点；在真实世界实验中比 Spatial Forcing 高 11.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: Bridge3D 在高精度、空间敏感操作任务中表现突出，验证了将隐式与显式 3D 几何引导融入 2D VLA 模型的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable generalization in robotic manipulation via large-scale multimodal pretraining. However, VLA models are mainly trained on 2D-centric observations, which inherently constrains their capacity for precise spatial manipulation. Previous methods enhance 3D awareness by introducing implicit spatial priors, but still lack explicit geometry guidance. In this paper, we propose Bridge3D that integrates both implicit and explicit 3D geometry guidance into pre-trained 2D VLA models, enabling them to ''see'' and ''act'' in 3D. Bridge3D introduces two strategies: 1) Implicit Fusion, which enriches visual tokens with features from 3D foundation models to improve ''seeing'' in 3D; 2) Explicit Conditioning, which integrates action denoising with an explicit 3D semantic field to achieve ''acting'' in 3D. Furthermore, we utilize the proposed layer-wise linear probing to improve learning efficiency. Experiments show that Bridge3D achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art methods. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, Bridge3D exceeds $\pi_0$ by 14.0 percentage points, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms Spatial Forcing by 11.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate Bridge3D's strong capabilities in high-precision and spatial-sensitive manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [67] [InsertAnything: Generalizable Contact-Rich Precision Insertion from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24511)
*Zhenghua Ma,Xinpan Meng,Zeyu Liu,Muyuan Ma,Hengdi Zhang,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种完全在仿真中训练的强化学习框架，实现接触丰富的精密插入策略的直接真机部署与跨任务复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密插入对对齐误差敏感，小间隙下易碰撞和卡阻；零件几何与间隙变化使策略难以复用，且依赖真机示教或微调成本高。

Method: 在仿真中训练插入策略，输入目标位姿与紧凑的三维指尖力反馈，使策略在孔位估计有误差时搜索对齐并修正运动；采用解耦门控奖励协调对齐与插入，并通过力信号平滑和状态无关标准差稳定学习。

Result: 策略在最小名义间隙0.02 mm的多种孔几何上完成真机插入，在孔位误差下提高成功率并降低峰值接触力；跨间隙与跨几何评估证实泛化。系统在ManipulationNet的peg-in-hole基准Human-in-the-Loop协议下以完全自主插入动作首次获得20/20满分；仅在仿真六边形插入任务上训练的单一策略在八个未见真机插入任务上取得95.0%总体成功率。

Conclusion: 完全仿真学习可产生可直接部署并跨真机任务复用的精密插入技能，项目提供开源仿真与真机实验脚本、资产及训练检查点。

Abstract: Contact-rich precision insertion is a key manipulation skill in robotic assembly. Tight clearances make insertion more sensitive to alignment errors and prone to collisions and jamming, while variations in geometry and clearance across parts further complicate policy reuse. We present a reinforcement learning framework that trains insertion policies entirely in simulation for direct deployment without real-world demonstrations or policy fine-tuning. By combining target poses with compact three-dimensional fingertip force feedback, the policy learns to search for alignment and correct its motion despite errors in the estimated hole position. A decoupled gated reward coordinates alignment and insertion. Force-signal smoothing and state-independent standard deviations stabilize the learning process. The resulting policies perform real-world insertion across multiple hole geometries with a minimum nominal clearance of 0.02 mm and improve success while reducing peak contact forces under hole-position errors. Cross-clearance and cross-geometry evaluations further confirm policy generalization. The system achieved the first perfect score of 20/20 on ManipulationNet's peg-in-hole benchmark under its Human-in-the-Loop protocol, with fully autonomous insertion motions. A single policy trained only on a simulated hexagonal insertion task achieved an overall success rate of 95.0% across eight unseen real-world insertion tasks. These results show that learning entirely in simulation can yield precision insertion skills that can be deployed directly and reused across real-world tasks. The project website ( this https URL ) provides open-source simulation and real-robot experiment scripts, assets, and trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [68] [TACIT: Tactile Contact Supervision for Spatial Attention in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24507)
*Yanhou Lai,Fucai Zhu,Ruiqiang Wang,Koichi Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACIT用遥操作演示中测得的触觉接触监督空间注意力，无需额外点标注，从而在少量演示下提升视觉触觉扩散策略的接近与放置/插入成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少量演示训练的视觉运动策略常能复现轨迹，却不能可靠跟随物体位置变化；现有显式注意力方法通常依赖人工标注或视觉模型获取空间先验。

Method: TACIT（tactile contact informs attention）利用遥操作演示中测得的触觉接触来监督空间注意力，无需额外点标注。其以前序相机点云上的高斯目标监督注意力头，注意力头池化输出条件化视觉触觉扩散策略。训练时使用目标，推理时触觉观测仍作为输入。

Result: 在真实机器人基准中，每任务10条演示、5个放置区域，TACIT在球体放置达66.7%、插销插入达73.3%，而输入匹配的3D视觉触觉融合为10.0%和20.0%，仅视觉DP3为20.0%和43.3%。TACIT在每任务30次试验中均于12秒内进入150 mm掌-物接近区域，剩余失败均发生在到达之后。真实球体和仿真插销上三种训练种子显示，TACIT优于输入匹配融合和无显式注意力监督的架构匹配控制，支持监督贡献超出分支容量；接触前与接触时监督无一致排序。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在所评估工作空间内，测得的触觉接触能为少量演示下的接近行为提供有效的空间监督。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies trained from a few demonstrations may reproduce demonstrated trajectories without reliably following changes in object position. Existing approaches with explicit attention typically obtain spatial priors from human annotation or visual models. We introduce TACIT (tactile contact informs attention), which uses measured tactile contacts from teleoperated demonstrations to supervise spatial attention without additional point annotation. Gaussian targets over preceding camera point clouds supervise an attention head whose pooled output conditions a visuotactile diffusion policy. Targets are used only during training; tactile observations remain inputs at inference. In the primary real-robot benchmark, with ten demonstrations per task and five demonstrated placement regions, TACIT achieves 66.7% success on ball placement and 73.3% on peg insertion, compared with 10.0% and 20.0% for input-matched 3D visuotactile fusion and 20.0% and 43.3% for vision-only DP3. TACIT enters the 150 mm palm-to-object approach region within 12 seconds in all 30 trials per task; all remaining failures occur after arrival. Across three training seeds on real ball and simulated peg, TACIT outperforms input-matched fusion and an architecture-matched control without explicit attention supervision, supporting the contribution of supervision beyond branch capacity. Pre-contact and contact-time supervision show no consistent ordering. These results demonstrate that measured tactile contact provides effective spatial supervision for approach behavior from few demonstrations within the evaluated workspace.

</details>


### [69] [A Monolithic Force-Proprioception Soft Acutuator Enabled by Single-Material 3D printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24499)
*Nan Huang,Lele Liu,Junfeng Lu,Yipan Zhu,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MFPS一体化设计与制造方法，通过单材料一步FDM将非对称折纸弯曲气腔与力感知柔性传感器集成，实现软体执行器的自感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气动本体感知执行器常因异质材料集成导致装配误差或应力集中，需要一种单材料、一体化且兼具驱动与力感知能力的软体执行器方案。

Method: 基于导电TPU的应变电阻响应设计并分析FPS传感器结构，开展传感特性参数化分析；分析MFPS执行器的FDM制造参数，完成执行器制造，并对其驱动与本体感知性能进行表征。

Result: MFPS执行器实现40°弯曲角度、12.5 N输出力；外加力从0增至45 N时电阻变化26.9%；基于该执行器开发两指力感知夹爪，并实验验证抓取与力感知能力。

Conclusion: MFPS设计方法为自感知执行器的发展提供了新途径，验证了单材料一体化集成驱动与力感知的可行性。

Abstract: Pneumatic proprioceptive actuators integrate actuation and sensing for soft robots that attract interest due to functional potential. Existing approaches often suffer from assembly errors or stress concentrations caused by heterogeneous materials. In this work, we propose the Monolithic Force-Proprioception Soft (MFPS) design and fabrication method that integrates an Asymmetric Origami Bending (AOB) chamber and a Force-Proprioception Soft (FPS) sensor with single material through one-step Fused Deposition Modeling (FDM) fabrication. Based on the resistance response to strain of conductive thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU), we design and analyze the structure of the FPS sensor, and conduct parametric analysis on the sensing characteristics. The FDM fabrication parameters of the MFPS actuator are analyzed, followed by actuator fabrication and characterization of the actuation and proprioception performance. Experimental results show that the MFPS actuator achieves a bending angle of 40°, an output force of 12.5 N, and a resistance change of 26.9% as the applied external force increased from 0 to 45 N. A two-finger force-proprioception gripper is developed based on the MFPS actuator. The grasping and force-proprioception capabilities are experimentally validated, proving that the MFPS design method provides a new approach for the development of self-sensing actuators.

</details>


### [70] [ScaleMPA: Rethinking Scalable RRT* Acceleration With a Grid-Native Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24497)
*Zilong Wang,Yuzhou Chen,Xinyue He,Chen Zhang,Guanghui He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScaleMPA 是一种面向 RRT* 的网格原生运动规划加速器，通过多分辨率网格搜索与哈希网格存储，在 28 nm CMOS 上实现毫秒级规划延迟，并较当前最优运动规划加速器取得 4.7×–44.4× 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、高维环境中的实时运动规划仍具挑战。已有 RRT* 加速方案采用树中心的状态组织，虽降低单次查询开销，但端到端复杂度仍为超线性，且结构依赖限制并行性。

Method: 提出 ScaleMPA，以网格原生表示重构 RRT*：用直接网格访问替代层次化遍历，缩短规划器关键路径并暴露细粒度并行；针对稀疏高维规划，设计多分辨率网格搜索引擎和哈希网格内存系统；在 28 nm CMOS 中实现。

Result: 实现毫秒级规划延迟，相比当前最优运动规划加速器获得 4.7×–44.4× 的加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明，网格原生的 RRT* 加速架构可有效降低规划关键路径并提升并行性，为大规模高维实时运动规划提供可行硬件方案。

Abstract: Real-time motion planning remains challenging in large and high-dimensional environments. Prior acceleration of RRT* follows tree-centric state organization, which reduces per-query cost but preserves superlinear end-to-end complexity and limits parallelism through structural dependencies. This paper presents ScaleMPA, a motion-planning accelerator that rethinks RRT* with a grid-native representation. By replacing hierarchical traversal with direct grid-based access, ScaleMPA reduces the planner critical path and exposes fine-grained parallelism. To make this reformulation practical under sparse high-dimensional planning, ScaleMPA further proposes a multi-resolution grid search engine and a hash-grid memory system. Implemented in 28 nm CMOS, ScaleMPA achieves millisecond-level planning latency and delivers 4.7$\times$--44.4$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art motion-planning accelerators.

</details>


### [71] [FoldQuantVLA: Native Low-Bit Quantization of Vision-Language-Action Models via Consistent Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24433)
*Hung T. Ho,Khanh D. Nguyen,Quang D. Nguyen,Thanh Q. Duong,Ngan Le,Meng Guo,Vien A. Ngo,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldQuantVLA 是一种无需策略重训练的后训练量化框架，通过统一激活表示与混合 W4A4/W8A8 执行，在降低视觉-语言-动作模型推理延迟的同时保持机器人动作保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特视觉-语言-动作推理需要在降低观测到动作延迟的同时保持机器人行为。现有量化若不一致或需重训练，难以兼顾效率与动作质量。

Method: 提出 FoldQuantVLA 后训练量化框架，使校准、权重量化和原生整数执行共享一致的激活表示；结合通道缩放、块 Hadamard 变换与动态逐 token 量化，无需策略重训练。通过自定义 TensorRT 插件，在语言骨干和迭代动作专家中执行 4 比特权重与激活（W4A4），面向 Ada GPU 与 Jetson AGX Orin；并对部分层采用 W8A8，包括语言注意力输出和 FFN 下投影。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv 和两个机器人平台上评估，覆盖三个 GR00T checkpoint 与 π0.5。W4A4 在 Orin 上相对浮点 TensorRT 加速 1.20 至 1.33 倍，在桌面端加速 1.25 至 1.52 倍。将语言注意力输出和 FFN 下投影保留为 W8A8 可提升全部四个 checkpoint 的留出动作保真度。在四个真实机器人任务中，该配置将 GR00T N1.7 成功率从均匀 W4A4 的 80.0% 提升至 92.5%，每种配置 80 次试验，Orin 额外延迟测得 1 ms。

Conclusion: FoldQuantVLA 表明，W4A4 可显著加速 VLA 推理，而少量关键层采用 W8A8 的混合量化能在可接受的额外延迟下保持甚至提升真实机器人任务成功率，适合部署于边缘与桌面平台。

Abstract: Low-bit vision-language-action inference must reduce observation-to-action latency while preserving robot behavior. We present FoldQuantVLA, a post-training quantization framework that carries a consistent activation representation through calibration, weight rounding, and native integer execution. It combines channel scaling and block Hadamard transforms with dynamic per-token quantization, without policy retraining. Custom TensorRT plugins execute projections in both the language backbone and iterative action expert with four-bit weights and activations (W4A4) on Ada GPUs and Jetson AGX Orin. Evaluation spans LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and two robot platforms. Across three GR00T checkpoints and $\pi_{0.5}$, W4A4 achieves $1.20$ to $1.33\times$ speedups over floating-point TensorRT on Orin and $1.25$ to $1.52\times$ on desktop. Retaining language attention-output and feed-forward down projections at eight bits (W8A8) improves held-out action fidelity on all four checkpoints. Across four real-robot tasks, this configuration raises observed GR00T N1.7 success from $80.0\%$ with uniform W4A4 to $92.5\%$ over 80 trials per configuration, with a measured additional Orin latency of 1 ms.

</details>


### [72] [Robotic Valve Turning: Axial Misalignment Correction Using Reaction Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24413)
*Amit Kumar,Sri Harsha Turlapati,Gautami Golani,Yang Lin,Ravi N. Banavar,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用反作用力矩修正机器人阀门操作中轴向错位的触觉更新控制律，并证明其一致渐近稳定性，通过仿真和Kinova Gen3机械臂实验验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的错位估计易受标定误差、遮挡以及接触几何不确定性影响；而反作用力矩直接来源于夹爪与阀门之间的物理交互，因此更适合用于修正轴向错位。

Method: 建立错位向量与反作用力矩之间的几何关系，并据此设计稳定控制器；控制律被证明为一致渐近稳定。通过仿真验证，并在Kinova Gen3机械臂上进行实验，测试初始错位从-15°到15°、3个不同阀门位置下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在全部18个测试案例中，中位错位绝对值在2.46°以内，反作用力矩中位绝对值在0.23 Nm以内。

Conclusion: 所提出的触觉更新控制律能够利用反作用力矩有效修正轴向错位，具有一致渐近稳定性，并在不同初始错位和阀门位置下表现出较好鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a haptic update control law that uses reaction torques to correct axial misalignment during robotic valve manipulation. Unlike vision-based estimates, which can be affected by calibration errors, occlusion, and uncertainty in the contact geometry, reaction torques arise directly from the physical interaction between the gripper and valve. A geometric relationship exists between the error (misalignment) vector and these torques. The primary aim of this work is to propose a stable controller exploiting this geometric property. Our control law is proven to be uniformly asymptotically stable. Simulations are performed for verification. Furthermore, we experimentally test the robustness of our method using a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm for initial misalignments ranging from $-15^\circ$ to $15^\circ$ at 3 different valve positions and report the resulting data distribution. The absolute value of the median misalignment across all 18 test cases is found to be within $2.46^\circ$ and that of reaction torques within $0.23\mathrm{Nm}$.

</details>


### [73] [Zeva-Ego: Egocentric Mid-Training with In-Context Causal Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24411)
*Bingjia Huang,Xin Ding,Fu Chen,Kun Li,Wei Sun,Hao Wu,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Zeva-Ego 提出统一框架，从第一视角人类视频学习物理先验，并通过机器人交互持续进化；ACE 支持 VLA 中期训练，ICCL 实现部署时无参数自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频可规模化提供物理交互经验，但将其转化为机器人可执行知识并实现持续适应仍具挑战。

Method: Zeva-Ego 统一框架包含：Action-Centric Encoder（ACE），将自我中心视觉状态转移转换为以动作为中心的监督，用于 VLA 中期训练；In-Context Causal Learning（ICCL），在部署阶段基于动作-效果反馈实现无参数适应。

Result: 将 Ego 数据扩展至 10K 小时，使 RoboTwin 成功率从 63.8% 提升至 75.3%，匹配 2K 小时机器人演示的 74.7%，经验数据比约为 4-5:1；累积交互经验后，ICCL 在四次尝试内将成功率从 58% 提升至 89%，且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一条可扩展的具身智能路径：从人类经验中学习，并通过自身交互持续改进。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers a scalable source of physical interaction experience, yet translating it into robot-executable knowledge and enabling continual adaptation remain challenging. We introduce Zeva-Ego, a unified framework that learns physical priors from human experience and evolves through robot interaction. An Action-Centric Encoder (ACE) converts egocentric visual transitions into action-centered supervision for VLA mid-training, while In-Context Causal Learning (ICCL) enables parameter-free adaptation from action-effect feedback at deployment. Scaling Ego data to 10K hours improves RoboTwin success from 63.8% to 75.3%, matching 2K hours of robot demonstrations (74.7%), corresponding to an empirical data ratio of roughly 4-5:1. With accumulated interaction experience, ICCL further improves success from 58% to 89% within four attempts without parameter updates. These results demonstrate a scalable path toward embodied intelligence that learns from human experience and continuously improves through its own interaction.

</details>


### [74] [Tactile-JEPA: Topology-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Distributed Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24385)
*Elizaveta Kovtun,Matvey Konovalov,Andrey Sakhovskiy,Semen Budennyy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tactile-JEPA 是一种面向分布式电子皮肤的自监督预训练方法，利用传感器空间连接图进行掩码建模，学习拓扑感知的触觉表征，并在力估计、手内姿态估计和策略学习等任务上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人接触丰富灵巧操作尤其在视觉遮挡下依赖触觉；图像编码器通常预训练，但触觉编码器多从原始噪声信号从头训练，表征能力受限。现有触觉自监督学习主要针对视触觉传感器，未充分覆盖稀疏且不规则排布的分布式电子皮肤，直接套用视觉自监督方法效果欠佳。

Method: 提出 Tactile-JEPA：利用触觉传感元件的空间排布与传感器连接图引导空间掩码，通过从未掩码部分预测被掩码传感元件的嵌入，学习拓扑感知表征；采用双尺度掩码以同时捕捉局部接触细节与全局触觉表面状态。在磁性与压阻传感器、不同机器人本体、单/双传感器配置的三个数据集上验证。

Result: 相较先前最优方法，力估计误差降低 6.3%，手内朝向误差降低 20.8%，并在策略学习等其他下游应用中取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 触觉感知的收益关键取决于编码器预训练质量，Tactile-JEPA 直接针对该问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is an essential modality for robots performing contact-rich, dexterous manipulation, particularly under visual occlusion. While pre-trained image encoders are standard in robot learning pipelines, tactile encoders are still commonly trained from scratch from raw, noisy signals, which might limit their expressivity. Existing self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches focus predominantly on vision-based tactile sensors, leaving distributed electronic skins largely unaddressed. These sensors, however, have a distinctive property: their sensing elements are sparse and irregularly arranged over the surface they cover, which makes direct reuse of visual SSL methods suboptimal. We present Tactile-JEPA, an efficient self-supervised pre-training method that uses the spatial arrangement of tactile sensors to learn topology-aware representations. Specifically, it is trained to predict the embeddings of masked sensing elements from the unmasked remainder, using the sensor connectivity graph to guide spatial masking. Our analysis shows that effective tactile representations require capturing both local contact details and the global state of the tactile surface, which we achieve through dual-scale masking. Across three diverse datasets spanning magnetic and piezoresistive sensors, different robot embodiments, and single- and paired-sensor configurations, Tactile-JEPA reduces force estimation error by 6.3% and in-hand orientation error by 20.8% over the prior state-of-the-art, with consistent gains in other downstream applications, including policy learning. Overall, our results demonstrate that the benefit of tactile sensing depends critically on the quality of encoder pre-training, a problem which Tactile-JEPA addresses directly. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Multi-Agent Transportation of Free-Flyers in Microgravity Via Pushing Interaction Under Human-in-the-Loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24376)
*Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Sihyun Cho,Youngkyoung Kong,Elias Krantz,Mani Hemanth Dhullipalla,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种微重力下多追机器人协同运输被动目标的安全关键框架，利用单侧推挤接触在间歇驱动与配置相关约束下实现期望旋量跟踪与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力中自由飞行器协同运输需兼顾目标稳定与安全，但仅靠单侧推挤会导致稀疏且依赖构型的驱动约束；当期望推力分配变化时，追机器人必须重新定位，控制权威间歇，现有方法难以同时保证跟踪、稳定与安全。

Method: 构建延迟感知反馈控制架构，将控制李雅普诺夫函数（CLF）用于稳定性约束、控制屏障函数（CBF）用于安全性约束，并嵌入混合整数推力分配程序，以处理单侧推挤、离散重定位和间歇控制权限。

Result: Gazebo仿真验证了该框架：在控制权限间歇条件下仍能跟踪人类给定的期望旋量，并保证对圆形障碍物的避障。

Conclusion: 该工作为空间环境中人类监督下的自由飞行器协同运输提供了基础，表明CLF/CBF与混合整数推力分配相结合可有效处理推挤式协同运输的安全关键控制问题。

Abstract: We propose a safety-critical framework for the cooperative transportation of passive targets in microgravity, where a team of chaser robots acts through unilateral pushing contacts to track a human-provided desired twist while ensuring safe target motion. The pushing-only nature of the interaction introduces sparse, configuration-dependent actuation constraints requiring chasers to physically relocate on the target body when the desired pushing allocation changes. To address these challenges, we formulate a delay-aware feedback control architecture leveraging Control Lyapunov Function (CLF) and Control Barrier Function (CBF) constraints within a mixed-integer thrust allocation program to enforce stability and safety of the target, respectively. The proposed framework enables reference tracking while guaranteeing obstacle avoidance with a circular obstacle despite intermittent control authority, providing a foundation for human-supervised cooperative transportation of free-flyers in space environments. The proposed framework is validated through Gazebo simulations.

</details>


### [76] [LIBERO-VPro: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Visual Robustness of Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24350)
*Huiqiong Li,Zhiting Mei,Anirudha Majumdar,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-VPro 基准，系统评估机器人基础模型在闭环执行中面对视觉扰动时的鲁棒性，发现标称性能会掩盖视觉 grounding 与适应能力的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型在标准操作基准上表现优异，但评估通常假设视觉观测干净、及时且一致，缺乏对执行过程中视觉证据被扰动时闭环鲁棒性的系统诊断。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-VPro，通过扰动执行中的视觉证据评估闭环视觉鲁棒性，涵盖视觉证据退化、相机陈旧、视觉源一致性和任务相关场景变化四个维度，包含 12 类挑战、96 种实验设置、3296 个任务条件案例；评估 3 个 VLA 和 3 个 WAM，约 196,000 个仿真回合，并在 Franka Research 3 上补充 200 次真实世界 rollout。

Result: 强标称性能可掩盖视觉 grounding 与适应弱点：模型在严重物体遮挡下常仍成功，但局部交互线索被破坏或熟悉空间先验被违反时性能骤降；对陈旧或缺失观测高度敏感，任务前置条件变化需行为适应时表现困难；VLA 与 WAM 呈现不同鲁棒性剖面，表明视觉鲁棒性是多维且依赖架构的。

Conclusion: LIBERO-VPro 提供了系统诊断框架，有助于开发在挑战性视觉条件下更可靠地进行动作 grounding 与适应的机器人基础模型。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models achieve impressive performance on standard manipulation benchmarks, yet these evaluations typically assume clean, timely, and consistent visual observations throughout execution. We introduce LIBERO-VPro, a benchmark for systematically evaluating the closed-loop visual robustness of robotic foundation models by perturbing the visual evidence available during execution. LIBERO-VPro covers four complementary dimensions, including Visual Evidence Degradation, Camera Staleness, Visual Source Consistency, and Task-Relevant Scene Variation, spanning 12 challenge categories, 96 experimental settings, and 3,296 task-condition cases. We evaluate three vision-language-action models and three world-action models over approximately 196,000 simulated episodes, complemented by 200 real-world rollouts on a Franka Research 3. Our results reveal that strong nominal performance can mask substantial weaknesses in visual grounding and adaptation. Models often remain successful despite severe object-level occlusion, yet degrade sharply when local interaction cues are disrupted or familiar spatial priors are violated. They are also highly sensitive to stale or missing observations and struggle when changed task preconditions require behavioral adaptation. Finally, VLAs and WAMs exhibit distinct robustness profiles, showing that visual robustness is multi-dimensional and architecture-dependent. LIBERO-VPro provides a systematic diagnostic framework for developing robotic foundation models that can more reliably ground and adapt their actions under challenging visual conditions.

</details>


### [77] [Performance-Preserving Online Adaptation in Social Navigation via Diffusion Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24317)
*Haruto Nagahisa,Kohei Matsumoto,Yuki Hyodo,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对社交导航中仿真到部署环境的差距，提出DSRL：固定扩散策略、仅用强化学习训练噪声策略，并融合多随机种子训练的扩散RL策略构建基础策略，实现保留性能的高效微调与社交规范适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航中人与机器人交互复杂，深度强化学习虽被广泛研究，但仿真难以完全复现多样场景、机器人动力学及跨部署环境变化的社交规范，因此需在部署环境中微调；同时微调必须保留基础模型性能，避免损害避让行人和到达目的地等导航主目标。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的扩散引导（DSRL）：在微调时固定扩散策略，仅训练噪声策略，以实现性能保持的学习；并通过集成多个不同随机种子训练的基于扩散的RL策略来构建基础策略，以提升学习性能。

Result: 评估表明，与其他方法相比，所提方法能够在保持性能的同时实现高效学习，并可通过适应社交规范实现灵活的行为控制；硬件在环仿真验证了其在物理机器人上的有效性。

Conclusion: DSRL为社交导航提供了一种在部署环境中保留基础策略性能并高效适应社交规范的有效微调方案，且具备向真实机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: In social navigation, modeling the complex interactions between humans and robots is difficult, and deep reinforcement learning has therefore been actively studied. However, because simulation alone cannot fully reproduce diverse scenarios, robot dynamics, and the social conventions that vary across deployment environments, fine-tuning in the deployment environment is promising. In doing so, learning that preserves the base model's performance is required, so as not to compromise the primary objective of navigation, namely avoiding pedestrians and reaching the destination. In this study, we propose a method that applies diffusion steering via reinforcement learning (DSRL), which trains only the noise policy while keeping the diffusion policy fixed, thereby achieving learning that preserves performance. Furthermore, we integrate diffusion-based RL policies trained with multiple seeds to construct the base policy, improving learning performance. Our evaluation shows that, compared with other methods, the proposed method enables efficient learning while preserving performance, and we confirm flexible behavior control through adaptation to social conventions, as well as its effectiveness on a physical robot through hardware-in-the-loop simulation.

</details>


### [78] [vla.simd: Efficient CPU Inference for Language-Conditioned Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24274)
*Khanh D. Nguyen,Hoang M. Truong,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向语言条件操作策略的CPU推理引擎，并给出基于ACT的IMPACT策略；通过SIMD微内核、计算复用和针对性优化，在无GPU情况下实现高效动作供给与多机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无专用GPU部署语言条件操作需要高效推理，同时动作块必须覆盖策略查询之间的延迟；因此需研究查询延迟/执行时域与动作可用性的关系，并区分动作供给与反馈频率。

Method: 提出一个CPU推理引擎，融合共享SIMD微内核、可复用计算与目标特定优化；分析滞后执行与时间对齐执行下的动作可用性。提出IMPACT：基于ACT，缓存文本表示并引入语言调制的视觉特征。在6种策略、4种CPU上评估，并在GPU上对LIBERO套件评估，另在SO-101、UR10e+Robotiq夹爪上做部署试验。

Result: 相比编译PyTorch参考实现，中位加速约1.4倍且保持fp32数值保真。IMPACT在Raspberry Pi 5上经90秒热稳定后，fp32供给33.5 actions/s，int8为81.2 actions/s，是评估集中唯一在Pi 5上达到至少30 actions/s的语言条件策略。GPU评估在四个LIBERO套件上平均成功率76.4%，无需机器人预训练；指令打乱测试表明能在熟悉目标中选择。IMPACT与SmolVLA分别在SO-101和UR10e上验证了CPU部署。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过CPU优化推理引擎与IMPACT策略，可在无GPU条件下实现语言条件操作策略的实时动作供给，并跨两种机器人本体部署。

Abstract: Deploying language-conditioned manipulation without a dedicated GPU requires efficient inference and action chunks that cover the delay between policy queries. We present this http URL , a CPU inference engine that combines shared SIMD micro-kernels, reusable computation, and target-specific optimization. We relate query latency and execution horizon to action availability under lagged and time-aligned execution, distinguishing action supply from feedback frequency. Across six policies and four CPUs, this http URL achieves approximately $1.4\times$ median speedup over compiled PyTorch references while preserving fp32 numerical fidelity. We also introduce IMPACT, an ACT-based policy with cached text representations and language-modulated visual features. IMPACT is the only language-conditioned policy in our evaluated set that supplies at least 30 actions/s on the Raspberry Pi 5: after a 90 s thermal soak, it supplies 33.5 actions/s in fp32 and 81.2 with int8. Separate GPU evaluations yield $76.4\%$ mean success across four LIBERO suites without robot pretraining; instruction-shuffling tests demonstrate selection among familiar goals. Trials with IMPACT on an SO-101 arm and SmolVLA on a UR10e with a Robotiq gripper demonstrate CPU deployment on two robot embodiments.

</details>


### [79] [ME-Brain-1.0: Memory, Cognition and Action for Evolving Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24271)
*Wei He,Hengtao Li,Zhongrui Yu,Xuhan Zhu,Maokui He,Zide Liu,Xiyue Zhang,Xianwei Mao,Chunpeng Zhou,Jia Shi,Yanze Xin,Jingwen Li,Jingxie Zheng,Sijie Zeng,Chenfeng Wang,Fan Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Shuai Guo,Hengxuan Zhang,Pengfei Yu,Jia Shi,Yu Liu,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MachEmbodied-Brain（ME-Brain），一种围绕“动作执行—经验获取—经验演化—改进执行”闭环的自演化具身系统，无需模型重训练即可从物理交互中持续学习并提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身系统主要依赖部署后固定的预训练能力，难以从物理交互中学习，限制了持续适应和技能迁移能力。

Method: ME-Brain 由三部分构成：Evolvable Memory 将多模态轨迹固化为分层、可复用经验；Cognitive Core 将物理经验转化为可迁移技能；Action Model 结合事件驱动关键帧、EventCell 局部世界预测和动作条件记忆调制，将计算聚焦于决策关键的时刻、区域与历史证据。整体实现从 train-and-freeze 到 deploy-and-evolve 的转变。

Result: Cognitive Core 在具身与智能体基准上分别超过最强对比模型 8.2 和 9.6 分。Action Model 在 RoboMME 上达到 47.88% 平均成功率，比最强基线提升 3.26 分。在 RoboDojo 上达到 21.51 平均 Score 和 16.03% 成功率，分别超过 π0.5 10.10 和 9.12 分。在六任务 ME-RealBench 上，ME-Brain 达到 69.5 平均 Score 和 66.7% 成功率，分别超过 DM0.5 12.8 和 11.7 分。

Conclusion: ME-Brain 通过可演化记忆、认知核心和高效动作模型，实现了部署后自演化具身智能，在多个具身与智能体基准上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Current embodied systems largely rely on pretrained capabilities that remain fixed after deployment, limiting their ability to learn from physical interaction. We introduce MachEmbodied-Brain (ME-Brain), a self-evolving embodied system organized around a closed loop of action execution, experience acquisition, experience evolution, and improved execution. Evolvable Memory consolidates multimodal trajectories into hierarchical, reusable experience; Cognitive Core transforms physical experience into transferable skills; and the Action Model combines event-driven keyframes, EventCell local-world prediction, and action-conditioned memory modulation to focus computation on decision-critical moments, regions, and historical evidence. Together, these modules shift embodied intelligence from train-and-freeze to deploy-and-evolve without model retraining. Cognitive Core outperforms the strongest comparison models by 8.2 and 9.6 points on embodied and agent benchmarks. The Action Model achieves 47.88% mean success on RoboMME, a 3.26-point improvement over the strongest baseline. On RoboDojo, it reaches a 21.51 mean Score and 16.03% success rate, exceeding $\pi_{0.5}$ by 10.10 and 9.12 points. On the six-task ME-RealBench, ME-Brain achieves a 69.5 mean Score and 66.7% success rate, outperforming DM0.5 by 12.8 and 11.7 points, respectively.

</details>


### [80] [OpenFlyScan: A Quality-Guided Aerial Reconstruction System for Consumer Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24253)
*Zhongrui You,Zhen Li,Junli Liu,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenFlyScan 是一个面向消费级无人机的质量引导航拍重建系统，通过预测 3DGS 重建质量并规划针对性重采集，实现快速低成本的城市资产创建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 可为大规模具身仿真提供高保真场景，但大规模城市资产构建受限于昂贵设备和滞后的质量反馈；预设航测常导致复杂表面观测不足，缺陷需在重建后才发现，造成返工与重复处理。

Method: 提出 OpenFlyScan，集成 GS 质量模型、重采集规划器和定制移动 App。质量模型从 GS 渲染误差中学习，预测区域重建质量；规划器据此生成互补的重采集条带，并通过 App 执行。App 还支持自动倾斜摄影和数据传输，无需额外机载硬件。

Result: 在真实航拍场景中，模型能有效识别可能重建较差的区域；在 Expo West 现场实验中，针对性重采集使附加视角的 PSNR 提升 10.95 dB。

Conclusion: OpenFlyScan 利用消费级无人机整合采集、针对性重采集与重建，支持快速、低成本的城市资产创建，代码与模型将公开。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides high-fidelity scenes for large-scale embodied simulation, but constructing large-scale urban assets remains constrained by expensive equipment and delayed quality feedback. Preset surveys can leave complex surfaces insufficiently observed, with defects discovered only after reconstruction, requiring return visits and repeated processing. We present OpenFlyScan, a quality-guided aerial reconstruction system for consumer drones that integrates a GS quality model, a reacquisition planner, and a custom-designed mobile app. The model learns from GS rendering errors to predict regional reconstruction quality. Based on these predictions, the planner then generates complementary reacquisition strips to be executed through the app, which also supports automated oblique surveys and data transfer without additional hardware on board. Across real aerial scenes, the model effectively identifies regions that are likely to be poorly reconstructed. In the Expo West field experiment, targeted reacquisition improves PSNR at additional views by 10.95 dB. With consumer drones, OpenFlyScan integrates capture, targeted reacquisition, and reconstruction to support rapid, low-cost urban asset creation. Code and models will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [81] [Audio-based UAV Localization with Adaptive Temporal Correspondence via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24218)
*Haoxiang Lei,Mingzheng Feng,Daotong Wang,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向反无人机预警的自适应时间对应音频定位框架：通过探测片段生成声学状态，并用强化学习控制器动态选择音频窗口，再结合Mamba网络进行三维定位；在保持竞争性精度的同时显著降低时间对应延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于音频的定位方法通常依赖预定义固定音频段长度，限制了时间对应能力，并在充足声学证据与及时定位之间形成权衡。

Method: 首先使用探测片段提取紧凑声学状态，以表征观测的可靠性与一致性；在该状态引导下，利用强化学习控制器为每次定位决策动态确定所需音频窗口大小；随后将选定音频段输入基于Mamba的定位网络，并通过自适应时间特征调制完成三维位置估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，与SOTA方法相比，该方法在取得有竞争力的三维定位精度的同时，显著降低了时间对应延迟，并在不同场景下表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应时间对应与动态音频窗口选择，缓解了固定音频段长导致的精度与实时性矛盾，为音频反无人机定位提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Audio-based localization provides a low-cost and illumination-independent sensing solution for anti-UAV early warning. However, existing methods typically rely on a predefined fixed audio segment length, which limits temporal correspondence and creates a trade-off between sufficient acoustic evidence and timely localization. To address this issue, we propose an audio-based localization framework with adaptive temporal correspondence. A probe segment is first used to extract a compact acoustic state that characterizes the reliability and consistency of the observation. Guided by the state, a reinforcement learning controller dynamically determines the required audio window size for each localization decision. The selected audio segment is then processed by a Mamba-based localization network with adaptive temporal feature modulation for 3D position estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive 3D localization accuracy with substantially reduced temporal correspondence latency compared to SOTA methods and exhibits strong generalization across scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [Odometry-Aided Real-Time Mapping for Underwater Robots Using Forward-Looking Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24195)
*Siyuan Du,Kanzhong Yao,Youdong Wang,Yingqi Liu,Qingwen Liu,Qunhui Yang,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向水下前视声呐的级联特征重建与2.5D建图流水线，在低可见度条件下实现低延迟、厘米级局部地图构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下光学感知受光衰减和散射严重退化；前视声呐可在恶劣光学条件下提供高帧率声学成像，但实时FLS建图仍面临目标高度不可解析、空间非均匀噪声、目标边界破碎等问题，导致特征提取困难并在投影中引入几何歧义。

Method: 采用级联特征重建流程：基于FFT的去噪、快速多尺度恒虚警率检测、梯度自适应边界连接；随后结合姿态感知几何投影与增量占据累积，构建深度参考的2.5D局部地图。声呐垂直位置参考外部传感器，目标高度通过显式几何假设赋值，而非由FLS直接测量。

Result: 在3 m×5 m水池实验中，三条序列的平面建图RMSE低于3 cm，平均每帧处理时间为42.4 ms。

Conclusion: 该方法可在受限水下环境中实现低延迟、厘米级精度的局部2.5D声呐建图，但目标高度依赖显式几何假设和外部垂直位置参考，并非FLS直接测量。

Abstract: Reliable perception is essential for underwater vehicles operating in complex environments, where light attenuation and scattering often degrade visibility and compromise optical sensing. Forward-looking sonar (FLS) offers an alternative by providing high-frame-rate acoustic imaging under poor optical conditions. However, real-time FLS mapping remains challenging due to unresolved target elevation, spatially non-uniform noise, and fragmented target boundaries, which hinder feature extraction and introduce geometric ambiguity during projection. To address these challenges, we propose a cascaded feature reconstruction pipeline combining fast Fourier transform (FFT)-based denoising, fast multiscale constant false alarm rate (MCFAR) detection, and gradient-adaptive boundary connection to extract geometric features from degraded sonar images with low latency. We integrate attitude-aware geometric projection with incremental occupancy accumulation to construct a depth-referenced 2.5D map for local mapping in confined underwater environments. The sonar's vertical position is referenced to an external sensor, while target elevation is assigned under an explicit geometric assumption rather than measured directly by FLS. Experiments in a 3 m X 5 m pool demonstrate centimeter-scale planar mapping accuracy, with a root-mean-square error (RMSE) below 3 cm across three sequences and an average processing time of 42.4 ms per frame.

</details>


### [83] [A Topological Representation with Object-Path Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Instance Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24189)
*Linwei Zheng,Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Haoang Li,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对象-路径图（object-path graph），将开放词汇语义推理与拓扑导航统一到轻量级拓扑框架中，并基于该图设计全局路径规划与局部节点间执行相结合的导航策略，无需稠密度量重建即可直接在图上游走，在HM3D、Replica及真实机器人上验证了开放词汇目标定位与导航的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法多将导航分解为顺序的语言引导决策，或依赖无先验环境知识的在线探索；场景图虽提供紧凑语义记忆，但与下游导航解耦，仍需稠密度量地图支撑。

Method: 构建对象-路径图，联合支持语义grounding、基于图的定位与导航；在此图上提出导航策略：全局路径规划结合局部节点间执行，通过轻量级节点定位与语义视觉伺服实现直接基于图的导航，避免稠密度量重建。

Result: 在HM3D和Replica上，所提分层图结构在开放词汇目标grounding上取得有竞争力的性能，并实现有效导航；真实机器人实验进一步验证框架的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将开放词汇语义与拓扑导航统一到对象-路径图中，可在无需稠密度量地图的情况下实现有效视觉语言导航，并为真实机器人部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires embodied agents to navigate environments using natural language instructions and visual observations. Existing approaches typically decompose navigation into sequential language-guided decisions or rely on online exploration without prior environmental knowledge. Scene graph representations offer compact semantic memory but remain decoupled from downstream navigation, which still depends on dense metric maps. To close this gap, we propose an object--path graph that unifies open-vocabulary semantic reasoning with topological navigation. The proposed representation jointly supports semantic grounding, graph-based localization, and navigation within a single lightweight topological framework. Building on this graph, we introduce a navigation strategy that combines global path planning with local inter-node execution through lightweight node localization and semantic visual servoing, enabling navigation directly over the graph without dense metric reconstruction. Experiments on HM3D and Replica demonstrate competitive performance in open-vocabulary object grounding through the proposed hierarchical graph structure, while achieving effective navigation performance. Real-world robot experiments further validate the practicality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [84] [StenoVLA-3D: 3D-Aware Reasoning VLA for Navigation Through Gastrointestinal Stenoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24187)
*Tamima Tabassum,Yiming Huang,Tianchun Wu,Changjing Liu,Zhiqing Tang,Chikit Ng,Beilei Cui,Liangjing Shao,Jiewen Lai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StenoVLA-3D：一种面向狭窄区域自主内镜导航的 3D 感知 VLA 框架，通过点图与几何门控融合增强几何基础，并支持病灶报告与穿越进度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主内镜导航需在纹理稀疏的单目观测下预测动作、做出安全控制决策，并在病灶离开视野后仍保留其证据。现有 VLA 主要依赖视觉外观与短期上下文，限制了几何基础与 episode 级报告能力。

Method: 将点图（point-map）通过可学习的几何门控融合集成到 Cosmos-Reason 2 主干；提出时序状态分支建模穿越进度；推理-动作主干输出 grounded reasoning 与动作；专用头估计狭窄形状并生成最终病灶报告。同时构建 EndoCausal episode 级数据集，包含病灶标注、动作与时间 grounded reasoning。

Result: 在 40 个留出录制测试 episode 上，语义准确率 95.2%，动作准确率 83.4%。在物理 3-DoF 内镜上，食管与结肠体模任务成功率分别为 88.9% 和 77.8%（各 36 次试验），显著优于所评估基线。

Conclusion: StenoVLA-3D 通过 3D 几何感知、时序状态建模与推理-动作联合预测，提升了内镜狭窄区域自主导航的安全性、动作准确性与 episode 级病灶报告能力，并在体模实验中大幅超越基线。

Abstract: Autonomous endoscopic navigation requires the policy model to predict actions from texture-poor monocular observations, make safe control decisions, and retain evidence of lesions after they leave the field of view. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) models primarily rely on visual appearance and short-term context, limiting geometric grounding and episode-level reporting. We introduce StenoVLA-3D, a 3D-aware VLA framework for navigating through stenotic regions. We integrate point-maps into the Cosmos-Reason 2 backbone through learned geometry-gated fusion, and also propose a temporal state branch to model traversal progress. Our reasoning-and-action backbone predicts grounded reasoning with actions, while dedicated heads estimate stenosis shape and generate the final lesion report. We further introduce EndoCausal, an episode-level dataset with lesion annotations, actions, and temporally grounded reasoning. On 40 held-out recorded test episodes, StenoVLA-3D reaches 95.2\% semantic accuracy and 83.4\% action accuracy. On the physical 3-DoF endoscope, it attains 88.9\% and 77.8\% task success in esophageal and colonic phantoms (36 trials each), substantially outperforming the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [85] [GraspTune: Tactile-Driven Execution Refinement for Robust Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24180)
*Juntao Li,Xingke Xia,Sichao Liu,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspTune 是一种触觉驱动的执行阶段细化框架，从视觉抓取提案出发，通过有界残差 TCP 运动与接触语义学习，将视觉上合理的抓取提案转化为稳定的物理抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉抓取提案生成发展迅速，但将选定的提案转化为稳定的物理抓取仍是执行阶段的核心挑战；需要提升接近、接触形成与最终执行中的稳定性，并实现无需真实世界策略微调的迁移。

Method: GraspTune 从名义提案开始，在接近、接触形成和最终抓取执行中施加有界残差 TCP 运动。它从局部深度、触觉信号、状态和历史中学习面向控制的接触语义，采用状态条件专家接触查询以及接触变化、接触风险和闭合后就绪度的多任务监督。所得表示用于条件化扩散预训练残差策略，并与 PPO 对齐以进行闭环执行。

Result: 在超过 60,000 次仿真执行、20 个物体类别和四种提案生成器上，GraspTune 将稳定抓取成功率分别提升：GraspNet +19.22、Contact-GraspNet +9.55、AnyGrasp +12.45、VGN +20.70 个百分点。四折留出类别研究中，未见物体执行成功率从 54.58% 提升至 70.33%，显示类别不相交的接触修正泛化能力。在 UR5e 与 Xense 指尖传感器的超过 1,000 次真实机器人试验中，GraspNet 执行成功率从 71.0% 提升至 84.3%，验证了无需真实世界策略微调的直接迁移。

Conclusion: 这些结果将视觉上合理的提案转化为稳定的物理抓取，有助于下游接触丰富的操作任务。

Abstract: Visual grasp proposal generation has advanced rapidly, yet converting a selected proposal into a stable physical grasp remains a central execution-stage challenge. This paper introduces GraspTune, a tactile-driven execution-stage refinement framework that starts from a nominal proposal and applies bounded residual TCP motions during approach, contact formation, and final grasp execution. GraspTune learns control-facing contact semantics from local depth, tactile signals, state, and history using state-conditioned expert contact queries and multi-task supervision for contact change, contact risk, and post-close readiness. The representation conditions a diffusion-pretrained residual policy and is aligned with PPO for closed-loop execution. Across more than 60,000 simulated executions over 20 object categories, GraspTune establishes an execution-layer benefit across four proposal generators, raising stable grasp success by +19.22, +9.55, +12.45, and +20.70 percentage points for GraspNet, Contact-GraspNet, AnyGrasp, and VGN. A four-fold held-out category study raises unseen-object execution from 54.58% to 70.33%, showing category-disjoint generalization of contact correction. Across more than 1,000 real-robot trials on a UR5e setup with Xense fingertip sensors, GraspTune raises GraspNet execution from 71.0% to 84.3%, validating direct transfer without realworld policy fine-tuning. Together, these results turn visually plausible proposals into stable physical grasps for downstream contact-rich manipulation. A supplementary video is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [Object-Centric Conditioning for Visuomotor Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24155)
*Jijie Li,Xu Yang,Junhong Zou,Chunhai Zhao,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SlotFlow——以对象为中心的流匹配策略，通过解耦语义与空间线索，在视觉干扰与空间分布外偏移下提升视觉运动操作鲁棒性，并保持A2A的低步数推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有A2A流匹配虽以历史动作先验替代随机噪声来提升推理效率，但过时的历史运动模式与纠缠的全局视觉表征会共同削弱空间OOD偏移和视觉干扰下的鲁棒性。

Method: SlotFlow将场景观测解耦为语义（what）特征与轻量级图像平面空间（where）线索：前者抑制无关背景关联并提供对象感知的策略条件，后者用于当前状态锚定并提升对物体配置偏移的适应。

Result: 仿真与真实实验显示，在视觉干扰物和严重空间扰动下鲁棒性提升，同时保持A2A的低步数推理效率；控制初始化与感知消融表明，以对象为中心的锚定是主要增益来源，并与有用的历史运动先验互补而非替代。

Conclusion: 以对象为中心的流匹配策略能有效增强视觉运动操作的鲁棒性，且不牺牲高效推理，并可与历史动作先验协同使用。

Abstract: Robot visuomotor policies are commonly formulated as autoregressive, diffusion-based, or more recently, flow matching models. Among them, Action-to-Action (A2A) flow matching improves inference efficiency by initializing generation from historical action priors rather than stochastic noise. However, stale historical motion patterns and entangled global visual representations can jointly reduce robustness under spatial out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts and visual distractors. In this work, we propose SlotFlow, an object-centric flow matching policy for robust visuomotor manipulation. SlotFlow decouples scene observations into semantic ("what") features and lightweight image-plane spatial ("where") cues to provide object-aware policy conditioning and current-state grounding. The semantic representation suppresses irrelevant background correlations, while the spatial cue improves adaptation to shifted object configurations. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved robustness under visual distractors and severe spatial perturbations while preserving the low-step inference efficiency of A2A. Controlled initialization and perception ablations further identify object-centric grounding as a major source of the gains and show that it complements, rather than replaces, useful historical motion priors.

</details>


### [87] [MimicAgent: Quadruped Skills via Prompt-to-Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24145)
*Lucky Kant Nayak,Narayanan Palghat Parameswaran,Neehar Peri,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicAgent 是一个 prompt-to-trajectory 生成框架：给定技能提示，用 coding agent 生成四足参考轨迹，再通过 example-guided RL 训练可在仿真和真实世界部署的动态四足技能；使用 Claude Fable 5.1 时 87% 的提示生成语义对齐的参考轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足策略训练广泛依赖 reward shaping，但奖励景观难以导航，常需大量人工试错；Eureka 虽用 LLM 自动设计奖励，却难以泛化到多样技能和形态。作者观察到，人类和 LLM 生成参考运动比设计奖励函数更容易。该假设受 humanoid 中基于大规模动捕数据的 example-guided RL 启发，但四足缺乏此类参考运动数据。

Method: 提出 MimicAgent，一个 agentic harness：输入技能 prompt，利用 coding agents 生成四足参考轨迹；这些粗粒度参考轨迹随后用于训练 example-guided RL 策略，并可在仿真和真实世界中部署。

Result: 在 agentic harness 中提示 Claude Fable 5.1 时，87% 的 prompt 产生语义对齐的参考轨迹。

Conclusion: MimicAgent 通过自动生成四足参考轨迹来缓解奖励设计难题，为四足 example-guided RL 和真实世界动态技能学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present MimicAgent, a prompt-to-trajectory generation framework for learning dynamic quadruped skills. Although reward shaping is extensively used when training quadruped policies, navigating the resulting reward landscape is notoriously difficult, requiring hours of "graduate student descent". Eureka attempts to automate reward design with LLMs, but we find that it struggles to generalize across diverse skills and morphologies. Our key observation is that it is far easier for a human - and by association, an LLM - to generate reference motions than to shape reward functions. Our hypothesis is motivated by the success of example-guided RL for humanoids, which exploits large-scale motion capture datasets as references for training locomotion policies. Unlike humanoids, quadrupeds lack such reference motion data. Towards this end, we propose MimicAgent, an agentic harness that, given a skill prompt, generates quadruped reference trajectories with coding agents. These coarse reference trajectories are then used to train example-guided RL policies that are deployable in simulation and in the real-world. Notably, we find that when prompting Claude Fable 5.1 within our agentic harness, 87% of prompts yield semantically aligned reference trajectories.

</details>


### [88] [BayesianGS-SLAM: Uncertainty-Aware Neural Rendering SLAM via Probabilistic Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24140)
*Kyeongsu Kang,Seongbo Ha,Sibaek Lee,Hyeonwoo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BayesianGS-SLAM，一种不确定性感知的3D高斯泼溅SLAM框架，在映射中估计颜色与深度预测不确定性，并将其一致地用于映射、跟踪和关键帧选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经渲染SLAM依赖渲染RGB-D残差进行相机跟踪与地图优化，但传感器噪声、观测覆盖不足和地图表示不完整会使预测可靠性变化显著；缺乏显式可靠性估计时，不可靠残差会损害位姿优化，而当前地图已充分解释的帧又会触发冗余建图更新。

Method: 构建可处理概率公式，将传感器噪声不确定性与不透明度引起的地图表示不确定性结合，并通过渲染过程传播，得到颜色和深度的预测不确定性；该不确定性用于增强建图、通过鲁棒位姿目标归一化跟踪残差，并基于预测惊奇度设计关键帧准则。

Result: 在真实RGB-D数据集上，深度不确定性与误差排序相比现有不确定性感知SLAM方法显著改善；所提关键帧选择策略在保持有竞争力的跟踪与渲染性能的同时，减少了关键帧数量与建图调用次数。

Conclusion: BayesianGS-SLAM表明，对颜色和深度同时估计预测不确定性并贯穿建图、跟踪和关键帧选择，可提升神经渲染SLAM的可靠性并降低冗余计算。 

Abstract: Neural-rendering-based SLAM relies on rendered RGB-D residuals for camera tracking and map optimization, but the reliability of these predictions can vary substantially because of sensor noise, limited observation coverage, and incomplete map representations. Without an explicit reliability estimate, unreliable residuals may adversely affect pose optimization, while frames already well explained by the current map may trigger redundant mapping updates. In this paper, we present BayesianGS-SLAM, an uncertainty-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM framework that estimates predictive color and depth uncertainty during mapping and consistently reuses it across the SLAM pipeline. Our tractable probabilistic formulation combines a sensor-noise uncertainty component with an opacity-induced map-representation component propagated through the rendering process. The resulting predictive uncertainty is used to augment mapping, normalize tracking residuals through a robust pose objective, and evaluate incoming frames using a predictive-surprise-based keyframe criterion. Unlike prior uncertainty-aware neural-rendering SLAM methods that primarily consider color uncertainty or use uncertainty only during mapping, our framework estimates predictive uncertainty for both color and depth and integrates it into mapping, tracking, and keyframe selection. Evaluations on real-world RGB-D datasets demonstrate substantially improved depth uncertainty-error ranking compared with existing uncertainty-aware SLAM methods. Moreover, the proposed keyframe-selection strategy reduces the number of selected keyframes and mapping calls while maintaining competitive tracking and rendering performance.

</details>


### [89] [Phrase-Level Robotic Guqin Performance: Bimanual Motion Planning and Audio-Tactile Interaction Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24133)
*Zhen Wang,Zhiheng Chen,Tianyuan Bao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种物理异构双臂机器人系统，将古琴演奏建模为离散-连续混合执行问题，并通过分层规划实现乐句级自主演奏，在25事件乐句上取得93.6%和96.8%的事件正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人与具身智能虽已能完成复杂操作，但乐器演奏仍是高难度基准：不仅要求无碰撞轨迹，还要求精确接触时序、非对称双臂协调以及在真实乐器上达成目标声学效果。古琴作为七弦无品拨弦乐器，具有毫米级弦距、右手瞬态拨弦和左手持续泛音按触等独特挑战，亟需专用操作与规划方法。

Method: 构建物理异构双臂机器人系统，将古琴演奏形式化为混合离散-连续执行问题，并设计分层规划框架，协调工作手指分配、构型连续性、避障以及连续音乐事件间紧密的双臂接触调度；系统集成视觉引导的乐器定位、基于触觉的泛音接触监测，以及听觉反馈驱动的拨弦参数校准。

Result: 在真实古琴上针对25事件乐句开展实验，系统能够可靠执行协调的空弦与七徽泛音序列；重复试验中分别达到93.6%和96.8%的事件正确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架可支持乐句级自主古琴演奏，验证了在真实乐器上实现精确接触时序、双臂协调与目标声学结果的可行性，并为机器人乐器演奏提供了具身智能操作基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics and embodied intelligence have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex manipulation tasks. However, musical instrument performance remains a formidable benchmark, demanding not only collision-free trajectory execution but also precise contact timing, asymmetric bimanual coordination, and target acoustic outcomes on physical instruments. The guqin, a seven-string fretless zither, presents unique manipulation challenges due to its millimetric string spacing, transient right-hand plucking, and sustained left-hand harmonic contacts. In this work, we present a physical heterogeneous dual-arm robotic system for phrase-level autonomous guqin performance. We formulate guqin playing as a hybrid discrete--continuous execution problem and develop a hierarchical planning framework that coordinates working finger assignment, configuration continuity, obstacle avoidance, and tight bimanual contact schedules across consecutive musical events. The system integrates vision-guided instrument localization, tactile-based harmonic contact monitoring, and auditory feedback-informed plucking parameter calibration. Real-world experiments on a 25-event phrase demonstrate that the system reliably executes coordinated open-string and seventh-hui harmonic sequences on a physical guqin, achieving 93.6% and 96.8% event correctness across repeated trials.

</details>


### [90] [ActiveArena: Benchmarking and Understanding Active Perception in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24124)
*Yibo Li,Enshen Zhou,Rui Chen,Yanjun Ding,Mengzhen Liu,Yi Han,Jiabo Zhan,Lipeng Wang,Shanghang Zhang,Lu Sheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActiveArena 仿真与基准，用于评估主动感知与操作；包含35个任务、记忆标注和13种VLA配置，并揭示ID-OOD差距及提升OOD泛化的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以评估机器人如何主动获取并在记忆中维护信息，因此需要主动感知与操作的可控评测平台。

Method: 构建 ActiveArena-Sim（可控视角、大工作空间仿真器）；基于此提出 ActiveArena-Bench（5类35任务，需多轮证据获取与记忆推理，提供记忆标注、训练数据及ID/OOD协议）；并提出 ActiveArena-VLA（13种模块化VLA配置，用于控制研究记忆写入、容量、本体状态、子任务监督与高层规划）。

Result: 基准显示明显ID-OOD差距；均匀记忆采样、可靠写入策略下增大记忆容量、本体输入与子任务监督可提升OOD泛化；规划器引导的记忆管理与决策仅用稀疏记忆即可接近最佳配置性能。

Conclusion: ActiveArena为主动感知与操作模型提供统一测试床，支持开发与诊断。

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate how robots effectively acquire and maintain information in memory in an active manner. To this end, we introduce ActiveArena-Sim, an active-perception simulator with controllable viewpoints and large-scale workspaces as the foundation. Built on this, we propose ActiveArena-Bench, which comprises 35 tasks across 5 fine-grained categories, covering visual exploration and interactive information acquisition. Each task is difficult to solve from passive observations alone, requiring multi-round evidence acquisition and memory-based reasoning. The benchmark provides rich memory annotations, standardized training data, and ID/OOD protocols featuring disjoint scenes, unseen distractor configurations, and novel backgrounds. Moreover, we present ActiveArena-VLA, a modular suite of 13 vision-language-action configurations for controlled studies of memory writing, memory capacity, proprioceptive state, subtask supervision, and high-level planning in active perception. Benchmark results reveal a substantial ID-OOD gap: uniform memory sampling, increased memory capacity under reliable write policies, proprioceptive inputs, and subtask supervision improve OOD generalization, while planner-guided memory management and decision-making achieve performance close to the best-performing configuration using only sparse memory. ActiveArena thus provides a unified testbed to develop and diagnose models for active perception and manipulation.

</details>


### [91] [CARE: Experience-Guided Atomic Corrective Execution for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24118)
*Junlan Xiao,Junwei Jiang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Zhongbo Zhang,Huchuan Lu,Lijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过从失败回滚中学习阶段条件化偏差，合成代表性失败状态与纠正示范，并结合阶段规划与物理接地3D监控触发原子恢复，从而提升VLA策略鲁棒性；同时提出FSR-Bench基准，仿真平均任务成功率提升14.5点，真实世界提升15.9点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在机器人操作中性能强，但一旦执行偏离标称轨迹便表现脆弱；现有纠正数据多依赖人工设计或随机扰动，未能充分利用真实执行中遇到的失败。

Method: CARE收集失败rollout，建模阶段条件化的失败后偏差，并利用经验分布合成代表性失败状态与纠正示范；推理时结合阶段级规划与物理接地3D监控，触发原子调整或重操作，同时保持任务进度。进一步提出FSR-Bench，评估局部偏差与结构异常下的中间失败状态恢复。

Result: 在多种VLA主干、仿真基准及真实双臂任务中取得一致提升，平均任务成功率在仿真中提升14.5点，在真实世界中提升15.9点。

Conclusion: CARE通过失败驱动纠正学习与在线监控恢复，有效缓解VLA策略对执行偏差的脆弱性；FSR-Bench为失败状态恢复提供评估基准。代码、模型与数据已公开。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain brittle once execution deviates from nominal trajectories. We propose CARE (Corrective Atomic Robotic Execution), a framework that improves recovery by learning from failures encountered during execution. Instead of generating corrective data from manually designed or random perturbations, CARE collects failed rollouts, models stage-conditioned post-failure deviations, and uses the resulting empirical distributions to synthesize representative failure states and corrective demonstrations. At inference time, CARE combines stage-wise planning with physically grounded 3D monitoring to trigger atomic adjustments or re-operations while preserving task progress. We further introduce the Failure State Recovery Benchmark (FSR-Bench), which evaluates recovery from intermediate failure states under local deviations and structural anomalies. Experiments across multiple VLA backbones, simulation benchmarks, and real-world dual-arm tasks show consistent improvements, with average task-success gains of 14.5 points in simulation and 15.9 points in the real world. Code, models, and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [92] [Ask Before It Tells: Benchmark-to-Robot Body-Cue Transfer for a Question-First Bedside Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24099)
*Dongsik Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Nuni 床边机器人将身体线索识别视为触发询问而非直接报警的理由，并用姿态中心混合管线在机器人视角下优于 RGB 分类器，同时验证问询优先控制器的状态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身体线索识别可支持辅助机器人，但基准精度不能保证机器相机视角下的可靠行为；直接报警可能放大误判后果，因此需要交互策略来限制不确定感知的影响。

Method: 构建 Nuni 床边机器人原型。比较两个 X3D-UGT RGB 外观分类器（在 NTU RGB+D 上六类准确率为 97.7% 和 94.8%）与一个姿态中心混合管线，在 28 段机器人相机录制的单演员脚本片段上评估。检测到痛苦线索时触发询问而非报警。另通过事件注入测试问询优先控制器。

Result: 混合管线的六类宏召回为 0.71，微调与从头训练 RGB 变体分别为 0.25 和 0.29。混合管线在 16 段痛苦片段中 12 段产生触发询问的痛苦线索，在 8 段正常片段中 2 段会不必要询问；RGB 变体仅在 2/16 和 3/16 痛苦片段中产生触发询问线索。事件注入的 13 次状态转换试验全部通过：有效响应导致解除警报，两次未应答提示产生一次报警，三个边界条件被正确处理。

Conclusion: 这是初步技术评估，不是用户研究或医学验证；结果表明交互策略可限制不确定感知带来的后果。

Abstract: Body-cue recognition can support assistive robots, but benchmark accuracy does not guarantee reliable behavior under a robot-camera viewpoint. We present Nuni, a bedside robot prototype that treats a detected distress cue as a reason to ask rather than a reason to alert. We compare two X3D-UGT RGB appearance classifiers, which reach 97.7% and 94.8% six-way accuracy on NTU RGB+D, with a pose-centric hybrid pipeline on 28 single-actor scripted clips recorded from the robot camera. The hybrid path achieved 0.71 six-way macro recall, versus 0.25 and 0.29 for the fine-tuned and from-scratch RGB variants. More importantly for interaction, it produced a question-triggering distress cue in 12/16 distress clips and would have prompted unnecessarily in 2/8 normal clips; the RGB variants yielded a question-triggering cue in only 2/16 and 3/16 distress clips. We separately tested the question-first controller through event injection. All 13 state-transition trials passed: valid responses caused stand-down, two unanswered prompts produced one alert, and three boundary conditions were handled correctly. These results are a preliminary technical evaluation, not a user study or medical validation, but they show how interaction policy can limit the consequences of uncertain perception.

</details>


### [93] [Dexterous Robot Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Contact-Anchored Retargeting and Residual Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24093)
*Zihao Yang,Chengyuan Liu,Yu Zhou,Runze Lv,Tianyu Cui,Sheng Yi,Haohua Zhu,Irvine Lu,JieQ Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段流水线，将人类动作捕捉转换为灵巧机器人策略：物理细化恢复接触与力、接触锚定重定向传递接触结构、残差RL适配机器人驱动，无需真机训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示学习灵巧操作受数据瓶颈限制，尤其决定抓取成败的接触力在可扩展人类演示中缺失。关键观察是：跨手迁移应保留接触结构而非关节运动；物理一致性可由单一残差RL策略统一修复，而非逐任务工程。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1）用仿真MANO手进行物理细化，从运动学记录恢复接触与力；2）接触锚定重定向，以独立于手形态的目标转移演示的接触结构；3）残差策略学习，将结果适配到机器人驱动。同一残差公式也用于重定向后恢复动态可行性。

Result: 重建25,454条单手机迹，成功率从7.3%提升至59.3%；25个双手任务从16.0%提升至62.4%，每个设置共享一个策略；将一个人体数据集迁移到四种形态不同的机器人手，提升62.4个百分点；在物理硬件上执行四个接触丰富双手任务，零真机训练数据。

Conclusion: 接触结构是跨手形态可迁移的关键表示；物理细化、接触锚定重定向与残差RL可从人类演示生成灵巧机器人策略，无需真机数据，并可跨多种机器人手与真实硬件部署。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from demonstrations is bottlenecked by data: the contact forces that determine whether a grasp succeeds are absent from every scalable source of human demonstrations. This paper builds on two observations. First, what survives the change from a human hand to a robot hand is the contact structure of a demonstration - which finger regions touch which object locations, and in what order - rather than its joint motion. Second, physical consistency need not be engineered per task: a single residual reinforcement learning (RL) policy, trained once across diverse demonstrations, can repair kinematic recordings into physically consistent, contact-annotated trajectories, and the same residual formulation restores dynamic feasibility after retargeting. These observations yield a three-stage pipeline that converts human motion-capture recordings into dexterous robot policies with no real-robot training data: physics refinement with a simulated MANO hand recovers contacts and forces, contact-anchored retargeting transfers the demonstrated contact structure through an objective independent of hand morphology, and residual policy learning adapts the result to robot actuation. The pipeline reconstructs 25,454 single-hand trajectories (success 7.3% -> 59.3%) and 25 dual-hand tasks (16.0% -> 62.4%) with one shared policy per setting, transfers one human dataset to four morphologically distinct robot hands (+62.4 pp), and executes four contact-rich bimanual tasks on physical hardware with zero real-robot training data.

</details>


### [94] [When Does Touch Matter? Charting the Vision-Interaction Gap in Cluttered Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24068)
*Hao Jiang,Luis Dominguez,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对杂乱场景中的目标导向灵巧抓取进行受控真实世界研究，比较仅视觉、力/力矩、指尖触觉及组合策略；组合策略显著优于仅视觉，表明交互感知在遮挡和受限条件下至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中的灵巧抓取存在遮挡与接触不确定性，仅靠视觉几何难以判断抓取质量，因此需要系统评估触觉测量与外部力/力矩估计何时优于视觉信息。

Method: 在灵巧手系统上融合视觉、每指与腕部力/力矩估计及分布式指尖触觉单元；固定演示、视觉观测、动作空间和柔顺控制，在五种桌面场景条件下对比仅视觉、力/力矩、触觉和组合策略，并进行表征与融合基线及消融实验。

Result: 组合策略成功率为24/25，仅视觉为14/25；在三种受限条件下组合策略为15/15，仅视觉为6/15。消融显示力/力矩与触觉反馈互补；行为上交互反馈使系统更早拒绝不佳接触、举升前重抓并实现更稳定抓取。

Conclusion: 这是首个在真实世界中结合并分别评估这些交互模态用于杂乱场景目标导向灵巧抓取的研究；结果揭示视觉与交互感知之间的差距扩大，并将杂乱灵巧抓取定位为判断学习策略何时需要交互感知的基准。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in clutter poses a basic sensing question: when do tactile measurements and external wrench estimates improve on visual geometry? Occlusion and contact can obscure grasp quality, motivating a controlled evaluation of these interaction signals. We present a controlled real-world study over five tabletop scene conditions on a dexterous system that combines vision, per-finger and wrist wrench estimates, and distributed fingertip taxels. With demonstrations, visual observations, action space, and compliant control fixed, we compare vision-only, wrench, taxel, and combined policies plus representation and fusion baselines. The combined policy succeeds in 24/25 trials versus 14/25 for vision only, and 15/15 versus 6/15 across the three confined conditions. Ablations show that wrench and taxel feedback are complementary. Behavioral comparisons show that interaction feedback enables earlier rejection of inadequate contacts, regrasping before lift, and more stable grasps. To our knowledge, this is the first real-world study to combine and separately evaluate these interaction modalities for target-oriented dexterous grasping in clutter. These results chart a widening vision-interaction gap and position cluttered dexterous grasping as a benchmark for determining when the learned policy needs interaction sensing. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [95] [Safety Control of a Hyper-redundant Robot via Adaptive Weighted Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24062)
*Zijian Cai,Kiwan Wong,Wenci Xin,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出加权控制屏障函数（W-CBFs）框架，用于超冗余机器人在杂乱环境中安全操作，并减少因负载不均导致的沿体跟踪误差；仿真与实验表明跟踪误差显著降低，自主清洁策略覆盖性能相当或更优且无碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冗余机器人因高灵活性适合受限空间操作，但在杂乱环境中安全运行仍具挑战；其细长结构易导致负载分布不均和沿身体非均匀跟踪误差，因此需要在施加安全约束的同时改善跟踪性能。

Method: 提出W-CBFs控制框架，在安全约束下引入权重以抑制负载不均引起的跟踪误差。先在圆形路径跟踪任务和不同障碍配置下评估固定权重；再依据仿真与实验性能差异，研究基于不同映射函数的自适应加权策略；最后在矩形区域覆盖清洁任务中与人工遥操作对比。

Result: 固定权重下，相比非加权方法，均方根（RMS）跟踪误差最大降低仿真59.6%、物理实验87.7%。自适应加权进一步使RMS误差分别降低21.9%和8.5%，但当障碍靠近机器人本体时误差增加。清洁任务中，自主策略虽未针对覆盖优化，仍取得相当或更好的覆盖效果，并避免与周围框架碰撞，而人工遥操作发生碰撞。

Conclusion: W-CBFs能有效兼顾超冗余机器人的安全约束与跟踪误差抑制；自适应权重可进一步降低误差但受障碍距离影响；在区域覆盖任务中展现出安全自主操作的潜力，安全性优于人工遥操作。

Abstract: Hyper-redundant robots are well suited for confined-space manipulation due to their high dexterity, but safe operation in cluttered environments remains challenging. In addition, their slender structures often lead to uneven load distributions and nonuniform tracking errors along the body. To address these issues, this work proposes a weighted control barrier functions (W-CBFs) framework that enforces safety constraints while reducing tracking errors caused by uneven loading. The proposed controller was first evaluated on a circular path-following task under different obstacle configurations. With fixed weights, compared to the non-weighted method, the maximum reduction in root-mean-square (RMS) tracking error was 59.6\% in simulation and 87.7\% in physical experiments.
