Abstract: We present Elevator-VIGS, a visual-inertial 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM system that keeps tracking and mapping through elevator rides. Inside a moving elevator, the two sensors are in conflict. The camera sees only the robot's motion relative to the elevator, while the IMU senses that motion plus the elevator's motion relative to the world. This conflict is challenging for existing visual-inertial estimators. If vision dominates, the estimator tracks only the robot's motion within the elevator and misses the elevator's rise, and if the conflict remains, the estimator diverges. We observe that the conflict comes from forcing both observations into a single coordinate frame. We instead estimate the robot's pose in the elevator's coordinate frame, and the elevator's motion relative to the world as a per-keyframe transport state, the elevator's rise and vertical velocity, within dense visual-inertial bundle adjustment. Elevator-VIGS detects rides zero-shot with a vision-language model and a depth network, and constrains the transport state at the departure and the arrival. We record real-world and simulated elevator sequences. On these sequences, Elevator-VIGS achieves state-of-the-art tracking and rendering performance. On four elevator-free public benchmarks it keeps the state-of-the-art performance of VIGS-SLAM. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [131] [FeasibleFlow: One-Step Joint Transport of Configuration Feasibility and Trajectories for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23488)
*Xiang Li,Bikun Wang,Qing Xu,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FeasibleFlow，一种一步式端到端生成框架，联合传输配置空间可行性场与多模态自车轨迹；通过 Asymmetric Joint MeanFlow、ARR 和 Pareto-ReinFlow 平衡安全与进度，在 NAVSIM 上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶将当前观测直接映射为未来轨迹，但场景演化要求轨迹持续可行。未来状态建模可缓解时序失配，但通用表示常含与自车规划无关信息，且仅通过辅助监督、静态条件或候选评估影响轨迹生成，难以有效用于规划。

Method: FeasibleFlow 为一步式端到端生成框架，联合传输配置空间可行性场与多模态自车轨迹。其 Asymmetric Joint MeanFlow 利用 MeanFlow 恒等式中的路径雅可比-向量积，将可行性场演化纳入轨迹传输。针对安全反馈较进度反馈更稀疏，引入 Anchor-relative ranker (ARR) 用于候选选择，并设计 Pareto-ReinFlow 用于生成，以平衡安全与进度。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上，FeasibleFlow 表现出强性能，并验证了可行性与轨迹联合传输以及所提安全优先机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将可行性场与轨迹联合生成、并显式平衡安全与进度，可提升端到端自动驾驶规划的有效性与可行性；FeasibleFlow 为未来状态建模与安全决策提供了一种生成式解决方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving maps current observations directly to future trajectories, yet those trajectories must remain valid as the scene evolves. Future state modeling aims to address this temporal mismatch, but general representations often contain information unrelated to ego planning and affect trajectory generation only through auxiliary supervision, static conditioning, or proposal evaluation. We propose FeasibleFlow, a one-step end-to-end generative framework that jointly transports a configuration-space feasibility field and multimodal ego trajectories. Our Asymmetric Joint MeanFlow uses the pathwise Jacobian-vector product in the MeanFlow identity to incorporate field evolution into trajectory transport. Because safety feedback is sparser than progress feedback, we further introduce the Anchor-relative ranker (ARR) and Pareto-ReinFlow to balance safety and progress in candidate selection and generation, respectively. Experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark demonstrate the strong performance of FeasibleFlow and validate both the joint transport of feasibility and trajectories and the proposed safety-first mechanisms.

</details>


### [132] [Design and Control of a Cable-Driven Switchable Actuator with Torque/Tension Dual Modes for Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23487)
*YuanLong Ji,Xu Liu,Xinyuan Cai,Qihan Ye,Xiangyu Xie,Ruizhe Jiang,Shuhan Xiang,Wenjing Liu,Qijun Wang,Yang Chen,Xingbang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种缆驱动可切换执行器（CDSA），可在关节扭矩与线性拉力两种模式间快速切换，并将传感与驱动集中在前端驱动单元，用于提升可穿戴外骨骼对多种训练场景的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴外骨骼架构通常仅支持单一机械输出模态：或围绕解剖关节输出扭矩，或沿肢体训练方向输出线性牵引，限制了其对多样化训练任务的适应能力。

Method: 设计CDSA，通过耦合可动滑轮机构（CMPM）在末端实现拉力放大，通过双向缆驱动棘轮机构（CDRM）实现模式切换与预载调节；集成多源近端传感器并采用数据驱动融合模型估计远端输出力，避免远端仪器；提出基于迭代学习控制（ILC）的自适应双模式力控制策略。

Result: 平台实验显示扭矩模式与拉力模式的传动效率分别为(92.4±2.0)%和(96.5±3.3)%，拉力模式下拉力放大比为2.77±0.10；模拟膝关节步态轨迹与短行程拉力轨迹跟踪控制稳定，RMSE分别为未受控峰值的(4.52±0.51)%和(3.15±0.19)%；坐姿人体耦合实验验证了关节扭矩与线性牵引两种应用模式下可控的力生成。

Conclusion: 该CDSA架构能够在统一系统中实现扭矩与拉力双模式输出，保持驱动与传感近端集成且无需远端仪器，并表现出良好的传动效率、力估计与控制性能及人体应用可行性。

Abstract: Existing wearable exoskeleton architectures are typically constrained by a single mechanical output modality, providing either joint torque around an anatomical joint or linear traction along a limb-training-oriented direction, which limits adaptability to diverse training scenarios. This letter presents a cable-driven switchable actuator (CDSA) that can rapidly switch between torque and tension modes while centralizing all sensing and actuation components at the proximal drive unit. A Coupled Movable Pulley Mechanism (CMPM) provides tension amplification at the distal end-effector, while a bidirectional Cable-Driven Ratchet Mechanism (CDRM) enables mode switching and preload regulation. To eliminate the need for distal instrumentation, multi-source proximal sensors are integrated with a data-driven fusion model to estimate distal output forces. An adaptive dual-mode force control strategy based on iterative learning control (ILC) is further developed. Platform experiments demonstrate transmission efficiencies of $(92.4 \pm 2.0)\%$ and $(96.5 \pm 3.3)\%$ in the torque and tension modes, respectively, along with a tension amplification ratio of $2.77 \pm 0.10$ under tension mode. Tracking tests on simulated knee-joint gait trajectories and short-stroke tension profiles yield stable control, with RMSEs of $(4.52 \pm 0.51)\%$ and $(3.15 \pm 0.19)\%$ of the uncontrolled peak value, respectively. Finally, seated human-coupled experiments validate the system's controllable force generation in both joint-torque and linear-traction application modes.

</details>


### [133] [Cognitive Action Reasoning for Proactive Robots from Human-Centered Multimodal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23486)
*Zhihao Gu,Kechao Zhu,Yuanfeng Wu,Mohan Liu,Ankit Kumar Shaw,ChenDong Hong,Xuanyu Chen,Dengchen Mei,Xu Tianyi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“主动式机器人动作推理”（ProRobo）问题，并构建真实世界多模态数据集ProAction（1万样本，视觉/音频/文本，12类日常场景）与参考模型MMC2Act，表明通用MLLM难以从多模态线索主动推理高层动作，而基于ProAction训练可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人通常执行显式指令，多数机器人学习数据集也将观测与任务指令或低层动作配对；尽管已开始探索主动式具身辅助，但已有资源面向不同设定和动作层级，真实世界中以人为中心的多模态决策仍缺乏研究。

Method: 将问题形式化为ProRobo：机器人无需显式动作指令，仅依据人类与环境的多模态线索判断应执行何种动作。为此构建ProAction数据集，包含5类常见场景、12种日常生活场景中的1万条视觉、音频和文本样本。采用两阶段人在回路流程生成认知基础的高层动作监督：评估引导的候选生成，以及情感心理理论引导的人工精修，将人类状态、紧迫性、可行性与潜在风险等情境判断纳入标注。基于该监督对代表性MLLM进行基准测试，并提出MMC2Act参考模型，隐式学习从多模态观测到认知基础高层动作的映射。

Result: 在多种模态设置、主体不相交泛化、跨数据集迁移和人工评估中，通用MLLM难以从多模态线索主动推理高层动作；而在ProAction上训练可显著提升性能。

Conclusion: ProAction与MMC2Act填补了以人为中心的真实世界多模态主动决策资源与方法空白，证明主动式高层动作推理需要专门监督，且基于该数据训练能有效改善机器人主动辅助能力。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-centered environments are typically designed to execute explicit instructions, and most robot-learning datasets likewise pair observations with task instructions or low-level actions. Although recent work has begun to explore proactive embodied assistance, existing resources target different settings and action levels, leaving real-world human-centered multimodal decision-making underexplored. We formulate this problem as \textit{Proactive Robot Action Reasoning} (\textit{ProRobo}), an upstream cognitive decision problem in which a robot must determine which action to take based on multimodal human and environmental cues without explicit action instructions. To support ProRobo, we introduce \textit{ProAction}, a real-world multimodal dataset containing 10K samples of visual observations, audio signals, and text inputs across 12 daily-life scenarios in five common scenes. To construct cognitively grounded high-level action supervision, we develop a two-stage human-in-the-loop pipeline that combines appraisal-guided candidate generation with Affective Theory-of-Mind-guided human refinement, explicitly incorporating contextual judgment about human states, urgency, feasibility, and potential risk into action annotation. Based on this supervision, we benchmark representative Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and introduce \textit{MMC2Act}, a reference model that implicitly learns the mapping from multimodal observations to cognitively grounded high-level actions. Experiments across modality settings, subject-disjoint generalization, cross-dataset transfer, and human evaluation show that general-purpose MLLMs struggle with proactively reasoning high-level actions from multimodal cues, whereas training on \textit{ProAction} substantially improves performance.

</details>


### [134] [STRIDER: Stepping-Enabled Multi-Gait Hierarchical 3D Loco-Manipulation Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23483)
*Yuanzhuo Li,Wen Zhao,Zhe Yong,Xiang Meng,Gang Han,Hengle Ren,Xiaoyang Zheng,Zhen Wang,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STRIDER分层多步态框架与LD-PPO蒸馏算法，实现人形机器人精确落脚与全身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续速度控制难以精确调节单个落脚点，专用落脚跟踪模块难以集成到全身操作中；标准动作模仿蒸馏仅迁移专家动作，未显式促进异构技能的共享表征。

Method: STRIDER集成地形感知3D踏步逻辑、基于AMP的自然行走和笛卡尔上身控制；踏步专家在支撑脚坐标系中选择可行落脚点，并生成考虑离地间隙的摆动轨迹。提出LD-PPO，通过教师条件潜变量对齐增强蒸馏，联合优化在线强化学习、基于DAgger的动作重建和潜变量对齐，将行走与踏步专家融合为可执行的学生策略。

Result: 在TianGong Omni人形机器人仿真与实机评估中，LD-PPO在落脚跟踪和姿态跟踪精度上优于vanilla distillation-PPO；实机部署实现多步态loco-manipulation，并保持准确的落脚与末端执行器跟踪。

Conclusion: STRIDER有效桥接了精确落脚与全身操作，LD-PPO通过共享异构技能表征提升了多步态移动操作的可部署性与跟踪性能。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation faces two prominent limitations: controllers using continuous velocity commands cannot precisely regulate individual footholds, while specialized foothold-tracking modules are difficult to integrate with whole-body manipulation. Furthermore, standard action-based imitation distillation primarily transfers expert actions, without explicitly encouraging a shared representation of heterogeneous skills. This paper introduces STRIDER, a hierarchical multi-gait framework to bridge these gaps. The framework integrates terrain-aware 3D stepping logic, Adversarial Motion Priors (AMP)-based natural walking, and Cartesian upper-body control: its stepping expert selects feasible footholds in the stance-foot frame and generates clearance-aware swing trajectories. To fuse distinct walking and stepping experts into one executable student policy, we propose Latent Distillation Proximal Policy Optimization (LD-PPO), a distillation algorithm augmented with teacher-conditioned latent alignment. By jointly optimizing on-policy reinforcement learning, DAgger-based action reconstruction, and latent alignment, LD-PPO transfers expert actions while encouraging a shared skill representation across heterogeneous modes. Simulation and real-robot evaluations on the TianGong Omni humanoid show that LD-PPO outperforms vanilla distillation-PPO in foothold-tracking and posture-tracking accuracy. Deployed on hardware, STRIDER realizes multi-gait loco-manipulation with accurate foothold and end-effector tracking.

</details>


### [135] [Smartphone GNSS Booster: Centimeter-Level Pedestrian Positioning Using a Portable Signal Re-Radiator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23456)
*Taro Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可附着于智能手机的外置GNSS信号再辐射系统，通过提升接收信号强度与相位中心稳定性，使智能手机在行人场景下实现厘米级载波相位定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机内嵌紧凑线极化GNSS天线导致观测噪声大、载波相位跟踪不稳定，现有行人定位精度仅米级，难以满足人行道级导航和高精度位置服务需求。

Method: 设计外置GNSS信号再辐射系统：将紧凑有源螺旋天线与薄型无源贴片天线直接相连并附着于智能手机，复用手机内置GNSS接收机与天线，实现信号再辐射；旨在提高接收信号强度、抑制多径并稳定天线相位中心变化。

Result: 静态实验表明，相较标准智能手机定位，GNSS载波相位观测质量显著提升；行人实验可实现稳定的载波相位整周模糊度固定，并获得厘米级定位精度。

Conclusion: 该外置再辐射增强方案无需更换手机内置GNSS接收机即可改善观测质量，验证了智能手机实现厘米级GNSS定位的可行性，可用于行人导航等高精度应用。

Abstract: High-precision positioning using the global navigation satellite system (GNSS) embedded in smartphones is demanded for sidewalk-level pedestrian navigation and pinpoint location-based applications. However, current smartphone positioning accuracy for pedestrians remains at the meter level. This is mainly because limitations of the compact linearly polarized (LP) antenna in smartphones increase GNSS observation noise and hinder stable carrier phase tracking. In this paper, we propose an external GNSS signal re-radiation system that boosts smartphone GNSS observation quality while still using the smartphone's built-in GNSS receiver and antenna. The system directly connects a compact active helical antenna and a thin passive patch antenna for re-radiation, and is designed to be attached directly to the smartphone. The proposed GNSS booster achieves (1) reduced thermal noise and stable signal tracking via increased received signal strength, (2) improved multipath robustness compared with a LP antenna, and (3) stabilization of antenna phase center variation. Static experiments confirm a substantial improvement in the observation quality of GNSS carrier phase measurements compared with standard smartphone positioning. Furthermore, in pedestrian experiments, the proposed booster enabled stable carrier phase integer ambiguity resolutions, achieving centimeter-level positioning using a smartphone.

</details>


### [136] [BiRoAD: Learning Shared and Role-Adaptive Representations for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23445)
*Yan Shen,Yuchen Liu,Feng Jiang,Hangtian Hu,Xiaoqi Li,Shu Chen,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BiRoAD：一种双臂角色自适应分解框架，通过将双臂轨迹或动作 token 特征分解为交换对称与交换反对称分量，学习共享协调结构与随功能角色变化而变化的角色特定表征，从而提升双臂策略在不同角色配置下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作需要两条手臂协调，并根据场景几何、物体构型和任务上下文动态调整功能角色。现有学习面临两个问题：演示数据中角色分布不均衡，导致对欠代表的手臂-角色配置泛化差；许多策略在固定的左臂/右臂动作空间中预测动作，未显式建模功能角色交换时行为应如何变换。实际上，不同场景初始化下双臂可能保持相似协调模式，但各臂承担的角色特定行为应随场景改变。

Method: BiRoAD 以双臂轨迹或动作 token 特征为输入，将其分解为两部分：交换对称分量，表示对左右臂交换不变的协调结构；交换反对称分量，表示随功能角色分配一致变化的角色特定差异。随后将这两个分量重组合为对原始成对手臂表示的残差更新。该方法作为模块化特征变换，不改变策略输入、模仿学习目标，也不需要人工定义角色标签。

Result: 在多个具有均衡与不均衡角色分布的双臂操作任务中，BiRoAD 相比对应基础策略提升了跨角色配置的鲁棒性，并在欠代表角色配置上取得显著增益。

Conclusion: BiRoAD 通过学习共享且角色自适应的分解表征，能够在无需显式角色标签的情况下增强双臂策略对角色配置变化的适应性与泛化，尤其改善角色分布不均衡时的表现。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires policies that coordinate two arms while adapting their functional roles to scene geometry, object configuration, and task context. Learning such scene-conditioned role adaptation remains challenging, as demonstrations may contain uneven role distributions that limit generalization to underrepresented arm--role configurations. In addition, many bimanual policies predict actions in fixed left- and right-arm action spaces. While this provides a natural parameterization for robot control, it does not explicitly specify how behaviors should transform when functional roles are exchanged across arms. Across different scene initializations, the two arms may follow a similar coordination pattern, but the role-specific behavior assigned to each arm should change with the scene. Therefore, we propose BiRoAD, a Bimanual Role-Adaptive Decomposition framework for learning shared and role-adaptive representations in bimanual policies. Given bimanual trajectory or action-token features, BiRoAD decomposes these features into swap--symmetric and swap--antisymmetric components: the former captures coordination structure invariant to arm exchange, and the latter captures role-specific distinctions that vary consistently with functional role assignment. The two components are then recomposed as residual updates to the original paired arm representations, allowing BiRoAD to serve as a modular feature transformation without changing the policy inputs, imitation-learning objective, or requiring manually defined role labels. Across multiple bimanual manipulation tasks with balanced and imbalanced role distributions, BiRoAD improves robustness across role configurations over corresponding base policies, with notable gains on underrepresented role configurations.

</details>


### [137] [Receding-Horizon Pushing with Composable Object-Centric Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23439)
*Zhiyi Yuan,Tianrun Hu,Anxing Xiao,Yuhong Deng,David Hsu,Hanbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种反馈引导的分层推物框架，结合学习式接触策略、准静态稳定性评估与BIT*路径搜索，实现任意形状3D物体的长时程非抓取推动，并支持零样本仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取操作适用于大型、重型或几何上难以抓取的物体；但任意形状3D物体的长时程推动同时耦合三个难题：1）推哪里才能接近目标位姿，2）每次推动是否稳定且可达，3）后续动作是否仍然可行。

Method: 采用以物体为中心的分层框架。低层使用学习策略，从经过位姿与尺度归一化的点云中，以近单步子目标为条件预测接触动作；再用准静态滑动与倾覆分析计算稳定性分数，评估预测接触。高层先用BIT*搜索物体路径，并对后续若干子目标通过接触预测和机器人运动规划检查未来可行性；运动规划失败作为反馈，修改局部路径代价并触发重规划。执行时只执行第一个可行动作。

Result: 在仿真中评估22个物体、6种场景，并进行全面消融实验。结果表明该方法以明显优势优于基线，能在不同情况下可靠完成长时程物体推动任务。还报告了Franka机械臂的定量真机实验，以及移动操作臂对大型重物的定性演示，均实现直接零样本仿真到真实迁移。

Conclusion: 该反馈引导分层框架可有效协调接触选择、稳定性与未来可行性，在仿真和真实机器人上实现可靠的长时程非抓取推动，并展现出直接零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation is practical for relocating large, heavy, or geometrically ungraspable objects. Yet, long-horizon pushing of arbitrarily-shaped 3D objects couples three problems: 1) where to push the object so as to approach the target pose, 2) whether each push is stable and reachable, 3) whether subsequent actions remain feasible. We present an object-centric pushing policy within a feedback-guided hierarchical framework. At the low level, a learning-based policy predicts contact actions from a pose- and scale-normalized point cloud, conditioned on a near single-step subgoal. A stability score is applied to evaluate the predicted contacts by a quasi-static sliding-versus-tipping analysis. At the high level, BIT$^*$ first searches for an object path, and the next several subgoals are checked by contact prediction and robot motion planning for future feasibility. Failed motion plans, as feedback, change the local path costs and trigger re-planning. During execution, only the first feasible action is executed. In simulation, we evaluate 22 objects in six different scenes, upon which we also conduct comprehensive ablation studies. Results demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines with a clear margin and can reliably achieve long-horizon object pushing tasks under different situations. We also report quantitative real-robot experiments with a Franka arm and qualitative demonstrations with a mobile manipulator for large and heavy objects, with directly zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [138] [RopeFormer: Cross-Trial Adaptation from Interaction History for Dynamic Rope Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23432)
*Menglin Wu,Kaixiang Yao,Shangbo Luan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RopeFormer，一种历史条件化框架，利用先前任务交互作为后续控制上下文，无需在线估计绳索参数，即可提升动态绳索操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态绳索操作对未知物体动力学高度敏感：相同机器人动作在不同绳索上响应差异显著，而显式辨识相关物理参数又很困难。

Method: RopeFormer 采用历史条件化策略，在固定策略权重、无需显式在线绳索参数估计的前提下，保留跨试验的动作-响应历史，并将其作为后续控制的上下文。

Result: 在持续单臂旋转、双臂旋转和瞬态鞭打等匹配仿真评估中，保留上下文相比重置同一检查点能改善后续控制，且收益随绳索动力学和观测设置变化。在 Unitree H1-2 上部署冻结策略并操作未见过的物理绳索时，从 T1 到 T3，Rope Swing 目标获取时间减少 30.9%，Rope Twirl 减少 33.9%，Rope Whip 平均目标命中数从 0.2 增至 2.3（满分 3）。

Conclusion: 先前交互可为动态可变形物体操作提供有效的控制上下文，表明无需显式参数估计也能提升跨绳索泛化控制。

Abstract: Dynamic rope manipulation is highly sensitive to unknown object dynamics: the same robot motion can produce substantially different responses across ropes, while explicitly identifying the relevant physical properties is difficult. We present RopeFormer, a history-conditioned framework that uses prior task interaction as context for subsequent control. The policy retains cross-trial action-response history while keeping its weights fixed and requires no explicit online rope-parameter estimation. In matched simulation evaluations across sustained single-arm rotation, bimanual rotation, and transient whipping, retaining context improves subsequent control relative to resetting the same checkpoint, with the benefit varying across rope dynamics and observation settings. We further deploy the frozen policies on a Unitree H1-2 with previously unseen physical ropes. From T1 to T3, target-acquisition time decreases by 30.9% for Rope Swing and 33.9% for Rope Twirl, while mean Rope Whip target hits increase from 0.2 to 2.3 out of three. These results show that prior interaction can provide effective control context for dynamic deformable-object manipulation. Robot videos, code, and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [139] [RiverVLN: Phase-Grounded Temporal Vision--Language Navigation for Unmanned Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23423)
*Jieling Wu,Yuehao Huang,Jiajun Lv,Tao Huang,Yong Liu,Weiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向连续河流运动的长时程USV视觉语言导航基准RiverVLN，以及阶段接地的时序导航框架PGT-NAV，在仿真闭环和真实USV实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN主要面向室内与地面机器人，常将语言视为静态目标、运动近似为离散或瞬时动作；但USV河流导航需在惯性下连续运动且机动性受限，长时程指令还需通过稀疏且视觉模糊的航标执行，这些假设不再成立。

Method: PGT-NAV不直接将整条指令映射为运动，而是将其转化为有序、可视觉验证的语义阶段序列，并利用接地的视觉与运动证据在线维护当前活动阶段；融合显式语义进度状态、视觉-运动历史和阶段特定接地，预测六个局部SE(2)位姿增量；轨迹以“预测-执行-重观察”闭环执行，向W3推进，更新阶段与接地，并通过基于地图的安全层重规划。

Result: 相比GNM式和ViNT式基线，PGT-NAV显著降低递归位置与航向漂移，在Unity-ROS闭环导航中平均成功率达0.79；未见桥开合试验和真实USV实验进一步表明，阶段接地表示可从受控评估迁移到物理USV部署。

Conclusion: 阶段接地的显式语义进度表示能有效支持连续河流环境下USV的长时程视觉语言导航，并具备从仿真/受控评估向真实USV部署迁移的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) has largely been developed for indoor and terrestrial robots, where language can often be treated as a static goal and motion is approximated by discrete or near-instantaneous actions. These assumptions break down for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs): river navigation requires continuous motion under inertia and limited maneuverability, while long-horizon instructions must be executed through sparse and visually ambiguous maritime landmarks. We introduce RiverVLN, to our knowledge the first benchmark designed for long-horizon USV VLN under continuous riverine motion, and PGT-NAV, a phase-grounded temporal navigation framework for USVs. Rather than directly mapping an entire instruction to motion, PGT-NAV converts it into an ordered sequence of visually verifiable semantic phases and maintains the active phase online through grounded visual and motion evidence. This explicit semantic progress state is fused with visual-motion history and phase-specific grounding to predict six local SE(2) pose increments. The resulting trajectory is executed in a predict-execute-re-observe loop, where the vessel executes toward W3, updates phase and grounding, and replans through a map-based safety layer. Experiments show that PGT-NAV substantially reduces recursive position and heading drift relative to GNM-style and ViNT-style baselines and achieves an average success rate of 0.79 in Unity-ROS closed-loop navigation. Unseen bridge-opening trials and real-world USV experiments further demonstrate that the phase-grounded representation transfers from controlled evaluation to physical USV deployment.

</details>


### [140] [HEARTH: An Object-Centric RGB-Thermal-3D Dataset for Temperature-Aware Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23418)
*Yuning Su,Borui Li,Yonghao Shi,Bofei Liu,Xing-Dong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HEARTH：一个面向机器人学习的物体级 RGB-热成像-3D 数据集，用于训练机器人理解和执行与温度相关的语言指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的操作可能依赖外观无法揭示的物理属性，温度就是其中之一；但现有机器人学习物体数据集很少将实测温度与物体外观和几何关联起来。

Method: 构建包含 18 个日常类别、90 个物理物体、145 个捕捉状态的 RGB-热成像-3D 数据集；通过相机标定和位姿迁移将表观表面温度映射到重建网格，并提供原始温度测量、相机参数、RGB 纹理网格和用于仿真的热纹理。基于这些资产构建三个 LIBERO 衍生任务，采集 1,200 条演示用于微调预训练视觉-语言-动作模型 π0.5。

Result: 消融研究中，在 VLA 中加入热观测后，温度相关物体选择任务成功率从仅 RGB 基线的 35.0% 提升到 75.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 HEARTH 能有效支持训练机器人策略遵循温度相关指令，并凸显热感知对语言引导操作的价值。

Abstract: Language-guided manipulation can depend on physical properties that visible appearance does not reveal. Temperature is one such property, but object datasets for robot learning rarely associate measured temperatures with object appearance and geometry. We present HEARTH, an object-centric RGB-thermal-3D dataset of 90 physical objects from 18 everyday categories, comprising 145 captured object states. Our pipeline maps apparent surface temperatures onto reconstructed meshes through camera calibration and pose transfer. The dataset includes raw temperature measurements, camera parameters, RGB-textured meshes, and thermal textures for simulation. We use these assets to construct three LIBERO-derived tasks and collect 1,200 demonstrations for fine-tuning a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model, $\pi_{0.5}$. In an ablation study, adding thermal observations to the VLA increases success on temperature-dependent object-selection tasks from 35.0% for the RGB-only baseline to 75.0%. These results demonstrate the utility of HEARTH for training robot policies to follow temperature-related instructions.

</details>


### [141] [EmoPose: Vision-Language Model Guided Emotion-Aware Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23414)
*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Fulong Ma,Wenjun Yue,Liang Zhang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EmoPose：一种由视觉语言模型（VLM）引导、通过可执行语义接口连接社交意图与机器人控制的框架，将语言和上下文映射为有序手势计划，并由机器人自有动作库与确定性模块执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交人形机器人需通过手势和语音表达情感与意图，但开放式交互必须转化为特定身体上既具表达性又可执行的动作。这要求兼顾上下文社交语义的灵活性与确定性、具身感知的机器人控制，而不能把原始关节命令交给基础模型。

Method: 给定语言、对话历史和可选视觉上下文，VLM选择有序手势计划，包含交际类别、库变体、强度和语音锚点；可扩展的机器人自有动作库定义表达词汇并提供14-DoF关节目标。Pose Studio支持自动轨迹生成、MuJoCo预览，以及新库条目与VLM指南的自动同步；机器人端确定性模块负责验证计划、构建轨迹、调度手势并管理队列与中断。

Result: 在EmoPose-Bench上，GPT-5.5结构化规划在Easy层级达到98.25±0.52%，总体达到76.50±0.54%，超过同模型直接标签提示。进一步测试验证了对话上下文利用和有序多动作组合。系统完成名义MuJoCo套件，并在物理Unitree G1上实现全部29种创作变体。四站实验室导览展示了带中断的表达性叙述、相机接地对话和导航。

Conclusion: EmoPose通过可执行语义接口和机器人侧确定性控制，在不改变控制接口、也不向基础模型委派原始关节命令的前提下扩展社交交互动作库，从而桥接表达性社交意图与具身可执行动作。

Abstract: Socially competent humanoid robots must communicate affect and intent through gesture as well as speech, yet open-ended interaction must become motion that is both expressive and executable on a specific body. This demands semantic flexibility for contextual social intent while preserving deterministic, embodiment-aware robot control. We present EmoPose, a vision-language model (VLM)-guided framework that bridges this gap through an executable semantic interface. Given language, dialogue history, and optional visual context, the VLM selects an ordered gesture plan containing a communicative class, library variant, intensity, and speech anchor. A scalable robot-owned motion library defines the available expressive vocabulary and the source of 14-DoF joint targets. Pose Studio supports automatic trajectory generation, MuJoCo preview, and automatic synchronization of new library entries with the VLM guide; deterministic robot-side modules validate plans, construct trajectories, schedule gestures, and manage queueing and interruption. This division lets the interaction repertoire grow for new social contexts without changing the control interface or delegating raw joint commands to the foundation model. On the EmoPose-Bench, structured GPT-5.5 planning reaches $98.25\pm0.52\%$ on the Easy tier and $76.50\pm0.54\%$ overall, exceeding same-model direct-label prompting. Further tests validate dialogue-context use and ordered multi-action composition. The system completes the nominal MuJoCo suite and realizes all 29 authored variants on the physical Unitree G1. A four-stop laboratory tour demonstrates expressive narration with interruption, camera-grounded dialogue, and navigation.

</details>


### [142] [Towards Reliable Underwater Diver-Robot Interaction: Gesture Design, Interaction Logic, and Real-World Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23392)
*Yingqi Liu,Kanzhong Yao,Zimeng Peng,Yuanbo Bi,Anran Li,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个闭环潜水员-机器人手势交互框架，通过七手势词表、轻量级关键点识别和命令级逻辑，提升水下手势命令的可用性与识别可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下人机交互需要潜水员易用且机器人可可靠识别的 gesture 命令，因此需同时考虑人的可用性、手势可识别性与执行可靠性。

Method: 构建闭环 diver–robot 交互框架，集成紧凑的七手势词表、基于轻量级 landmark 的识别方法以及命令级交互逻辑；通过用户研究和实验室水槽与游泳池中的水下机器人实验进行评估。

Result: 用户研究表明手势在简短学习后具有可重复性；识别分析发现视觉相似性与手势混淆相关，手势形成过程中的中间姿态带来时间歧义；命令级处理缓解了瞬态识别错误对机器人执行的影响，减少了意外触发和任务过早中断。

Conclusion: 可靠的水下手势交互依赖于人类可用性、手势可识别性以及水下交互中的执行可靠性。

Abstract: Underwater human--robot interaction requires gesture commands that are both easy for divers to use and reliable for robots to recognize. We investigate these aspects through a closed-loop diver--robot interaction framework integrating a compact seven-gesture vocabulary, lightweight landmark-based recognition, and command-level interaction logic. We evaluate the framework through a user study and underwater robot experiments in a laboratory tank and a swimming pool. The user study supported the reproducibility of the gestures after brief learning. Recognition analysis further showed that visual similarity was associated with gesture confusion, while intermediate poses during gesture formation introduced temporal ambiguity. Command-level processing mitigated the effects of transient recognition errors on robot execution, reducing unintended triggers and premature task interruptions. These findings show that reliable underwater gesture interaction depends on human usability, gesture recognizability, and execution reliability in underwater interaction.

</details>


### [143] [Manipulation Feasible Navigation Among Movable Obstacles with Discrete Contact Pushing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23312)
*Shaohu Wang,Aiguo Song,Yulong Yuan,Zhongyu Sun,Tianyuan Miao,Qinjie Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向移动操作臂的分层可移动障碍物导航（NAMO）框架，结合高层搬迁规划、LLM辅助依赖推理与底层接触模式推挤控制，实现大型不可抓取障碍物环境下的交互式导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在大型可移动障碍物的环境中，仅绕行导航效率低甚至不可行；而交互式导航需同时推理导航收益、可行放置位置与可执行操作，难度较大。

Method: 采用分层框架：高层从参考路径识别关键阻塞障碍物，搜索同时满足几何、操作与下游导航约束的搬迁方案；当直接搬迁受其他可移动物体阻碍时，选择性调用LLM推断辅助操作依赖，并由确定性几何规划验证。执行层针对箱形障碍物表面定义离散接触模式，依据位置与姿态误差在线选择接触面与区域，通过接触切换实现直推、侧推与角推；使用循环强化学习策略协调移动底盘与机械臂，在持续推挤中跟踪TCP目标并保持末端可达性。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验在绕行、单/多障碍物搬迁及依赖约束场景中验证了导航-操作可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于大型不可抓取障碍物的交互式导航，并已开源项目。

Abstract: In environments with large movable obstacles, detour-only navigation can be inefficient or even infeasible, while obstacle interaction requires reasoning about navigation benefit, feasible placement, and executable manipulation. We present a hierarchical navigation among movable obstacles (NAMO) framework for mobile manipulators. At the high level, the planner identifies key blocking obstacles from reference paths and searches for relocation plans that jointly satisfy geometric, manipulation, and downstream navigation constraints. When direct relocation is hindered by other movable objects, a large language model (LLM) is selectively invoked to infer auxiliary manipulation dependencies, which are then verified by deterministic geometric planning. To execute the resulting relocation goals, we define discrete contact modes on the surfaces of box-shaped obstacles and select contact faces and regions online based on position and orientation errors, enabling straight, side, and corner pushing through contact switching. A recurrent reinforcement-learning policy coordinates the mobile base and manipulator to track tool center point (TCP) targets while preserving end-effector reachability during sustained pushing. Simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate feasible navigation-manipulation in detour, single- and multi-obstacle relocation, and dependency-constrained scenarios, validating the framework for interactive navigation with large non-graspable obstacles. The open-source project is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [Shared Execution-Clock Drifting Policy for Dynamic Precision Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23305)
*Zhenchen Dong,Qingran Wu,Jinna Fu,Jiaming Wu,Fulin Chen,Hongyu Yu,Yide Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Shared Execution-Clock Drifting（SECD），将执行节奏显式建模为一步式动作生成的一部分，联合预测进度索引动作曲线与共享单调时钟，以在时限内完成机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间约束下的操作既要求动作准确，也要求执行节奏与动态场景匹配；机器人拦截移动物体或按截止时间完成连续调整时尤为关键。一步式策略虽降低生成成本，但直接预测动作序列会使时间分配隐含、不受控。

Method: SECD在给定观测与潜变量样本下，联合预测一条按进度索引的动作曲线和一个共享单调时钟，将固定控制时刻映射到曲线位置。利用演示数据导出的对齐来锚定该分解，并通过在解码动作上进行drifting联合训练。该策略保留固定频率控制接口，且仅需一次网络评估。

Result: 在四个真实机器人任务、NVIDIA Thor推理下，300次试验取得77.00%任务平均成功率，并在每个任务上优于所评估的一步式基线；其中从16 m/min传送带取杯成功率达91%，90 s内恢复并折叠皱衬衫达54%。固定时钟变体在同一传送带协议上达79%。补充的状态型RoboMimic实验（含Transport与Square的跨随机种子消融）进一步支持该设计。

Conclusion: 将时间表示与演示对齐联合设计，可在不牺牲一步式生成效率的情况下显式控制执行节奏，并保持固定频率控制接口；实验表明SECD适合时间约束下的真实机器人操作。

Abstract: Manipulation under time constraints requires both accurate actions and an execution rhythm that matches the evolving scene. This becomes critical when a robot must intercept moving objects or complete a sequence of adjustments before a deadline. Although one-step policies reduce generation cost, their directly predicted action sequences leave temporal allocation implicit. We propose Shared Execution-Clock Drifting (SECD), which makes execution rhythm an explicit part of one-step action generation. Conditioned on an observation and a latent sample, the policy jointly predicts a progress-indexed action curve and a shared monotone clock that maps fixed control times to locations on the curve. Demonstration-derived alignment anchors this decomposition, which is trained jointly through drifting on the decoded actions. The resulting policy retains a fixed-rate control interface and requires one network evaluation. We evaluate SECD across four real-robot tasks with inference on NVIDIA Thor. Across 300 trials, it achieves 77.00% task-averaged success and outperforms the evaluated one-step baselines on every task, including 91% success in cup retrieval from a 16 m/min conveyor and 54% in restoring and folding a crumpled shirt within 90 s. A fixed-clock variant reaches 79% on the same conveyor protocol. Complementary state-based RoboMimic experiments, including cross-seed ablations on Transport and Square, further support the joint design of the temporal representation and demonstration alignment. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [145] [Safety-Critical Control under Uncertainty via Adaptive Conformal Quantile Prediction Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23280)
*Hao Zhou,Yanze Zhang,Yiwei Lyu,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应不确定性量化安全控制框架，利用ACP扩展CQR构造校准且状态相关的非对称预测区间，并融入概率控制屏障函数与MPC，实现高概率安全保证并降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键控制需要统计有效且能与可执行安全约束兼容的不确定性表示；现有方法常假设特定分布，或采用对称且与输入无关的预测区间，导致控制综合中安全约束错位或过度保守。

Method: 该框架基于自适应保形预测（ACP），并扩展保形分位数回归（CQR），构建分布无关、非对称且具有可证明概率覆盖的不确定性区间；将所得不确定性集集成到概率控制屏障函数（CBF）中，形成不确定性感知的安全约束，并嵌入模型预测控制（MPC）以提供高概率可证明安全行为。

Result: 仿真与理论结果表明，该方法能有效构建校准的状态相关预测区间，在MPC中实现高概率安全保证，同时改善约束控制性能并减少保守性。

Conclusion: 所提框架将自适应保形不确定性量化与概率安全约束控制相结合，为不确定性下的安全关键控制提供了统计有效、可证明且较少保守的高概率安全保证方案。

Abstract: Safety-critical control under uncertainty requires uncertainty representations that are both statistically valid (for certifiable performance) and compatible with enforceable safety constraints. However, existing methods often assume particular distributions of uncertainty for provable safety guarantees or establish symmetric and input-agnostic prediction intervals for robust safety, which can lead to misaligned or overly conservative safety constraints in control synthesis. In this paper, we introduce a novel safe control framework with adaptive uncertainty quantification that constructs calibrated and state-dependent prediction intervals to enable high-probability safety guarantees, while improving constrained control performance. The framework leverages adaptive conformal prediction (ACP) and extends it with conformal quantile regression (CQR) to capture distribution-free, asymmetric uncertainty intervals with certifiable probabilistic coverage, and integrates the resulting uncertainty sets into a probabilistic control barrier function formulation to enforce robust safety with reduced conservativeness. This yields uncertainty-aware safe control constraints that can be incorporated within a model predictive control(MPC) framework to provide provably safe behaviors with high probability. Simulation and theoretical results are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [146] [SCULPT-VLA: Learning Structured Control through Staged Action Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23275)
*Wenbo Li,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Wenhao Li,Jun Yang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCULPT-VLA 通过分阶段动作 grounding 学习结构化控制状态，在多个 VLA 基准和真实机器人任务上优于共享骨干基线及 π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略日益引入动作标签之外的结构化中间监督，但即使规定了中间表示应编码什么，仍未解决动作预测应如何学会依赖该表示。

Method: 提出 SCULPT-VLA：动作条件状态由任务进展、场景动态和空间 grounding 等互补因子构成。训练先用教师 scaffold 形成这些因子，再通过因子组合 grounding 粗粒度动作预测并逐步撤除 scaffold；随后恢复直接感知访问以结合学习状态与感知细节进行连续精修。该课程将基于结构条件化动作与精修连续控制分开，部署时无需教师或离散动作自回归。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv-WidowX 和 RoboTwin 2.0 Full 上平均成功率高于共享骨干基线；SimplerEnv-WidowX 最终成功率 83.5%，而 Stage-II 动作学习直接访问视觉语言时为 71.3%。四个真实机器人任务在测试分布偏移下平均成功率 58.1%，π0.5 为 45.6%。训练消融和因子干预支持分阶段设计，并表明恢复直接感知后学习状态仍贡献于控制。

Conclusion: 分阶段结构化动作 grounding 能有效学习并利用互补动作条件因子，提升 VLA 策略的成功率与分布偏移鲁棒性；结构化状态在直接感知恢复后仍持续服务于连续控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies increasingly incorporate structured intermediate supervision beyond action labels. Yet specifying what an intermediate representation should encode leaves open how action prediction learns to depend on it. We introduce \textbf{SCULPT-VLA}, a policy that learns structured control through staged action grounding. Its action-conditioning state comprises complementary factors for task progression, scene dynamics, and spatial grounding. Training first forms these factors with teacher scaffolds, then grounds coarse action prediction through their composition as scaffold inputs are withdrawn. Direct perceptual access is subsequently restored for continuous refinement, combining the learned state with perceptual detail. The curriculum separates learning to condition actions on structure from refining continuous control. Deployment requires neither teachers nor discrete-action autoregression. SCULPT-VLA achieves higher average success than shared-backbone baselines on LIBERO, SimplerEnv-WidowX, and RoboTwin 2.0 Full. On SimplerEnv-WidowX, final success is 83.5\%, versus 71.3\% when Stage-II action learning directly accesses vision and language. Across four physical robot tasks, average success under the tested distribution shifts reaches 58.1\%, compared with 45.6\% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Training ablations and factor-wise interventions support the staged design and show that the learned state continues to contribute to control after direct perceptual access is restored.

</details>


### [147] [Steering Through Contact: A Finite-Support Motion Model for Single-Track Center-Articulated Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23271)
*Mohamed Dhia Ounally,Nicolas Samson,Mathis Turgeon-Roy,Veronica Vannini,Johann Laconte,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对铰接式单轨车辆，提出有限支撑二次模型（FSQ），通过准静态侧滑平衡纳入接触点沿线的转动阻力，并用闭式解实时预测横向速度与横摆角速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人轨迹规划与控制依赖对推进和转向在接触点打滑时如何影响车辆运动的预测。现有铰接车辆的点接触运动学模型（PCK）考虑了连杆几何，但忽略了沿接触分布的转动阻力，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 提出适用于单轨、中心铰接车辆的有限支撑二次模型（FSQ）。该模型通过侧向滑移的准静态平衡引入转动阻力，放松点接触假设，从而推广标准PCK。将二次滑移代价精确约化为接触力矩，得到紧凑闭式解，用于实时预测横向速度和横摆角速度。

Result: 在沥青、草地、冰面及混合路线的真实实验中，使用超过7 km数据评估。对于5 s预测，FSQ相对PCK将加权中位平移误差和横摆误差分别降低53.5%和68.9%。

Conclusion: FSQ能有效建模铰接车辆接触区转动阻力，显著提升野外条件下横向运动预测精度，并保持实时性，优于传统PCK。

Abstract: Trajectory planning and control in field robotics rely on predicting how propulsion and steering affect vehicle motion when contact points undergo slip. For articulated vehicles, the point-contact kinematic model (PCK) accounts for the linkage geometry but neglects the rotational resistance distributed along the contacts. We propose a finite-support quadratic model (FSQ) for single-track, center-articulated vehicles that incorporates this resistance through a quasi-static balance of lateral slip. Our approach generalizes the standard PCK formulation by relaxing the contact-point assumption. An exact reduction of the quadratic slip cost to contact moments gives a compact closed-form solution for real-time prediction of lateral velocity and yaw rate. We evaluate the proposed method in real-world experiments across asphalt, grass, ice, and mixed routes, using more than 7 km of data. For five-second predictions, FSQ reduces the weighted median translation and yaw errors by 53.5% and 68.9%, respectively, relative to PCK.

</details>


### [148] [Latent Telepathy: Multi-Robot Communication with Self-Supervised Perceptual Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23269)
*Howard Wang,Han Zheng,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Telepathy：机器人广播自身感知编码器输出的潜在向量作为通信消息，编码器以自监督联合嵌入预测目标训练并冻结，接收者仅通过任务奖励学习利用该消息；在内容控制协议下，潜在消息显著优于位置、轨迹和原始图像，并跨网格世界、像素连续控制与实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的分散多机器人团队中，决定机器人下一步动作的信息往往只有队友能看见；现有分散方法传递位置或规划轨迹等运动学信息，无法传达队友感知内容；MARL 学习通信虽可承载感知信息，但消息通常任务耦合、不透明且未必高效。

Method: 每个机器人广播其自身感知已计算的潜在向量；该编码器由自监督联合嵌入预测目标训练，训练后冻结并在团队内共享；接收机器人不被告知消息含义，仅从任务奖励学习基于该消息行动；因编码器已用于感知，消息不增加额外计算，仅占一个紧凑向量的带宽。评估采用内容控制协议：固定带宽、延迟、拓扑和接收者，仅改变消息内容。

Result: 广播潜在向量使导航者避开被遮挡危险达99.7%，与无噪声手工设计消息相当；位置和轨迹消息仅达随机水平；原始相机图像宽186倍但可靠性更低。结果从离散网格世界扩展到连续速度控制下的渲染像素；编码器在物理机器人相机上102/102次实时决策中解码出危险。还指出将MARL通信结果迁移到连续控制的要求：消息所影响的决策必须仍可通过探索到达，并展示恢复方法。

Conclusion: Latent Telepathy 表明，复用冻结的自监督感知潜在可实现高效、可共享且接收者无需显式语义的机器人间通信，并给出向连续控制迁移的关键条件与修复思路。

Abstract: In a decentralized multi-robot team under partial observability, the fact that decides a robot's next action is often visible only to a teammate. Existing decentralized methods communicate kinematic information, such as position or planned trajectory, which cannot convey what the teammate perceives. Learned communication in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) can carry perceptual content, but the resulting messages are task-coupled and opaque. We propose Latent Telepathy. Each robot broadcasts the perceptual latent vector it already computes for its own use, the output of an encoder trained with a self-supervised joint-embedding predictive objective, frozen, and shared across the team. A teammate learns to act on it from task reward alone. Because the encoder already runs for perception, the message costs no additional computation and a single compact vector of bandwidth. Because the encoder is frozen before any policy is trained, the message means the same thing to every robot, and the receiving robot is never told what it means. We evaluate Latent Telepathy with a content-controlled protocol in which bandwidth, latency, topology and receiver are held fixed and only the message content varies. Broadcasting the latent lets a navigator avoid an occluded hazard in 99.7% of episodes, matching a noiseless hand-designed message. Position and trajectory messages remain at chance, and the raw camera image, 186 times wider, is less reliable than the compressed latent. The result holds from a discrete gridworld to rendered pixels under continuous velocity control, and the encoder decodes the hazard from a physical robot's camera in 102 of 102 live decisions. We also identify a requirement for porting MARL communication results to continuous control, that the decision a message informs must remain reachable by exploration, and show how to restore it.

</details>


### [149] [Scenario MPC with STL Specifications and Pareto-Based Feasibility Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23263)
*Tianhao Wu,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将可行性修复视为帕累托优化的模型预测控制框架，用于含随机且不可控智能体的多智能体系统，并在随机环境下对信号时序逻辑（STL）违反率提供概率性证书，在自动驾驶场景中验证了其安全恢复可行控制的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多智能体应用（如自动驾驶）常涉及随机、不可控智能体，而现有STL控制合成多假设智能体协作且动力学已知；近期鲁棒/概率方法要么只认证严格满足STL约束而不处理可行性恢复，要么以自车目标松弛不可行约束，难以显式刻画智能体间目标权衡。

Method: 提出基于模型预测控制（MPC）的框架，将可行性修复建模为帕累托优化问题，以显式表征智能体目标之间的权衡；同时针对随机且不可控智能体，给出STL违反率的概率性证书，从形式上量化不确定性。

Result: 在两个自动驾驶场景中评估该框架；结果表明框架能够恢复可行控制，并表现出安全行为。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将可行性修复与帕累托权衡结合，并辅以STL违反率概率保证，可在随机不可控多智能体环境中实现更安全、可行的控制，适用于自动驾驶等实际场景。

Abstract: Temporal logic is a formal language for reasoning about system behaviors over time. Signal temporal logic (STL), in particular, has been used to encode spatio-temporal requirements for control synthesis in multi-agent systems, often under the assumption that agents are cooperative and their dynamics are known. However, real-world multi-agent applications, such as autonomous driving, typically involve stochastic and uncontrollable agents. Recent work explored robust control with worst-case or probabilistic formulations, but remains limited in that it either (1) certifies strict satisfaction of STL constraints without addressing feasibility recovery, or (2) relaxes infeasible constraints with ego-centric objectives. In this paper, we propose a model predictive control (MPC) framework that treats feasibility repair as a Pareto optimization problem to explicitly characterize tradeoffs among agent objectives. We further provide a probabilistic certificate on STL violation rate to formally quantify uncertainty under stochastic and uncontrollable agents. The proposed framework is evaluated on two autonomous driving scenarios. Results show that the framework recovers feasible control with demonstrated safe behaviors.

</details>


### [150] [Robot World Models Are Not Invariant to How the Actions Are Written](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23252)
*Ahmed Karim,Leon Chlon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 动作参数化（绝对关节目标 vs 相对当前状态的增量）是机器人策略中常被当作工程细节的选择，但以动作为条件的潜在世界模型会静默继承它。论文表明这种继承会导致灾难性失效：用一种参数化训练模型、用另一种参数化输入同一命令轨迹时，模型检索、目标条件动作选择和未来预测均崩溃。修复核心是对两种编码做目标平均，并对方向值预测增加不一致惩罚；但在 PushT 上仅平均不能修复该轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中动作参数化的选择常被视为实现细节，但世界模型会隐含地继承该选择，而视觉模型的不变性研究主要关注裁剪、抖动和相机位姿，未审计动作通道的参数化。作者希望区分真正的有效重参数化、有损摘要或传感器替换，并揭示动作编码不一致对潜在动力学模型的破坏性影响。

Method: 在三个机器人数据集和两种形态上，用某一动作参数化（绝对关节目标或相对增量）训练潜在动力学模型，再向模型输入用另一参数化表达的相同命令轨迹。提出判别测试以区分有效重参数化与有损摘要/传感器替换。修复方案是比较两种编码的平均位置：目标平均与输出平均；对方向值预测指出输出平均因归一化均值可能低于轨道所有成员，因此采用目标平均并加入不一致惩罚，并评估潜在 rollout 中的最坏情况。

Result: 检索性能退化 2.6–13.4 倍；目标条件动作选择从 53% 降至 15%；在 PushT 上，对同一未来的两种信念近乎正交（cos=0.067，最坏 -0.377）。给定关节输入时，两种编码可互相重构，R²=0.996，说明并非信息丢失；提出的四个轴中测试拒绝三个。目标平均本身可恢复任务性能，输出平均不适用于方向预测。目标平均后最坏情况一致性为 0.78，不一致惩罚将其提升到 0.995；在潜在 rollout 中，这决定最坏情况是侵蚀还是保持。PushT 上仅靠平均不能修复该轴。

Conclusion: 动作参数化是世界模型动作通道中被忽视却关键的不变性缺陷，不应被静默继承；需将动作参数化纳入不变性审计。修复需在目标层面平均，并显式处理方向值预测的尾部不一致；PushT 的结果进一步表明，仅平均不足以修复所有场景，仍需额外机制。

Abstract: A robot policy is trained with one of two action parameterizations: absolute joint targets, or deltas relative to the current state. The choice is a live engineering decision in robot learning, and a world model conditioned on actions inherits it silently. We show the inheritance is catastrophic. A latent dynamics model trained on one parameterization and handed the identical commanded trajectory written in the other collapses: retrieval degrades by 2.6-13.4x across three robot datasets and two morphologies, goal-conditioned action selection falls from 53% to 15%, and on PushT the two beliefs about the same future are near-orthogonal (cos = 0.067, worst case -0.377), so the predictor does not degrade gracefully, it answers a different question. This is not a distribution-shift artifact in the usual sense: the two encodings are mutually reconstructible at R^2 = 0.996 given the joint input, so no information is lost, and we give the test that separates a valid re-parameterization from a lossy summary or a sensor swap. The test rejected three of the four axes we proposed. The defect lives in the action channel, which the invariance literature for visual models does not examine: work there concerns crops, jitter and camera pose, while the parameterization of the commands goes unaudited. The repair is averaging over the two encodings, and where it goes matters. Averaging the objective restores task performance by itself; averaging the outputs, safe for probabilities by concavity, is not available for direction-valued prediction, where the normalized mean can score below every member of the orbit. What objective-averaging leaves behind is the tail: worst-case agreement stays at 0.78, a disagreement penalty closes it to 0.995, and over a latent rollout it is the difference between a worst case that erodes and one that holds. On PushT, averaging alone does not repair the axis.

</details>


### [151] [Probabilistic Scene Graphs: Hierarchical Representation and Real-time System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23144)
*Waqas Ali,Michele Antonazzi,Timon Homberger,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Patric Jensfelt,Yixi Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出概率场景图PSG，将场景图表为可能图的后验，分解为离散图结构与连续空间状态；用分层高斯HGG实例化空间 grounding，并在建图管线中传播不确定性，在六类数据集上实现传感器速率、近恒定内存、SOTA物体精度和零样本图对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图虽提供语义丰富且分层的机器人感知表示，但未将不确定性作为显式信念维护，也未在构图与精化操作中传播；几何通常依赖单独构建的度量地图，而非由场景图直接承载。

Method: PSG泛化传统场景图，表示实体、关系、语义属性离散结构与空间 grounding 连续状态的后验，并对两者维护不确定性；几何由节点直接携带，度量地图按需从图导出。实例化HGG：对象基元为全协方差高斯，采用Normal-Inverse-Wishart信念，递归参数化形成细化表面的几何图。建图管线以纯图粗到细对齐比较节点信念，并用嵌套EM与因子图优化联合精化位姿、对象参数和内部几何。

Result: 在室内RGB-D、室外LiDAR及跨模态部署等六个数据集上，HGG以传感器速率运行，内存近恒定，并取得SOTA物体精度与零样本图对齐。

Conclusion: PSG与HGG证明可将概率信念贯穿场景图构建与精化，使几何由场景图直接表达，从而实现高效、准确且跨模态鲁棒的机器人感知表示。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide semantically rich and hierarchical representations for robot perception. However, existing systems do not maintain uncertainty as an explicit belief or propagate it through the operations that construct and refine the graph. We introduce Probabilistic Scene Graph (PSG), a generalization of the conventional scene graph that represents a posterior over possible graphs, factorized into a discrete graph structure of entities, relations, and semantic attributes, and continuous states that ground them spatially, with uncertainty maintained over both components. Geometry is carried directly by the nodes rather than selected from a separately constructed metric map, so a metric map, where needed, follows from the graph rather than preceding it. We instantiate PSG's probabilistic spatial grounding with hierarchical graphs of Gaussians (HGG): each object primitive is represented by a full-covariance Gaussian under a Normal-Inverse-Wishart belief, and the same parametrization applied recursively within a node yields a geometry graph that resolves its surface at finer resolution. We then build a mapping pipeline that preserves these beliefs throughout graph construction and refinement: a purely graph-based coarse-to-fine alignment registers observations by comparing node beliefs, while a nested Expectation-Maximization and factor-graph optimization jointly refines poses, object parameters, and internal geometry. Across six datasets spanning indoor RGB-D, outdoor LiDAR, and cross-modality deployment, HGG operates at sensor rate with near-constant memory and achieves state-of-the-art object accuracy and zero-shot graph alignment.

</details>


### [152] [AquaCap: A Training-Free Underwater Embodied Agent with Code-as-Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23133)
*Xiaoshi Li,Yule Xu,Chunghiu Kong,Yizhou Zhou,Yang Liu,Hao Yang,Zihao Huang,Yunxiao Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AquaCap：一种免训练的 Code-as-Policy 框架，用于水下自主导航与操作，通过双层智能体、结构化感知和失败感知记忆实现无需任务特定训练或参数更新的在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型虽推动具身智能发展，但依赖大规模交互数据；水下数据采集成本高且稀缺，限制其应用。因此需要一种无需大量水下训练数据的自主水下导航与操作方法。

Method: AquaCap 采用双层智能体，将任务指令与环境观测转化为条件感知规划和可执行控制程序；结构化感知在退化水下条件下提供语义、几何和可靠性感知观测；失败感知记忆诊断失败动作，支持闭环重规划与代码修订，从而实现在线适应，无需任务特定训练或参数更新。

Result: 在仿真中达到 66.43% 的成功率；真实世界实验使用 ROV 实现了自主抓取与物体运输，包括对受水动力扰动而发生位移目标的操控。

Conclusion: AquaCap 展示了免训练代码即策略框架在水下具身智能中的可行性，可缓解水下数据稀缺问题，并支持复杂扰动环境下的自主导航与操作。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action models have stimulated growing interest in underwater embodied intelligence. However, their reliance on large-scale interaction data limits their applicability underwater, where data collection is costly and scarce. To address this challenge, we present AquaCap, a training-free Code-as-Policy framework for autonomous underwater navigation and manipulation. AquaCap employs a dual-layer agent that translates task instructions and environmental observations into condition-aware plans and executable control programs. Structured perception then provides the agent with semantic, geometric, and reliability-aware observations under degraded underwater conditions. A failure-aware memory diagnoses unsuccessful actions and supports closed-loop replanning and code revision. This design enables online adaptation without task-specific training or parameter updates. AquaCap achieves a 66.43% success rate in simulation. Real-world experiments further demonstrate autonomous grasping and object transport with an ROV, including the manipulation of targets displaced by hydrodynamic disturbances.

</details>


### [153] [Tip Manipulation in Soft Everting Robots via Wall Retraction and Deployable Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23132)
*Nelson Badillo Perez,Niccolo Pagliarani,Matteo Cianchetti,Robert D. Howe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于壁面回缩的软体外翻机器人尖端操作与多工具部署策略，通过独立控制外壁膜流动和内部尾段生长，实现膜上器件在近尖端处的输送、暴露和重定位，并集成超轻褶皱充气手指，完成抓取、操作、检测和递送等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体外翻机器人虽能沿狭长受限路径连续生长，但主动交互通常局限于固定在尖端或附近的单一工具，无法按需重定位，且难以与柔软本体兼容。

Method: 设计基部滚轮组件独立计量外壁膜流动，并用尾部卷轴调节内部尾段生长；协调壁面与尾部驱动，使机器人长度与膜材料位置解耦。将TPU涂层尼龙制成、内置气密气囊的超轻褶皱充气手指集成到膜上，可在低压下大角度弯曲，运输时保持柔软。

Result: 高褶皱设计在50 kPa下弯曲约100度，阻塞力达1.9 N。系统实现21–550 g、16–200 mm家用物体自适应抓取，三维物体操作与堆叠，环境支撑伸展，狭小空间远端相机转动检测，以及可控顺序载荷递送。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了软体生长机器人在杂乱曲折环境中的具身、可逆尖端操作，扩展了其主动交互与多工具部署能力。

Abstract: Soft everting robots can traverse long, confined paths by continuously growing, yet active interaction remains limited to a single tool fixed at or near the tip, unable to be repositioned on demand and difficult to reconcile with the robot's soft body. We introduce a tip-manipulation and multi-tool deployment strategy for soft everting robots based on wall retraction, implemented with a base roller assembly that independently meters membrane flow in the outer wall while a tail spool regulates growth in the internal tail section. Coordinated wall and tail actuation decouples robot length from membrane-material position, enabling membrane-mounted devices to be transported, exposed, and repositioned at selected locations near the distal tip. We pair this capability with ultralight pleated inflatable fingers integrated into the membrane, fabricated from TPU-coated nylon with an internal airtight bladder. The fingers achieve large bending at low pressures (approximately 100 degrees at 50 kPa in high-pleat designs) and generate blocking forces up to 1.9 N, while remaining limp during transport. The system demonstrates adaptive grasping across diverse household objects (21 to 550 g; 16 to 200 mm), three-dimensional object manipulation and stacking, environmentally braced extension, distal camera panning for confined-space inspection, and controlled sequential payload delivery. These results enable embodied and reversible tip manipulation for soft growing robots in cluttered and tortuous environments.

</details>


### [154] ["Dear LLaVA, Please Drive": A Depth-Aware Vision-Language Agent for Closed-Loop Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22925)
*Sebastian Berger,Katharina Winter,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于指令式学习与LoRA的参数高效VLM导航方法，无需标注轨迹即可实现自然语言驱动的移动机器人自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航框架多依赖模仿学习，需要大量标注轨迹数据，限制可扩展性与鲁棒性；VLM具备丰富上下文理解和大规模预训练带来的强泛化能力，因此适合作为导航基础。

Method: 采用指令式学习范式微调预训练VLM，通过优化可微几何代价场而非标注轨迹来学习无碰撞路径；仅使用立体深度观测；构建统一的端到端自然语言驱动机器人控制流水线，共享VLM主干并配合任务特定LoRA模块，连接语义目标选择与低层轨迹规划。

Result: 在未见环境中取得有竞争力的SPL，更新参数少于模型总参数的1%；真实世界定性实验验证了仿真到现实的泛化能力，且无需真实世界数据微调即可稳定规划路径。

Conclusion: 该方法为在移动机器人上部署VLM智能体提供了实用途径，可在无需大规模标注轨迹数据的情况下实现高层语义导航。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a compelling foundation for reasoning-driven mobile navigation, offering rich contextual understanding and strong generalization from large-scale pretraining. Most existing navigation frameworks rely on imitation learning and therefore require substantial labeled trajectory data, limiting their scalability and robustness. In this work, we propose a parameter-efficient approach to fine-tune a pretrained VLM for autonomous navigation using an Imperative Learning paradigm. By optimizing against differentiable geometric cost fields rather than labeled trajectories, our model learns to generate collision-free paths exclusively from stereoscopic depth observations. We introduce a unified end-to-end navigation pipeline for natural-language-driven robotic control. This system leverages a shared VLM backbone with task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules, effectively bridging the gap from semantic target selection to low-level trajectory planning. Our approach achieves competitive Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) in unseen environments while updating less than 1% of the model's total parameters. Qualitative real-world experiments validate sim-to-real generalization and stable path planning without fine-tuning on real-world data. These results highlight a practical approach for deploying VLM-based agents on mobile robots, enabling high-level semantic navigation without the prohibitive requirement for large-scale, labeled trajectory data.

</details>


### [155] [H-VLA: Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Model with Key-Action Reasoning and Motion Planning in a Unified Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22895)
*Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Zirui Zheng,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Inseop Chung,Hyun-woong Cho,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-VLA是一种分层VLA框架，将高层关键动作推理与低层密集运动生分解耦，并采用统一相机中心动作空间，从而提升机器人在空间变化下的操作泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法多直接将语言和视觉观察映射为密集动作，这会削弱预训练VLM的语义推理能力，因为VLM主要面向视觉-语言理解而非低层控制；同时，这类方法在物体位置、场景布局、机器人本体和相机视角等空间变化下表现脆弱。

Method: 提出H-VLA：1）关键动作模型预测下一操作子目标作为关键动作；2）运动规划模型以关键动作为条件生成未来密集动作；3）设计统一相机中心动作空间，实现跨数据集、跨本体和跨视角的一致表示；4）采用两阶段训练，预训练强调关键动作推理，微调强调密集运动生成。

Result: 在SimplerEnv上，H-VLA达到Google Robot visual matching 91%、Google Robot variant aggregation 84%、WidowX visual matching 81%。在Agilex真实机器人任务中，相比最强基线，在分布内、分布外位置和分布外场景/物体设置下分别提升10、47和16个百分点。

Conclusion: H-VLA通过分层解耦关键动作推理与运动生成，并结合统一相机中心动作空间和两阶段训练，有效增强了空间变化下的鲁棒性与泛化能力，验证了该框架在机器人操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation, but many existing methods still rely on direct mappings from language and visual observations to dense actions. This formulation can weaken the semantic reasoning capability inherited from pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which are mainly optimized for visual-linguistic understanding rather than low-level control, and becomes fragile under spatial variations, including changes in object positions, scene layouts, robot embodiments, and camera viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose H-VLA, a hierarchical VLA framework that decouples high-level key-action reasoning from low-level motion generation. H-VLA combines a Key-Action Model for predicting a key-action as the next manipulation subgoal, a Motion Planning Model for generating dense future actions conditioned on the predicted key-action, and a Unified Camera-Centric Action Space for consistent representation across datasets, embodiments, and viewpoints. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy that emphasizes key-action reasoning during pre-training and dense motion generation during fine-tuning. Experiments show that H-VLA achieves strong performance on SimplerEnv, reaching 91% on Google Robot visual matching, 84% on Google Robot variant aggregation, and 81% on WidowX visual matching. On Agilex real-robot tasks, H-VLA improves over the strongest baseline by 10, 47, and 16 percentage points under in-distribution, out-of-distribution position, and out-of-distribution scene/object settings, respectively.

</details>


### [156] [Stable and Efficient Real-World Online VLA Post-Training via Asynchronous Replay-Anchored Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22888)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiajin Zhang,Bin Zhu,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPolicy 是一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的异步在线后训练框架，通过回放锚定评论家和演员更新，在真实机器人任务中实现高效稳定的策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在线后训练需要高效利用机器人交互，并从持续收集的经验中可靠提升策略。现有方法可能计算开销大、依赖回放覆盖之外的动作价值估计，难以兼顾效率与稳定性。

Method: 提出异步 Replay-Anchored Policy improvement（RAPolicy），并行执行 rollout 与学习，并将评论家和演员更新锚定在回放行为上。评论家从记录动作中学习 chunk 级价值，构建 Bellman 目标时无需预测下一动作，降低计算量和对回放覆盖外动作价值估计的依赖；一步 flow 演员复用 rollout 期间存储的初始噪声，通过优势加权条件似然学习，直接监督执行所用动作映射。

Result: 在真实世界四个单任务设置和一个联合五任务设置中评估，在线训练预算仅 1–2 小时。从每任务仅 10 条演示微调得到的策略出发，RAPolicy 快速适应新单任务，平均成功率达 86.3%；联合多任务设置中总体成功率从 52% 提升到 88%，同时保持已可靠任务性能并改善较弱能力。

Conclusion: RAPolicy 在总体任务成功率上显著优于基线，所需人工干预更少，表现出稳定的策略改进和高在线训练效率，验证了异步回放锚定策略改进对 VLA 模型在线后训练的有效性。

Abstract: Online post-training of vision-language-action (VLA) models requires efficient use of robot interaction and reliable policy improvement from continually collected experience. We propose asynchronous Replay-Anchored Policy improvement (RAPolicy), a framework that performs rollout and learning concurrently while grounding both critic and actor updates in replayed behavior. The critic learns chunk-level values from recorded actions and constructs Bellman targets without predicting next actions, reducing computation and dependence on action-value estimates outside replay coverage. The one-step flow actor reuses the initial noise stored during rollout and learns through advantage-weighted conditional likelihood, directly supervising the action mapping used for execution. We evaluate RAPolicy across four single-task settings and one joint five-task setting in the real world, with online training budgets of only 1--2 hours. Starting from policies fine-tuned on just 10 demonstrations per task, RAPolicy rapidly adapts to new single tasks and achieves an average 86.3% success rate. In the joint multi-task setting, RAPolicy improves overall success rate from 52% to 88% while preserving performance on already reliable tasks and improving weaker capabilities. Overall, RAPolicy substantially outperforms the baselines in aggregate task success while requiring fewer human interventions, demonstrating stable policy improvement and high online training efficiency. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [157] [Barrier Certificate Synthesis for Non-Polynomial Robotic Dynamics via Polynomial Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22885)
*Shivam Chaubey,Francesco Verdoja,Shankar Deka,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出精确多项式提升方法，将非多项式机器人动力学转化为带代数约束的多项式增广系统，用于联合障碍证书合成与采样数据安全滤波，在协调转弯和平面多旋翼模型上提高认证覆盖率、降低成本，并减少闭环干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人安全运行要求轨迹在允许控制输入下保持在给定安全集内。障碍证书可认证安全集内的受控不变区域，而平方和优化能系统合成此类证书，但直接应用要求多项式动力学，无法处理三角函数等常见机器人非线性。

Method: 采用精确多项式提升，以多项式增广动力学替代非多项式动力学，并加入提升诱导的代数约束，不近似地保留非线性几何。构建提升域联合障碍合成，计算证书及状态反馈控制见证；设计零阶保持实现的采样数据安全滤波器；并将样本引导逐次障碍法适配到提升表示以比较不同合成框架。

Result: 在协调转弯和平面多旋翼模型上，精确提升提升了两类方法的认证覆盖率。相同样本量下，提升逐次障碍合成以更少障碍和更低计算成本获得更高覆盖率；提升联合障碍合成比最细测试分段分辨率覆盖率更高、计算成本更低。闭环实验中，安全滤波器在所有评估轨迹上保持可行性与安全性，降低空间保守性，并对两模型减少干预。

Conclusion: 精确提升使平方和障碍证书合成无需近似即可扩展到非多项式机器人动力学，并能跨合成框架持续获益，实现更高效、更低保守性的安全保障。

Abstract: Safe operation of robotic systems requires trajectories to remain within a prescribed safe set under admissible control inputs. Barrier certificates provide such guarantees by certifying a controlled-invariant region within that set. Sum-of-squares optimization offers a systematic way to synthesize such certificates, but its direct application requires polynomial dynamics, excluding common robotic nonlinearities, including trigonometric terms. We address this limitation using exact polynomial lifting, which replaces non-polynomial dynamics with polynomial-augmented dynamics subject to lifting-induced algebraic constraints, preserving nonlinear geometry without approximation. We formulate lifted-domain joint barrier synthesis that computes a certificate with a state-feedback control witness and develop a sampled-data safety filter for zero-order-hold implementation. To assess whether the benefits of lifting persist across synthesis frameworks, we also adapt a sample-guided successive-barrier method to the lifted representation. On coordinated-turn and planar multirotor models, exact lifting improves certified coverage in both methods: at matched sample sizes, lifted successive-barrier synthesis achieves higher coverage with fewer barriers and lower computational cost, while lifted joint barrier synthesis provides higher coverage and lower computational cost than the finest tested piecewise resolution. In closed-loop experiments, the safety filter maintains feasibility and safety across all evaluated trajectories, reduces spatial conservativeness, and requires less intervention for both models.

</details>


### [158] [A Reconfigurable Dual-Opposition Architecture for Single-Hand Assembly and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22871)
*William Su,Yunosuke Nakamura,Yixiao Wang,Yitong Li,Mingrui Yu,Huanan Qi,Boyuan Liang,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构双对掌架构，用四个独立驱动手指组成两组虚拟手指对掌，并通过可重构手掌控制其相对构型，从而在单手内同时实现双部件抓取装配与单物体协调操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手内装配受限于需同时保持对两个分离部件的抓取，并在单手内控制两者的相对运动；现有手爪难以兼顾双物体抓取与灵巧操作。

Method: 设计四指独立驱动、两组虚拟手指对掌和可重构手掌；建立手指与手掌运动学模型，以及以物体尺寸为条件的工作空间公式；表征指关节运动与手掌跟踪；通过抓取、对齐、紧固、按压任务演示手内装配；通过消融实验验证手指外展/内收和手掌重构的重要性；在仿真中训练策略并进行外部扰动微调。

Result: 仿真中，所提手在直径40–230 mm的连续球体旋转任务上平均成功率为98.6%，LEAP Hand为73.8%；经扰动策略微调后，在多方向外部扰动下成功率为92.8%，LEAP Hand为45.2%；硬件演示了使用仿真训练策略对不同尺寸物体进行手内旋转。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该架构能够在单手内同时支持两个分离部件的装配和单一物体的协调操作。

Abstract: In-hand assembly is constrained by the need to maintain grasps on two separate parts while controlling their relative motion within a single hand. To enable both in-hand assembly and manipulation, we present a reconfigurable dual-opposition architecture. Specifically, to support simultaneous grasping of two parts and coordinated in-hand manipulation, four independently actuated fingers are organized into two virtual finger (VF) oppositions, with their relative configuration controlled by a reconfigurable palm. To describe hand motion and simultaneous two-object grasping configurations, a kinematic model of the fingers and palm and an object-size-conditioned workspace formulation are built. To further evaluate motion performance and assembly capability, finger-joint motion and palm tracking are characterized, and in-hand assembly is demonstrated through tasks involving grasping, alignment, fastening, and pressing. Ablation experiments further demonstrate the importance of finger abduction/adduction and palm reconfiguration for successful in-hand assembly. In simulation, the proposed hand achieves a mean continuous sphere rotation success rate of 98.6% over diameters of 40-230 mm, compared with 73.8% for the LEAP Hand. After policy fine-tuning with external disturbances, the proposed hand achieves 92.8% success under disturbances from multiple directions, compared with 45.2% for the LEAP Hand. Hardware demonstrations further show in-hand rotation of objects of different sizes using policies trained in simulation. Together, these results show that the proposed architecture supports both assembly of two separately held parts and coordinated manipulation of a single object within one hand.

</details>


### [159] [PileBelief: Persistent Physical State for Interaction-Driven World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22858)
*Hongyi Lin,Song Zhang,Haiquan Liu,Yang Liu,Jinhua Zhao,Xiaobo Qu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PileBelief 是一种交互驱动的持久世界模型，用于部分可观测挖掘任务，通过保留可见表面之外的物理证据，实现多步地形变化与动作结果预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挖掘中每次铲挖都会重塑地形并影响后续动作，机器人需在执行前预判动作后果；但局部观测无法充分揭示底层支撑与物料条件，仅依赖当前观测难以进行可靠的多步预测与动作选择。

Method: 提出 PileBelief，将观测条件化的物理先验、世界寻址的形变记忆和物理响应记忆相结合；采用动作对齐读取与门控残差修正来改进地形变化和结果预测。部署权重固定，已完成交互更新实测信念，假设动作则推进独立的想象状态。

Result: 相比仅使用当前观测的基线，PileBelief 将五步联合预测误差降低 10.8%，将离线动作选择遗憾降低 65.5%；在 Newton/MPM 和真实挖掘数据集上，地形变化与铲斗体积预测均得到改善。

Conclusion: 持久物理信念是机器人持续重塑环境的世界模型的有效表示，能够在底层土壤状态未知时，仅凭局部观测支持多步预测与候选动作排序。

Abstract: World models allow robots to anticipate action consequences before execution. This capability is especially valuable in excavation, where each scoop reshapes the terrain and affects subsequent actions. Local observations, however, cannot fully reveal the underlying support and material conditions. We present PileBelief, an interaction-driven persistent world model for partially observed excavation that retains physical evidence beyond the visible surface. It combines an observation-conditioned physical prior with world-addressed deformation memory and physical-response memory. Action-aligned reads and gated residual corrections refine terrain-change and outcome predictions. With deployment weights fixed, completed interactions update measured belief, while hypothetical actions advance a separate imagined state. Compared with a current-observation-only baseline, PileBelief reduces five-step joint prediction error by 10.8% and offline action-selection regret by 65.5%. Experiments on Newton/MPM and real excavation datasets further demonstrate improved terrain-change and bucket-volume prediction. Our method enables multi-step prediction and candidate-action ranking from local observations, even when the underlying soil state is unknown. These results identify persistent physical belief as a useful representation for world models of environments that robots continually reshape.

</details>


### [160] [SmoLSTM: A Compact Vision-Language-Action Model with Recurrent Memory that Persists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22854)
*Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Parsa Mastouri Kashani,Connor Gäde,Matthias Kerzel,Philipp Allgeuer,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter,Jae Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SmoLSTM：用循环状态记忆整个 episode，将冻结的 SmolVLM 与矩阵记忆 LSTM 结合，以 O(1) 状态存储解决视觉语言动作模型仅依赖当前观测的记忆不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见 VLA 仅从当前观测预测动作，在物体遮挡或视觉相同物体场景中缺少 episode 历史会带来歧义；扩大观测窗口会把历史长度变成超参数，并使每步计算成本随窗口增长。

Method: 将冻结的 256M 参数 SmolVLM 主干与矩阵记忆 LSTM 控制层耦合；观测 token 与动作 query 统一在单个因果流中，且整个 episode 中从不重置循环状态，因此循环状态存储为 O(1)。使用 flow-matching 动作头在每个控制步预测 10 个末端执行器位姿增量与夹爪命令的动作块。

Result: 单一策略在 140 个任务、7,461 条演示上联合训练，并在留出初始状态上评估；在 LIBERO-Mem 上达到 85.1% 子目标覆盖率和 77.5% 全任务成功率，可训练参数仅 0.04B，超过基准自身的 object-centric 基线及近期记忆方法。若每个控制步重置循环状态，全任务成功率降至 7.0%。同一模型在标准 LIBERO 上平均成功率 79.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，跨决策携带的循环上下文对策略性能至关重要；SmoLSTM 以高效 O(1) 记忆在记忆型与标准 LIBERO 基准上均取得强性能，并显著优于无循环状态或仅依赖当前观测的替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models often predict actions from only the current observation, which can leave tasks involving object occlusion or visually identical objects ambiguous without episode history. The usual countermeasure, widening the observation window, turns the horizon into a hyperparameter and lets per-step cost grow with it. We instead capture the episode in a recurrent state. SmoLSTM couples a frozen 256M-parameter SmolVLM backbone to a matrix-memory LSTM control layer in which observation tokens and action queries are unified in a single causal stream that is never reset throughout the entire episode. Recurrent-state storage is therefore O(1) in episode length. A flow-matching action head predicts chunks of 10 end-effector pose deltas and gripper commands at each control step. Our single policy, trained jointly on 7,461 demonstrations across 140 tasks and evaluated on held-out initial states, performs best, reaching 85.1% subgoal coverage and 77.5% full-task success on LIBERO-Mem with 0.04B trainable parameters, surpassing both the benchmark's own object-centric baseline and recent memory-based approaches. Resetting the recurrent state at every control step reduces full-task success to 7.0%, showing that the trained policy relies on context carried between decisions. The same model achieves 79.6% average success on standard LIBERO.

</details>


### [161] [Physical-Touch Observability from Wrist Wrench in Granular Scooping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22852)
*Hongyi Lin,Song Zhang,Xubo Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 腕部力/力矩可观测铲掘最终收集体积，且依赖正确配对的行动-地形交互；在6700次真实铲掘中显著降低体积预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采矿与土方装载、铲掘仍常依赖熟练人工，预接触RGB-D只能感知表面几何，无法感知交互中的阻力、压实、工具啮合与载荷转移，因此需要利用交互触觉信息提升自主性。

Method: 提出“物理触觉可观测性”（physical-touch observability），使用6,700次真实机器人铲掘、67种地形，在地形留出测试下，比较正确配对的当前铲斗六轴腕部F/T、预接触预测以及破坏对应关系的控制条件。

Result: 在回顾性60%序列边界，正确配对F/T使最终收集体积预测的平均绝对误差较Action-only降低14.2%，较跨地形错配F/T降低21.9%；工程化信号摘要可在不同模型架构中复现该结果。

Conclusion: 腕部力/力矩不仅是低层反馈信号，更可作为任务级感知模态，用于推断交互中的隐藏物理状态，为采矿等接触丰富任务的响应感知自主提供基础。

Abstract: Mining and earthmoving are important real-world deployment settings for embodied intelligence. Autonomous transport and driving systems have improved substantially, but loading and scooping still often depend on skilled human operators, exposing personnel and equipment to operational risk. For robotic scooping, pre-contact RGB-D sensing reveals surface geometry but not the resistance, compaction, tool engagement, or load transfer that emerge during interaction. We test whether the current scoop's six-axis wrist force/torque (F/T), or wrist wrench, contains information about final collected volume, and whether that information depends on the correctly paired action-terrain interaction. We call this property physical-touch observability. Using 6,700 real-robot scoops across 67 terrains, we evaluate correctly paired current-scoop F/T against pre-contact prediction and correspondence-breaking controls under terrain-held-out testing. At the retrospective 60% sequence boundary, correctly paired F/T reduces mean absolute error by 14.2% relative to Action-only and by 21.9% relative to cross-terrain mismatched F/T. Engineered signal summaries reproduce the result across model architectures. Together, these findings position wrist wrench not merely as a low-level feedback signal, but as a task-level perceptual modality through which embodied robots can infer hidden physical states during interaction, providing a foundation for response-aware autonomy in mining and other contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [162] [ForceRFT: Refining VLA Actions through Force-Guided Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22840)
*Yichen Wang,Chaoyang Zhang,Xuqi Su,Jun Ma,Haiyue Zhu,Xiaocong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceRFT：一种力引导残差强化学习框架，在冻结的SmolVLA力条件先验上，用轻量残差actor结合人类纠正与自主任务结果，提升接触丰富操作任务的自主成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件VLA策略虽能响应接触，但仅用演示训练时恢复行为受演示覆盖限制，且无法从部署结果学习；人类纠正模仿虽提供恢复样本，却只匹配局部动作目标，未显式优化任务回报。

Method: ForceRFT由冻结的演示训练SmolVLA先验生成力条件动作块，轻量残差actor在执行动作块时利用腕部反馈细化末端位姿命令；决策时腕部力/力矩、其时序变化及所选基座运动共同条件化残差修正与价值估计。人类纠正监督残差actor，验证过的自主转移训练双critic并支持对同一actor的价值引导更新；自举仅限自主片段，避免TD信用跨越人类干预边界。

Result: 在插头插入、环-钉装配和白板擦拭真实机器人实验中，ForceRFT自主成功率高于所评估的演示训练基线和残差模仿基线；与残差模仿对比支持价值引导残差优化，插头插入消融表明直接使用执行时腕部反馈的收益。

Conclusion: ForceRFT通过融合人类监督与自主任务结果，实现力引导的残差强化学习，有效提升接触丰富操作任务中的自主恢复与成功率。

Abstract: Force-conditioned vision-language-action (VLA) policies can respond to contact, but when trained solely on demonstrations, their recovery behavior may be limited by demonstration coverage, and they do not learn from deployment outcomes. Human corrective imitation provides additional recovery examples, but its objective matches local action targets without explicitly optimizing task return. We present ForceRFT, a force-guided residual reinforcement learning framework that learns contact-dependent corrections from human supervision and autonomous task outcomes. A frozen, demonstration-trained SmolVLA-based prior generates force-conditioned action chunks, while a lightweight residual actor refines individual end-effector pose commands using wrist feedback acquired during chunk execution. The decision-time wrist wrench, its temporal change, and the selected base motion condition both residual correction and value estimation. Human corrections supervise the residual actor, while verified autonomous transitions train the twin critics and support value-guided updates to the same actor. Bootstrapping is restricted to autonomous segments, preventing TD credit from crossing human-intervention boundaries. Real-robot experiments on plug insertion, ring-on-peg assembly, and whiteboard wiping show higher autonomous success rates than the evaluated demonstration-trained and residual-imitation baselines. Comparisons with residual imitation support value-guided residual optimization, while plug-insertion ablations indicate the benefit of direct execution-time wrist feedback.

</details>


### [163] [Whole-Body UMI: Transferring UMI Manipulation Skills to Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Real-Time Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22829)
*Yuxuan Nai,Leixin Chang,Liangjing Yang,Shuo Yang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WB-UMI：一种任务无关、实时、以末端执行器为条件的全身运动生成器，通过共享末端执行器接口，将全身协调学习与任务语义学习解耦，从而把 UMI 数据采集范式扩展到人形机器人全身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身示教主要依赖遥操作，成本高、难扩展；UMI 虽可扩展，但仅提供末端执行器轨迹，无法充分决定全身协调，因此不足以直接用于全身示教采集。

Method: WB-UMI 通过共享末端执行器接口解耦全身协调与任务语义：扩散策略从原生 UMI 示教中学习任务语义，WB-UMI 则从重定向运动捕捉中独立学习全身协调，无需身体跟踪器，也无需任务特定采集中的图像-全身配对示教。实际部署采用异步分层框架，集成扩散策略、运动生成器与全身控制器，并加入延迟补偿和实测状态反馈。

Result: 在 G1 真机实验中，实现四个任务的实时闭环迁移：关抽屉成功率 90%，货架抓放 80%，抛球 30%，Loco-PnP 40%。

Conclusion: 结果表明该分层框架能有效将原生 UMI 技能迁移到人形机器人全身操作中。

Abstract: Collecting whole-body demonstrations for humanoid manipulation mostly relies on teleoperation, which is costly and hard to scale up. The Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides a scalable data collection paradigm, but end-effector trajectories alone underdetermine humanoid whole-body coordination, which is insufficient for whole-body demonstration collection. Therefore, we introduce Whole-Body UMI (WB-UMI), a task-agnostic, real-time and end-effector conditioned motion generator that decouples whole-body coordination learning from task semantics learning through a shared end-effector interface. A diffusion policy learns from native UMI demonstrations, while WB-UMI learns independently from retargeted motion capture, requiring no body trackers or paired image--whole-body demonstrations during task-specific data collection. In real deployment, an asynchronous hierarchy integrates the diffusion policy, motion generator, and a whole-body controller with latency compensation and measured-state feedback. Real-robot experiments on G1 support real-time closed-loop transfer across four tasks, achieving 90% success in drawer closing, 80% in shelf pick-and-place, 30% in ball toss, and 40% in Loco-PnP, which shows the effectiveness of this hierarchy in transferring native UMI skills to humanoid whole-body manipulation.

</details>


### [164] [Commonsense-Grounded Path Planning from Abstract Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22813)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出常识排序搜索 CoRS：利用 LLM/VLM 推断环境中未明说的潜在考量，将区域按机器人应避让程度排序，并以此代价驱动传统搜索，生成符合常识的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有路径规划方法通常只遵守预先写明的约束，但机器人在人群中作业时必须处理未明确说明的潜在考量，例如湿滑地面应绕行。

Method: 给定抽象指令（如“小心移动”）和各区域的视觉观测，CoRS 用 LLM 与 VLM 为每个区域推导潜在考量（如“湿地板湿滑，值得绕行”），再比较不同区域的考量以判断哪个更应避让（如“人群比湿地板更危险”），据此形成常识排序，并将其转化为代价用于常规搜索，从而始终返回有效路径。

Result: 作者构建了潜在考量下的规划基准，包含 3 个环境、1350 个问题、5 条指令和 3 个抽象层级。实验表明，CoRS 能发现未明说的考量，对值得绕行的区域进行避让，同时穿越其余区域；近期基于 LLM 的规划器未能实现该行为。

Conclusion: CoRS 表明，借助 LLM/VLM 的常识知识对区域进行常识排序，可让路径规划在抽象指令下遵守隐含的社会/环境考量，并保持传统搜索的有效性。

Abstract: We present \emph{commonsense ranked search} (CoRS), a novel path planner that turns an abstract instruction into a route that follows commonsense. While existing methods respect the considerations written down in advance, a robot working among people must follow those left unstated too, as with a wet floor that a worker avoids without being told. CoRS leverages large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) as commonsense knowledge to reason about these latent considerations in its planning.
