We introduce ORDER (Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning), a benchmark built on a fictitious world: a 342,069-token synthetic corpus defining a self-consistent physics that cannot appear in any model's pre-training data. ORDER pairs a 500-question knowledge test (ORDER-BENCH) with a harder compositional task, ORDER-SPATIAL: ordering objects for safe manipulation across both familiar and entirely novel scenes. GPT-4.1 without adaptation scores below chance on ORDER-SPATIAL (Kendall's tau = 0.441), showing its priors actively conflict with the invented physics. After continual pre-training, small models improve substantially on both familiar and novel scenes alike evidence of genuine world-model induction rather than memorization. We then carry this through to a robot pipeline: models that answer the knowledge test well often cannot produce valid, executable plans without a further skill-adaptation stage, after which small, fully offline models outperform GPT-4.1 even when GPT-4.1 is given retrieval access to the same rules (Kendall's tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606), on a full perception-to-execution loop demonstrated on a simulated iiwa7 arm with human-in-the-loop correction. Throughout, ORDER-SPATIAL performance, not knowledge-test accuracy is what predicts real plan quality.

</details>


### [200] [DeViGrasp: Robust Visual Mobile Grasping for Quadruped Manipulators under Degraded Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22278)
*Liang Zhou,Jiaming Su,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出退化视觉下的四足机械臂移动抓取基准 DeViGrasp-Bench 与教师-学生控制框架 DeViGrasp-Net，以提升不可靠视觉感知下的抓取推理与目标跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机械臂全身控制策略多在较可靠观测下开发，尚未系统研究遮挡、分割掩码丢失、深度噪声和目标定位抖动如何影响抓取推理与目标跟踪。

Method: 构建 DeViGrasp-Bench，包含受控视觉退化、已见/未见物体、多难度等级和复杂地形，并评估任务成功率、执行效率和动作平滑度。提出 DeViGrasp-Net：特权教师基于物体、机器人、末端执行器和任务状态关注离线抓取候选；可部署学生融合双视角分割深度观测，并通过 Target Hold Memory 与 Temporal Memory Attention 融合当前、记忆和恢复目标假设。

Result: DeViGrasp-Net 在退化等级、未见物体和复杂地形上优于 VBC，并在所有评估退化等级上超过适配的 DQ-Net。困难设置下成功率为 62.3%，分别比 VBC 和 DQ-Net 高 16.1 和 4.3 个百分点；Hard 设置下对 DQ-Net 的优势增至 10.5 个百分点。消融实验验证了抓取感知监督与可靠性感知时序记忆的互补收益。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对退化视觉设计的基准与可靠性感知时序记忆框架能有效提升四足机械臂移动抓取的成功率、鲁棒性和执行质量。

Abstract: Quadruped manipulators enable mobile grasping in complex environments, yet their whole-body control policies remain vulnerable to unreliable onboard visual perception. Existing methods are typically developed under relatively reliable observations and have not systematically examined how occlusion, segmentation-mask dropout, depth noise, and target-localization jitter affect grasp reasoning and target tracking. To address this gap, we introduce DeViGrasp-Bench, a benchmark for mobile grasping under degraded vision that incorporates controlled visual degradations, seen and unseen objects, multiple difficulty levels, and complex terrains, and evaluates task success, execution efficiency, and action smoothness. We further propose DeViGrasp-Net, a teacher--student framework that combines state-conditioned grasp reasoning with reliability-aware temporal target estimation. The privileged teacher attends to offline grasp candidates conditioned on object, robot, end-effector, and task states, while the deployable student fuses dual-view segmented-depth observations with current, memory, and recovery target hypotheses through Target Hold Memory and Temporal Memory Attention. DeViGrasp-Net outperforms VBC across degradation levels, unseen objects, and complex terrains, and surpasses an adapted DQ-Net across all evaluated degradation levels. Under the Difficult setting, it achieves a success rate of 62.3\%, improving upon VBC and DQ-Net by 16.1 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively; under the Hard setting, its margin over DQ-Net increases to 10.5 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of grasp-aware supervision and reliability-aware temporal memory.

</details>


### [201] [D3DWA: Adaptive Weight and Prediction-Horizon for Dynamic Window Approach via Dueling Double Deep Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22276)
*Zahra Jooyandeh,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D3DWA，一种基于D3QN的自适应DWA框架，联合选择评价权重和预测时域，在仿真和真实机器人中均成功到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWA广泛用于局部导航，但性能受限于预固定参数。预测时域应随局部自由空间自适应：开阔区域宜用长时域以提高效率，狭窄区域宜用短时域以保持可行运动。

Method: D3DWA基于Dueling Double Deep Q-Network (D3QN)，在每个控制步从连续导航状态中联合选择DWA评价权重和预测时域，同时保留DWA的轨迹生成与碰撞检测。

Result: 在八个仿真环境（含未见布局）中，D3DWA均到达所有目标。真实机器人实验完成三种测试配置，包括仅调权重变体超时的受限场景。

Conclusion: 结果证明联合自适应评价权重和预测时域的有效性。更多材料见所附URL。

Abstract: The Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) is widely used for local navigation, but its performance depends strongly on parameters that are typically fixed before navigation. In particular, the appropriate prediction horizon can vary with local free space: longer horizons support efficient motion in open areas, whereas shorter horizons help preserve feasible motions in narrow or cluttered regions. This paper proposes D3DWA, an adaptive DWA framework based on a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network (D3QN), which jointly selects the DWA evaluation weights and prediction horizon from a continuous navigation state at every control step while retaining DWA's trajectory generation and collision checking. In eight simulated environments, including unseen layouts, D3DWA reached every goal. Real-robot experiments further showed that D3DWA completed all three tested configurations, including a constrained case in which the weights-only variant timed out. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly adapting the evaluation weights and prediction horizon. Additional material is available at this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Computationally efficient safe exploration in reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22919)
*Shreeram Murali,Shankar A. Deka,Dominik Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Nadaraya-Watson估计器的轻量级安全强化学习算法CoLSafe-MDP，用于约束MDP中的安全探索与优化，并在网格环境和火星地形数据上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中的强化学习需要在探索过程中提供安全保障，但常规RL算法缺乏此类保证；现有安全方法多依赖高斯过程，计算成本高，通常随数据量呈三次方增长。

Method: 采用Nadaraya-Watson估计器构建轻量级算法CoLSafe-MDP，对约束马尔可夫决策过程进行安全探索与优化；该估计器具有常数时间可扩展性，并提供估计值边界。

Result: CoLSafe-MDP在计算复杂度上显著优于基于GP的方法，从随数据点三次方增长改进为常数时间扩展；在网格环境和观测火星地形数据上进行了性能评估。

Conclusion: CoLSafe-MDP为约束MDP中的安全探索提供了一种计算轻量且带估计边界的替代方案，显示出相比GP类方法更好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in real-life applications requires safety guarantees during exploration. Typical reinforcement learning algorithms do not provide such guarantees, and many modifications that do rely on Gaussian processes (GPs), which have a large computational cost. We propose a computationally lightweight algorithm based on the Nadaraya-Watson estimator that safely explores and optimizes constrained Markov decision processes (MDPs). Our algorithm, \textsc{CoLSafe-MDP}, uses an estimator that scales in constant-time with bounds on the estimates, a significant improvement from its GP-based counterparts that scale cubically with the number of data points. We then evaluate its performance in a grid-based environment and on observational Martian terrain data.

</details>


### [203] [Industrial Kinematic Trajectory Model (IKTM): Coordinate-Free Autoregressive Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22173)
*Max Amiri,David Eyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无坐标工业轨迹生成模型IKTM，仅用速度与航向变化序列建模工业车辆运动，并在跨站点零样本场景下显著优于先验马尔可夫模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口、矿山、机场等工业环境的移动性仿真对物流、安全和通信规划很重要，但现有轨迹模型依赖绝对坐标、路网令牌或语义区域，这些表示具有站点特异性，不适合非结构化工业地形。

Method: IKTM是一种无坐标轨迹生成器，以运动学序列（速度与航向变化）表示工业车辆运动，不依赖绝对空间参考；采用自回归因果Transformer与概率混合头，并在先验无坐标马尔可夫模型基础上引入深度序列建模和显式持续时间条件信号。

Result: 在单站点训练、三未见过站点零样本评估；在所有四站点上，100个采样种子的每站点平均JS散度约0.035-0.050 bits（oracle长度持续时间目标协议）和约0.032-0.042 bits（完全零样本先验长度协议），OOD持续时间目标来自各留出站点经验长度分布或Site A先验时范围相似，均高于采样噪声下限<=0.0049 bits；按站点配对，oracle长度值比同1 Hz协议下先验马尔可夫模型复现低5.3-6.0倍；终止由持续时间条件控制而非空间条件，100%试验停止，长度跟踪误差+0.0 ± 0.0 s（100/100精确命中目标；N=100，T=0.2，未触碰的分布内测试集）。

Conclusion: IKTM表明无坐标运动学序列结合深度序列建模与持续时间条件可实现可迁移的工业轨迹生成，并支持零样本跨站点仿真；持续时间条件终止比空间终止更准确。

Abstract: Mobility simulation supports logistics, safety, and communications planning in industrial environments such as ports, mines, and airports. Existing trajectory models, however, rely on absolute coordinates, road-network tokens, or semantic zones: representations that are site-specific and not well suited to unstructured industrial terrain. We introduce the Industrial Kinematic Trajectory Model (IKTM), a coordinate-free trajectory generator that represents industrial vehicle motion through kinematic sequences (speed and heading change) with no absolute spatial reference. IKTM uses an autoregressive causal transformer with probabilistic mixture heads and extends our prior coordinate-free Markovian model with deep sequence modelling and an explicit duration-conditioning signal. Trained on one site and evaluated zero-shot on three unseen sites, it matches the small-turn shape of the empirical turn-rate distributions of held-out telematics; across all four sites, the per-site mean Jensen-Shannon divergence over 100 sampling seeds spans approximately 0.035-0.050 bits under an oracle-length duration-target protocol and approximately 0.032-0.042 bits under a fully zero-shot prior-length protocol, with similar ranges whether out-of-distribution (OOD) duration targets are drawn from each held-out site's empirical length distribution or from the Site A prior. Both protocols stay above the metric's sampling-noise floor (<=0.0049 bits). Paired by site, the oracle-length values are 5.3-6.0x lower than those of a re-implementation of our prior Markovian model under the same 1 Hz protocol. Termination is duration-conditioned rather than spatial: rollouts stop on 100% of trials with a length-tracking error of +0.0 +/- 0.0 s against the sampled target (100 of 100 exactly on target; N=100, T=0.2, untouched in-distribution test split).

</details>


### [204] [Correcting Learning-based Perception for Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22108)
*Yan Miao,Hussein Darir,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两步式运行时感知校正策略，利用感知契约原像刻画ML状态估计不确定性，并用风险启发式选择安全状态，从而提升基于视觉的ACC安全性且避免过度保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型感知在自主系统中很重要，但其失效边界难以刻画；错误感知会导致下游控制不安全或过度保守，因此需要校正ML状态估计的不确定性。

Method: 第一步离线计算：用感知契约的原像刻画ML模块状态估计所产生的不确定性。第二步运行时：使用风险启发式从不确定估计中选择特定状态来驱动控制决策。在基于Yolo和LaneNet的不同视觉自适应巡航控制（ACC）模块、不同天气与道路场景中进行大量仿真评估。

Result: 在45个原感知控制导致安全违规的ACC场景中，73%的场景经运行时感知校正后保持了安全；27%无法恢复，原因是感知契约原像构造不完全一致。校正策略不过度保守：被校正场景平均仅增加2.8%完成时间，且干预较温和。

Conclusion: 运行时感知校正能有效提升ML感知驱动控制的安全性，并在多数情况下避免安全违规且仅带来较小效率损失；其恢复能力受限于感知契约原像构造的完备性与一致性。

Abstract: Learning-enabled perception is important in many autonomous systems. Unlike traditional sensors, the boundary where ML perception does or does not work is poorly characterized. Incorrect perception can lead to unsafe or overtly conservative downstream control actions. In this paper, we propose a two-step strategy for correcting ML-based state estimation. First, an offline computation is used to characterize the uncertainties resulting from the ML module's state estimation, using preimages of perception contracts. Second, at runtime, a risk heuristic is used to choose particular states from the uncertain estimates to drive the control decisions. We perform extensive simulation-based evaluation of this runtime perception correction strategy on different vision-based adaptive cruise controllers (ACC modules), in different weather conditions, and road scenarios. Out of 45 ACC scenarios where the original perception-based control system using Yolo and LaneNet led to safety violations, in 73% of the scenarios, our runtime perception correction preserved safety; our method wouldn't be able to recover 27% of the scenarios where the construction of the preimages of perception contracts is not fully conformant. Further, our runtime perception correction strategy is not overly conservative---on the average only a 2.8% increase in completion time is experienced in the corrected scenarios, with mild interventions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Riemannian Density-Driven Optimal Control: Tangent-Space LQR for Second-Order Multi-Agent Systems on Curved Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22678)
*Kooktae Lee,Ruchika Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将密度驱动最优控制（D2OC）扩展到黎曼流形上的二阶多智能体系统，提出R-D2OC框架，实现流形几何下向指定空间分布的引导控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有D2OC主要针对欧氏域设计，未直接考虑智能体所处流形的内在几何结构，因此难以直接用于黎曼流形上的二阶多智能体分布控制。

Method: R-D2OC在每个智能体的切空间中通过对数映射构造局部分布目标，并以该目标的加权中心作为有限时域LQR的参考；控制律通过流形上的内在二阶动力学与平行传输执行，并采用滚动时域方案。理论分析给出局部曲率相关的近似界以量化切空间约化误差及目标偏差，并推导出在局部速度对齐条件下闭环目标函数下降的条件离散下降结果。

Result: 在三维椭球流形上的数值仿真实现了分布级控制，并经验性地支持了所提出的切空间近似界与离散下降结论。

Conclusion: R-D2OC为黎曼流形上二阶多智能体系统的密度驱动最优控制提供了有效扩展，但其目标偏差与闭环下降性质依赖于曲率相关近似及局部速度对齐等条件。

Abstract: Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) provides an effective framework for steering multi-agent systems toward prescribed spatial distributions. However, existing D2OC formulations are primarily developed for Euclidean domains and do not directly account for intrinsic manifold geometry. This paper extends D2OC to second-order multi-agent systems evolving on Riemannian manifolds. The proposed Riemannian D2OC (R-D2OC) constructs a local distribution objective in the tangent space of each agent through logarithmic maps and uses its weighted center as the reference for a finite-horizon LQR. The resulting control is executed on the manifold through intrinsic second-order dynamics and parallel transport within a receding-horizon scheme. We establish local curvature-dependent bounds that quantify the approximation introduced by the tangent-space reduction and characterize the resulting target bias. Furthermore, we derive a conditional discrete-descent result showing that the closed-loop objective decreases when a local velocity-alignment condition is satisfied. Numerical simulations on a 3D ellipsoidal manifold demonstrate distribution-level control and empirically support the proposed approximation and descent results.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [KerColle: Unlocking Fine-Grained GPU Concurrency in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22335)
*Anna Li,Christina Giannoula,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文针对VLA推理中GPU利用率低的问题，指出GPU的CTA调度器因硬件线程块分发器的队头阻塞，无法充分重叠VLM与动作头两个可异步并发的阶段。作者提出轻量级GPU调度框架KerColle，基于在线SM利用率和内核资源需求动态协同调度内核，以缓解队头阻塞并提升VLA推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型已成为下一代机器人基础模型，其推理吞吐需满足机器人控制频率要求。VLA推理由VLM和动作头两阶段组成，二者可解耦并跨独立机器人请求异步并发执行，但现有GPU调度框架难以应对其并发特征：不同机器人请求的独立阶段包含大量内核，运行时并行内核组合动态变化，静态或提前调度策略效果有限；动作头算子多为短时内核且数量众多，缺乏在线剖析或抢占机制的时间余量。

Method: 作者对四种SOTA VLA进行表征，发现GPU在VLA推理中严重未充分利用，根因是硬件线程块分发器的队头阻塞。为此提出KerColle：一种轻量级GPU调度框架，利用在线SM利用率和单个内核资源需求，智能且动态地协同调度内核，从而高效重叠两个执行阶段。其核心包括：(1) 缓解队头阻塞；(2) 将资源需求互补的内核进行协同调度。

Result: 在仿真环境下，跨两种GPU架构、针对四种SOTA VLA模型，KerColle实现了最高28%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: KerColle表明，通过面向VLA并发执行特征的动态内核协同调度，可有效缓解GPU硬件线程块分发器的队头阻塞并提升资源利用率，从而显著提高VLA推理吞吐，满足机器人控制速率需求。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as foundational models for next-generation robotics. High VLA inference throughput is critical for meeting the control-rate requirements of robots. VLA models comprise two phases, a vision-language model (VLM) and an action head, that can be decoupled and executed asynchronously and concurrently across independent robot requests. Through a detailed characterization of four state-of-the-art VLAs, we observe that GPUs are severely underutilized in VLA inference as the Cooperative Thread Array (CTA) scheduler of GPU is unable to fully overlap the two independent phases of VLA execution. We identify that this inefficiency is caused by head-of-line blocking in the hardware thread-block dispatcher. We demonstrate that prior scheduling frameworks do not address the challenges posed by VLA concurrency. First, the independent phases across different robot requests each comprise numerous kernels, and at any given time, there are different combinations of kernels that are executed in parallel. This makes static or ahead-of-time scheduling policies largely ineffective. Second, many of the action-head operators are short-running kernels and there are numerous such kernels. This leaves no headroom for online profiling or preemption-based mechanisms. To address these challenges, we present KerColle, a lightweight GPU scheduling framework that leverages online Streaming Multiprocessor (SM) utilization and individual kernel resource requirements to intelligently and dynamically co-schedule kernels to efficiently overlap the two phases of execution by (1) mitigating head-of-line blocking, and (2) co-scheduling kernels with complementary resource requirements. We demonstrate in simulation, across two GPU architectures, for 4 state-of-the-art VLA models, that KerColle delivers throughput gains of up to $28\%$.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [MoSAT: Human Motion Generation from Spatial Audio and Textual Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23797)
*Shuyang Xu,Zhiyang Dou,Yiduo Hao,Zekun Li,Liang Pan,Jingbo Wang,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu,Wenping Wang,Mingmin Zhao,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 论文研究空间音频与自然语言联合条件的人体动作合成新任务，提出STAM数据集、MoSAT潜空间流匹配框架和三模态评估器，实验取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体动作同时受外部声学事件和行为意图影响：空间音频传递环境线索并诱发或引导响应，文本指定期望动作及其执行方式。此前研究大多忽略了空间音频与自然语言联合条件的人体动作合成问题。

Method: 提出STAM数据集，包含与空间音频及详细文本标注配对的动作序列，其丰富词汇可精确细腻地描述人体动作。提出MoSAT，一种用于全身体动作生成的潜空间流匹配框架，在生成动作前通过分层交叉注意力联合条件化自然语言意图与方向性空间音频线索，从而增强动作的时间连贯性和语义对齐。设计三模态评估器以对该新任务进行全面评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，MoSAT取得SOTA性能，能够利用空间音频内在的动作塑造属性与文本语义，在多种场景下实现精确且多样的动作生成。

Conclusion: MoSAT验证了联合空间音频与自然语言条件用于人体动作合成的有效性，STAM数据集与三模态评估器为该新任务提供了数据支持和综合评估基准。

Abstract: Human motion is shaped by both external acoustic events and behavioral intent: spatial audio conveys environmental cues that elicit or guide a response, while text specifies the desired action and how it should be performed. In this paper, we study the novel task of human motion synthesis jointly conditioned on spatial audio and natural language, a problem that has been largely overlooked in previous research. To support this task, We introduce STAM, a dataset of motion sequences paired with spatial audio and detailed textual annotations whose rich vocabulary affords precise and nuanced specification of human motions. We further introduce MoSAT, a latent flow-matching framework for full-body motion generation jointly conditioned on natural-language intent and directional spatial-audio cues through hierarchical cross-attention before generating motion. Such a hierarchical design enhances temporally coherent and semantically aligned motion sequences. We also develop tri-modal evaluators for comprehensive evaluation on this novel task. Extensive experiments show that MoSAT achieves the SOTA performance by leveraging spatial audio's intrinsic motion-shaping properties alongside textual semantics, enabling precise and diverse motion in various scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Orientation-Aware Control and Trajectory Design for Aerial RIS-Assisted Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23157)
*Abdoul Karim A. H. Saliah,Hajar El Hammouti,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Giuseppe Silano*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种姿态感知的空中可重构智能表面（ARIS）框架，显式考虑无人机动力学、执行器限制与姿态相关信道，并通过通信引导参考轨迹规划与非线模型预测控制实现动态可行轨迹跟踪和有利的RIS姿态，同时进行轻量级波束成形和RIS相移更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARIS可结合可重构超表面与空中机动性，在遮挡环境中增强无线连接；但实际部署中RIS刚性安装在多旋翼无人机上，产生位置-姿态耦合动力学，直接影响通信质量。由于RIS性能对姿态高度敏感，点质量模型或姿态不变模型可能导致过于乐观的性能估计。

Method: 提出姿态感知ARIS框架，显式建模无人机动力学、执行器限制和姿态相关信道；将通信引导的参考轨迹规划器与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）结合，用于动态可行轨迹跟踪并获得有利RIS姿态；同时采用轻量级波束成形和RIS相移更新。

Result: 仿真表明，考虑姿态可使用户吞吐量提升13.77%；所提出的联合通信更新相比仅相移优化使网络吞吐量提升28%，相比仅波束成形优化至少提升2.5倍。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了在ARIS辅助无线系统中采用姿态感知建模以及通信-控制联合设计的重要性。

Abstract: Aerial Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (ARISs) can enhance wireless connectivity in obstructed environments by combining reconfigurable metasurfaces with aerial mobility. However, in practical deployments, the Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (RIS) is rigidly mounted on a multi-rotor Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV), which creates coupled position-orientation dynamics that directly affect communication quality. Since RIS performance is highly sensitive to orientation, point-mass or orientation-invariant models may lead to optimistic performance estimates. This paper proposes an orientation-aware ARIS framework that explicitly accounts for UAV dynamics, actuation limits, and orientation-dependent channels. The proposed approach combines a communications-guided reference trajectory planner with a Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for dynamically feasible trajectory tracking and favorable RIS orientation, while employing lightweight beamforming and RIS phase-shift updates. Simulations show that accounting for orientation improves user throughput by 13.77%, while the proposed joint communication updates improve network throughput by 28% over phase-shift-only optimization and by at least a factor of 2.5 over beamforming-only optimization. These results highlight the importance of orientation-aware modeling and joint communication-control design in ARIS-assisted wireless systems.

</details>


### [209] [Toward Mission-Critical ISAC: Reliable Energy-Aware Coordination in UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22292)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb,Amir Javadpour*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出两层无人机蜂群架构：任务无人机执行协同ISAC以跟踪空中移动目标，充电无人机利用太阳能与空中UAV-to-UAV WPT为低电量任务无人机补能；并联合优化轨迹、感知-通信时隙划分、WPT调度/准入及会合轨迹以最小化协同PCRB，采用RHAO四块算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键任务中的无人机蜂群需同时跟踪移动空中目标并维持可靠数据链，但主动ISAC带来推进与传输的双重能耗负担，可能因电池过早耗尽而威胁任务连续性；现有工作未联合考虑协同ISAC感知质量、空中WPT与双层能量管理。

Method: 建立两层无人机蜂群架构，任务无人机执行协同ISAC，充电无人机装备太阳能采集板并通过空中WPT为低电量任务无人机补能。问题建模为最小化协同后验克拉美-罗下界（PCRB），联合优化任务无人机轨迹、每时隙感知-通信时间分割、WPT调度与准入以及充电无人机会合轨迹，受最小上行速率、双层能量因果、WPT邻近、避碰和速度约束，形成非凸混合整数规划（MIP）。提出Receding-Horizon Alternating Optimization（RHAO），按四块逐时隙分解：匈牙利算法进行充电准入，SCA优化任务无人机轨迹与时间分割，充电无人机会合，WPT功率分配，并具单调收敛保证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，RHAO将平均PCRB较固定时间分割基线降低16.8倍，较静态轨迹方案降低5.4倍；空中WPT子系统在整个任务周期内使所有任务无人机保持在能量临界阈值以上。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将协同ISAC感知质量与空中WPT及双层能量管理联合耦合，表明RHAO可有效求解该非凸MIP，并同时提升无人机蜂群的感知跟踪性能与能量续航可靠性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms deployed in mission-critical applications must simultaneously track a mobile aerial target and maintain reliable data links. However, active integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) operation imposes a dual energy burden on propulsion and transmission, threatening mission continuity through premature battery depletion. In this paper, we propose a two-tier UAV swarm architecture in which mission UAVs (MUAVs) execute cooperative ISAC for mobile aerial target tracking while dedicated charging UAVs (CUAVs), equipped with solar harvesting panels, replenish low-battery MUAVs via aerial UAV-to-UAV wireless power transfer (WPT). We formulate the joint minimization of the cooperative posterior Cramér-Rao bound (PCRB) over MUAV trajectories, per-slot sensing-communication time splits, WPT scheduling and admission, and CUAV rendezvous trajectories, subject to minimum uplink rate, dual-tier energy causality, WPT proximity, collision-avoidance, and speed constraints, yielding a non-convex mixed-integer program (MIP) that, to the best of our knowledge, is the first to jointly couple cooperative ISAC sensing quality with aerial WPT and dual-tier energy management. To solve it efficiently, we propose Receding-Horizon Alternating Optimization (RHAO), a four-block per-slot algorithm that decomposes the problem into: charging admission via the Hungarian algorithm, MUAV trajectory and time-split via successive convex approximation (SCA), CUAV rendezvous, and WPT power allocation, with monotone convergence guarantees. Simulation results demonstrate that RHAO reduces the mean PCRB by 16.8 times over a fixed-time-split baseline and 5.4 times over a static-trajectory scheme, while the aerial WPT subsystem sustains all MUAVs above the energy-critical threshold throughout the full mission horizon.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Identity Continuity in Long-Term Embodied AI Relationships: From Agent-Specific Identity Representation to Identity-Continuity Appraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23356)
*Zijian Ru*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 文章提出一个用户侧框架，将长期具身AI表示为代理特定的身份表征，由具身感知、心理行为、关系自传三个开放身份内容域构成；系统变化后用户加权整合连续/断裂证据，形成从强连续到明确断裂的判断，并将身份连续性作为生命周期设计的心理目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期具身AI会经历学习、模型更新、记忆压缩、硬件维修和跨具身迁移，对已形成持续关系的用户而言，这不仅涉及产品一致性，更涉及身份连续性：变化后的系统是否仍被体验为同一特定代理。现有研究提示人-AI关系可能形成关系特殊性，且重大更新或平台中断伴随关系丧失与恢复渴望，因此需要用户侧框架。

Method: 提出用户侧框架：以代理特定身份表征表示长期具身AI，组织为至少三个开放身份内容域——具身感知域、心理行为域、关系自传域；各域信息权重不等，共享历史、关系角色与情境响应性可能更具身份诊断性。系统变化后，用户加权整合连续性与不连续性证据，形成连续谱判断；因果历史来源与用户参与作为语境证据，而非第四身份内容域。

Result: 框架给出身份连续性判断机制：从相对强连续、模糊或部分连续，到明确不连续。并进一步提出将身份连续性作为生命周期设计的心理目标，涵盖记忆选择、模型更新与跨具身迁移，且受隐私与用户控制约束。

Conclusion: 该框架将身份连续性从产品一致性中区分出来，为用户侧体验与具身AI生命周期设计提供概念工具，强调在隐私和用户控制下通过记忆、更新与迁移维护关系连续性。

Abstract: Long-term embodied AI will undergo learning, model updates, memory compression, hardware repair, and migration across embodiments. For users who have formed sustained relationships with such systems, these changes raise not only a problem of product consistency but also one of identity continuity: whether the changed system is still experienced as the same particular agent. Existing research suggests that human-AI relationships may develop relational particularity, that robotics and artificial-identity research has identified identity and migration signals across embodiments, and that major updates or platform disruptions can be accompanied by relational loss and restoration desire. This article proposes a user-side framework in which long-term embodied AI is represented through an agent-specific identity representation organized by at least three open identity-content domains: embodied-perceptual, psychological-behavioral, and relational-autobiographical. Information from these domains is not equally weighted; shared history, relational roles, and contingent responsiveness may make some information more identity-diagnostic than others. After system change, users may integrate continuity and discontinuity evidence in a weighted manner, yielding judgments along a continuum from relatively strong identity continuity through ambiguity or partial continuity to clear identity discontinuity. Causal-historical provenance and user participation are treated as contextual evidence rather than a fourth identity-content domain. The framework also proposes identity continuity as a psychological objective for lifecycle design, including memory selection, model updating, and migration across embodiments, under constraints of privacy and user control.

</details>
